--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /.github/workflows/sync-branches.yml
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
name: Sync All Branches

on:
  push:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write

concurrency:
  group: sync-all-branches
  cancel-in-progress: false

jobs:
  sync:
    name: Sync Branches with Default Branch
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    if: github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' || github.ref == format('refs/heads/{0}', github.event.repository.default_branch)

    steps:
      - name: Checkout Repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0
          token: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - name: Configure Git
        run: |
          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"

      - name: Detect and Sync All Branches
        shell: bash
        env:
          DEFAULT_BRANCH: ${{ github.event.repository.default_branch }}
        run: |
          set -u

          git fetch origin --prune

          echo "Default branch: $DEFAULT_BRANCH"

          branches=()
          failed=()

          while IFS= read -r branch; do
            [[ -z "$branch" ]] && continue
            [[ "$branch" == "HEAD" ]] && continue
            [[ "$branch" == "$DEFAULT_BRANCH" ]] && continue

            branches+=("$branch")
          done < <(git for-each-ref \
            --format='%(refname:strip=3)' \
            refs/remotes/origin)

          if (( ${#branches[@]} == 0 )); then
            echo "No branches found to synchronize."
            exit 0
          fi

          echo "Found ${#branches[@]} branches."

          for branch in "${branches[@]}"; do
            echo "::group::Syncing $branch"

            if ! git checkout -B "$branch" "origin/$branch"; then
              echo "::warning::Checkout failed for $branch."
              failed+=("$branch (checkout failed)")
              echo "::endgroup::"
              continue
            fi

            if git merge --no-edit "origin/$DEFAULT_BRANCH"; then
              if git push origin "HEAD:$branch"; then
                echo "Successfully synced $branch."
              else
                echo "::warning::Push failed for $branch."
                failed+=("$branch (push failed)")
              fi
            else
              echo "::warning::Merge conflict in $branch."
              git merge --abort || true
              failed+=("$branch (merge conflict)")
            fi

            echo "::endgroup::"
          done

          if (( ${#failed[@]} > 0 )); then
            echo "::error::Some branches could not be synchronized:"
            printf ' - %s\n' "${failed[@]}"
            exit 1
          fi

          echo "All branches successfully synchronized."

# Contributing Guidelines

Welcome to **BarcodeHub**! This document outlines the contribution workflow and development guidelines for all team members working on **Bar-Code-System-CPP**.

Please follow these guidelines to maintain a consistent and organized development workflow.

## 1. Clone the Repository

Clone the repository and navigate to the project directory:

```bash
git clone https://github.com/Syntrojex/Bar-Code-System-CPP.git
cd Bar-Code-System-CPP
```

## 2. Switch to Your Assigned Branch

Each team member has an assigned branch. Switch to your respective branch before making any changes.

```bash
git checkout your-branch
```

### Available Team Branches

| Team Member | Branch |
|---|---|
| Muhammad Mustafa Amir | `mustafa` |
| Ali Abdullah | `ali` |
| Farzam Zeeshan | `farzam` |
| Shauzab Mukhtar | `shauzab` |

**Important:** Always work on your assigned branch. Do not make changes directly on `main`.

## 3. Synchronize Your Branch

Before starting new work, make sure your local branch has the latest changes:

```bash
git pull origin your-branch
```

This helps prevent conflicts and keeps your local development environment up to date.

## 4. Make Your Changes

Implement the changes related to your assigned task.

Follow these practices:

- Maintain the existing project structure.
- Follow the established C++ coding conventions.
- Write clean, readable, and maintainable code.
- Avoid modifying unrelated files.
- Test your changes before committing.

## 5. Stage and Commit Changes

Review your modifications:

```bash
git status
```

Stage the relevant files:

```bash
git add .
```

Create a meaningful commit:

```bash
git commit -m "Add barcode generation functionality"
```

Keep commit messages concise and descriptive. Each commit should represent a meaningful change.

## 6. Push Your Changes

Push your commits to your assigned remote branch:

```bash
git push origin your-branch
```

## 7. Create a Pull Request

Once your changes have been pushed, create a Pull Request to propose merging them into `main`.

1. Open the project repository on GitHub.
2. Select **Compare & pull request**.
3. Set the base branch to `main`.
4. Select your assigned branch as the compare branch.
5. Provide a clear and descriptive title.
6. Explain the changes and their purpose in the description.
7. Create the Pull Request.

## 8. Review and Merge

All Pull Requests must go through the project's review process before being merged.

- The project lead reviews submitted Pull Requests.
- Address any requested changes before merging.
- Ensure the proposed changes do not introduce unnecessary modifications or conflicts.
- Only reviewed and approved contributions should be integrated into `main`.
- Team members must not push directly to `main`.

## 9. Automatic Branch Synchronization

The project uses a GitHub Actions workflow to automatically synchronize team branches with the latest `main` branch.

Whenever changes are pushed to `main`, the workflow:

- Detects existing team branches automatically.
- Attempts to merge the latest `main` changes into each branch.
- Pushes successfully synchronized branches back to GitHub.
- Reports branches that encounter merge conflicts or synchronization failures.

## General Guidelines

- Always work on your assigned branch.
- Keep commits meaningful and focused.
- Follow the established project structure and coding conventions.
- Test your changes before submitting a Pull Request.
- Keep Pull Requests limited to their intended tasks.
- Avoid committing generated build files and unnecessary dependencies.
- Communicate with the project lead when clarification is needed.
- Never commit directly to `main`.
---

Thank you for contributing to **BarcodeHub**!

FILE: /Backend/include/billing/Bill.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

/**
 * @file Bill.h
 * @brief Core data structures representing customer bills, line items, and monetary totals.
 * @author Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)
 *
 * This header defines the foundational domain models for Increment 2's Billing subsystem
 * in BarcodeHub. It encapsulates individual line items, aggregated bill totals, and
 * the full bill entity matching the Bill.txt specification.
 */

#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace barcodehub::billing {

/**
 * @struct BillLine
 * @brief Represents a single product entry within a customer bill.
 *
 * Each line item retains the product acronym, pricing breakdown (in PKR),
 * applicable tax and discount amounts, and purchased quantity.
 */
struct BillLine {
    std::string productAcronym;   ///< Up to 8-character product acronym (e.g. "MILK", "BREAD").
    double unitPrice{0.0};        ///< Base unit price in PKR (exclusive of tax).
    double taxPerUnit{0.0};       ///< Tax amount in PKR per unit (used for calculation, transient).
    double discountPerUnit{0.0};  ///< Applicable discount amount in PKR per unit.
    double quantity{0.0};         ///< Purchased quantity (supports integer counts or fractional weights).
};

/**
 * @struct BillTotals
 * @brief Aggregated financial summary of an entire bill.
 *
 * Maintains the subtotal before tax, cumulative tax, total discount,
 * and the final net payable amount calculated as:
 *   payable = withoutTax + tax - discount
 */
struct BillTotals {
    double withoutTax{0.0};  ///< Net subtotal of all items before taxes in PKR.
    double tax{0.0};         ///< Cumulative tax amount across all line items in PKR.
    double discount{0.0};    ///< Cumulative discount applied across all items in PKR.
    double payable{0.0};     ///< Final total payable by the customer in PKR.
};

/**
 * @struct Bill
 * @brief Complete representation of a finalized or previewed customer bill.
 *
 * Corresponds to records stored in data/Bill.txt. A bill contains a unique 9-digit
 * identifier (YYMMDD + 3-digit daily sequence), customer details, reference to the
 * originating order ID, payment status, associated line items, and total figures.
 */
struct Bill {
    std::string id;              ///< Unique 9-digit Bill ID formatted as YYMMDD### (e.g., "261118001").
    std::string customerName;    ///< Name of the purchasing customer.
    std::string orderId;         ///< 8-digit unique identifier of the source order from Order.txt.
    std::string paymentStatus;   ///< Current payment status (e.g., "paid", "in-progress", "cancelled").
    std::vector<BillLine> lines; ///< Collection of purchased products and quantities in this bill.
    BillTotals totals;           ///< Calculated monetary summary for the entire bill.
};

} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/include/billing/BillCalculator.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

/**
 * @file BillCalculator.h
 * @brief Pure monetary calculation and validation engine for customer bills.
 * @author Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)
 *
 * Implements the financial accounting logic for the BarcodeHub POS system.
 * The BillCalculator is decoupled from file storage and user interfaces,
 * ensuring high modularity, high testability, and strict adherence to
 * business rules regarding subtotal, taxes, discounts, and rounding.
 */

#include "billing/Bill.h"

#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace barcodehub::billing {

/**
 * @class BillCalculator
 * @brief Stateless arithmetic engine responsible for computing and validating bill totals.
 *
 * Enforces the standardized business calculation rule:
 * \f[
 *   \text{payable} = \text{totalWithoutTax} + \text{totalTax} - \text{totalDiscount}
 * \f]
 *
 * Validation checks executed during calculation:
 * - Empty bill check: At least one product line item must be present.
 * - Value sanity: Unit prices, taxes, and discounts must be finite and non-negative.
 * - Quantity validity: Purchased quantity must be finite and strictly greater than zero.
 * - Net total sanity: The net payable amount after discount cannot be negative.
 * - Precision: All computed totals are normalized to 2 decimal places using standard rounding.
 */
class BillCalculator {
public:
    /**
     * @brief Computes totals for a list of bill line items and validates financial boundaries.
     *
     * Iterates through all line items, accumulates subtotal, sales tax, and discounts,
     * rounds each component according to monetary precision rules, and derives final payable.
     *
     * @param[in] lines Collection of product line items to be totaled.
     * @param[out] totals Aggregated BillTotals structure populated upon success.
     * @param[out] error Optional pointer to receive a human-readable failure description on validation error.
     * @return True if all items are valid and totals were calculated successfully; false otherwise.
     */
    static bool calculate(const std::vector<BillLine>& lines, BillTotals& totals,
                          std::string* error = nullptr);
};

} // namespace barcodehub::billing

#pragma once

// One include for the whole Billing module.
#include "billing/Bill.h"
#include "billing/BillCalculator.h"
#include "billing/BillRecordCodec.h"
#include "billing/BillRepository.h"
#include "billing/BillingService.h"
#include "billing/PaymentMethod.h"


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/include/billing/BillingService.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

/**
 * @file BillingService.h
 * @brief Application service orchestrating order billing, payment execution, and bill persistence.
 * @author Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)
 *
 * Coordinates the checkout lifecycle in the BarcodeHub POS system. Acts as the primary
 * bridge between the graphical interface and backend domain modules (OrderManager,
 * BillRepository, and PaymentMethodRegistry).
 */

#include "billing/Bill.h"
#include "billing/BillRepository.h"
#include "billing/PaymentMethod.h"
#include "order/OrderManager.h"

#include <functional>
#include <string>

namespace barcodehub::billing {

/**
 * @brief Callback function type used by BillingService to fetch product metadata by ID.
 *
 * Inverts the dependency on the Product subsystem: Billing never directly includes
 * ProductModel headers, fulfilling clean architectural layering and decoupling.
 */
using ProductLookup = std::function<bool(const std::string& productId, order::ProductSnapshot& out)>;

/**
 * @class BillingService
 * @brief High-level workflow coordinator managing checkout, payment settlement, and transaction atomicity.
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 * - Order verification: Ensures the target order exists, has line items, and is not already paid or archived.
 * - Product resolution: Obtains live product snapshots (acronym, pricing, tax, discount) via injected callback.
 * - Bill calculation: Invokes BillCalculator to determine monetary figures.
 * - Payment execution: Delegates settlement to the appropriate strategy from PaymentMethodRegistry.
 * - ID generation: Assigns standardized 9-digit Bill IDs based on current date and repository history.
 * - Status transition: Updates the order status to Paid in Order.txt.
 * - Persistence & Rollback: Appends the bill into Bill.txt; rolls back order status if file writing fails.
 */
class BillingService {
public:
    /**
     * @brief Constructs a BillingService with required domain collaborators.
     * @param orders Reference to the active OrderManager instance.
     * @param bills Reference to the BillRepository for disk persistence.
     * @param lookup Injected function for resolving product details by product ID.
     * @param methods Strategy registry of supported payment methods (defaults to Cash, Card, Wallet).
     */
    BillingService(order::OrderManager& orders, BillRepository& bills, ProductLookup lookup,
                   PaymentMethodRegistry methods = PaymentMethodRegistry::withDefaults());

    /**
     * @brief Generates an in-memory bill preview for an active order without committing payment.
     *
     * Used by the POS interface to display itemized line items, tax, discount, and total
     * payable on the screen before the customer renders payment.
     *
     * @param[in] orderId Unique identifier of the order to preview.
     * @param[out] bill Populated Bill object containing preview lines and computed totals (id remains unset).
     * @param[out] error Optional pointer to store error message upon failure.
     * @return True if preview generation succeeded; false if order was missing or invalid.
     */
    bool preview(const std::string& orderId, Bill& bill, std::string* error = nullptr) const;

    /**
     * @brief Executes payment processing, generates a permanent Bill ID, and persists bill records.
     *
     * If cash payment is selected, calculates change due. Performs atomic order status update
     * and file persistence into data/Bill.txt. If bill writing encounters an error, the order
     * status update is rolled back.
     *
     * @param[in] orderId Unique identifier of the order being settled.
     * @param[in] payment Payment request detailing chosen mode, tendered amount, and merchant details.
     * @param[out] bill Finalized Bill object populated with generated Bill ID and saved data.
     * @param[out] change Optional pointer to receive change amount in PKR for cash transactions.
     * @param[out] error Optional pointer to receive failure details.
     * @return True if checkout and persistence succeeded; false otherwise.
     */
    bool checkout(const std::string& orderId, const PaymentRequest& payment, Bill& bill,
                  double* change = nullptr, std::string* error = nullptr);

private:
    order::OrderManager& orders_;           ///< Managed order subsystem.
    BillRepository& bills_;                 ///< Repository managing Bill.txt file I/O.
    ProductLookup lookup_;                  ///< Injected product metadata lookup callback.
    PaymentMethodRegistry methods_;         ///< Registered payment processors.
};

} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/include/billing/BillRecordCodec.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

/**
 * @file BillRecordCodec.h
 * @brief Serialization and deserialization engine for Bill.txt flat-file records.
 * @author Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)
 *
 * Implements bidirectional translation between structured Bill / BillLine objects
 * and raw delimited text lines stored in data/Bill.txt.
 *
 * Line layout specification (12 whitespace-delimited fields ending with '#'):
 *   [0]  billId           (9 digits: YYMMDD###)
 *   [1]  customerName     (encoded string via RecordText)
 *   [2]  orderId          (8-digit order identifier)
 *   [3]  productAcronym   (encoded string, max 8 chars)
 *   [4]  unitPrice        (formatted floating point, PKR)
 *   [5]  discountPerUnit  (formatted floating point, PKR)
 *   [6]  quantity         (formatted floating point)
 *   [7]  totalWithoutTax  (bill subtotal before tax, PKR)
 *   [8]  totalTax         (cumulative bill tax, PKR)
 *   [9]  totalDiscount    (cumulative bill discount, PKR)
 *   [10] totalPayable     (net bill payable, PKR)
 *   [11] paymentStatus    (encoded order status string, e.g. "paid")
 *   [12] "#"              (record terminator delimiter)
 */

#include "billing/Bill.h"

#include <string>

namespace barcodehub::billing {

/**
 * @class BillRecordCodec
 * @brief Stateless encoder and decoder for flat-file bill records.
 *
 * Operates purely on strings and data models without performing disk I/O,
 * facilitating isolated unit testing of file formatting and parsing logic.
 */
class BillRecordCodec {
public:
    /**
     * @brief Serializes a Bill header and a specific BillLine into a compliant Bill.txt line.
     *
     * Encodes text fields safely using common::encodeField, formats currency figures,
     * and appends the mandatory '#' terminal symbol.
     *
     * @param bill The overarching bill containing ID, customer name, totals, and payment status.
     * @param line The specific product line item to serialize.
     * @return Formatted record string ready for writing to disk.
     */
    static std::string format(const Bill& bill, const BillLine& line);

    /**
     * @brief Deserializes a raw record line into a Bill header and BillLine instance.
     *
     * Validates that the line ends with '#', splits tokens, checks for the 12-field contract,
     * verifies that the Bill ID comprises exactly 9 numeric digits, and decodes numeric and text fields.
     *
     * @param[in] record Raw line read from Bill.txt.
     * @param[out] header Output Bill instance populated with global bill metadata and totals.
     * @param[out] line Output BillLine instance populated with the item-specific values.
     * @param[out] error Optional pointer to store error message upon parse failure.
     * @return True if parsing succeeded and fields conform to specifications; false otherwise.
     */
    static bool parse(const std::string& record, Bill& header, BillLine& line,
                      std::string* error = nullptr);
};

} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/include/billing/BillRepository.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

/**
 * @file BillRepository.h
 * @brief Data access object (DAO) managing atomic read and write operations for Bill.txt.
 * @author Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)
 *
 * Provides safe flat-file persistence for finalized customer bills. Uses an atomic
 * write-through-temporary-file pattern to guard against partial writes or file corruption
 * during unexpected shutdowns or power loss.
 */

#include "billing/Bill.h"

#include <filesystem>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace barcodehub::billing {

/**
 * @class BillRepository
 * @brief Manages storage, retrieval, and identity generation for Bill domain models.
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 * - Atomic persistence: Rewrites the full bill file through a temporary `.tmp` file and
 *   atomically renames/replaces it, preventing data loss.
 * - Multi-line reconstruction: Parses flat lines sharing the same 9-digit Bill ID and
 *   recombines them into unified Bill aggregates containing all line items.
 * - Duplicate prevention: Rejects append operations if a bill with the same ID is already stored.
 * - Sequential ID generation: Generates structured 9-digit identifiers composed of the date
 *   in YYMMDD format followed by a 3-digit zero-padded sequence counter (e.g. 261118001).
 */
class BillRepository {
public:
    /**
     * @brief Constructs a repository bound to the specified file path.
     * @param billFile Absolute or relative path to the Bill.txt storage file.
     */
    explicit BillRepository(std::filesystem::path billFile);

    /**
     * @brief Reads all stored bills from disk, grouping multi-line records by Bill ID.
     *
     * If the target file does not yet exist, returns true with an empty result vector
     * (representing a clean database initialized without previous transactions).
     *
     * @param[out] bills Output vector populated with reconstructed Bill objects.
     * @param[out] error Optional pointer to store error description if file I/O or parsing fails.
     * @return True if read and parsed cleanly; false on I/O or syntax error.
     */
    bool loadAll(std::vector<Bill>& bills, std::string* error = nullptr) const;

    /**
     * @brief Atomically appends a new bill to disk.
     *
     * Validates that the bill has line items, loads existing bills to ensure ID uniqueness,
     * serializes all records to a temporary file, and performs an atomic replace.
     *
     * @param[in] bill The Bill aggregate to append.
     * @param[out] error Optional pointer to store failure description.
     * @return True if written successfully; false on duplication or I/O failure.
     */
    bool append(const Bill& bill, std::string* error = nullptr);

    /**
     * @brief Computes the next available 9-digit Bill ID for a given date.
     *
     * Scans existing bills matching the current YYMMDD prefix, identifies the highest
     * sequence number (001-999), and increments it by one.
     *
     * @param[in] date Date string formatted as YYYY-MM-DD.
     * @param[in] existing Current collection of recorded bills.
     * @param[out] error Optional pointer to store error message if date format is invalid or limit reached.
     * @return 9-digit Bill ID string (e.g., "261118001"), or empty string on error.
     */
    static std::string nextBillId(const std::string& date, const std::vector<Bill>& existing,
                                  std::string* error = nullptr);

private:
    std::filesystem::path file_; ///< Path to the underlying Bill.txt file.
};

} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/include/billing/PaymentMethod.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

/**
 * @file PaymentMethod.h
 * @brief Strategy pattern interfaces and implementations for processing customer payments.
 * @author Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)
 *
 * Implements extensible payment processing for BarcodeHub. Supports multiple payment
 * modes (Cash, Credit/Debit Card, Mobile Wallet) following the Open-Closed Principle (OCP):
 * new payment modes can be added without modifying existing processing workflows.
 */

#include "order/Order.h"   // PaymentMode (cash / card / wallet)

#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace barcodehub::billing {

/**
 * @struct PaymentRequest
 * @brief Input payload submitted by the POS cashier during the checkout workflow.
 */
struct PaymentRequest {
    order::PaymentMode mode{order::PaymentMode::Cash}; ///< Selected payment method (Cash, Card, Wallet).
    std::string merchant;        ///< For Card/Wallet: Bank or service provider name (e.g., "HBL", "JazzCash").
    double amountTendered{0.0};  ///< For Cash: Physical currency amount handed over by customer in PKR.
};

/**
 * @struct PaymentResult
 * @brief Outcome and financial reconciliation of an attempted payment transaction.
 */
struct PaymentResult {
    bool ok{false};              ///< True if the transaction succeeded; false if rejected.
    double change{0.0};          ///< Balance change to return to the customer in PKR (Cash payments).
    std::string error;           ///< Descriptive failure reason if the transaction was rejected.
};

/**
 * @class IPaymentMethod
 * @brief Abstract strategy interface defining standard behavior for all payment processors.
 */
class IPaymentMethod {
public:
    virtual ~IPaymentMethod() = default;

    /**
     * @brief Identifies the payment mode handled by this strategy implementation.
     * @return Corresponding order::PaymentMode enumeration.
     */
    virtual order::PaymentMode mode() const = 0;

    /**
     * @brief Validates and executes payment settlement against a payable amount.
     * @param request Payment parameters tendered by the cashier/customer.
     * @param payable Net bill total due in PKR.
     * @return PaymentResult indicating success status, change owed, or error detail.
     */
    virtual PaymentResult process(const PaymentRequest& request, double payable) const = 0;
};

/**
 * @class CashPayment
 * @brief Handles physical cash settlements, verifying sufficient funds and calculating change.
 */
class CashPayment : public IPaymentMethod {
public:
    order::PaymentMode mode() const override { return order::PaymentMode::Cash; }
    PaymentResult process(const PaymentRequest& request, double payable) const override;
};

/**
 * @class CardPayment
 * @brief Handles credit/debit card transactions, requiring valid acquiring bank identification.
 */
class CardPayment : public IPaymentMethod {
public:
    order::PaymentMode mode() const override { return order::PaymentMode::Card; }
    PaymentResult process(const PaymentRequest& request, double payable) const override;
};

/**
 * @class WalletPayment
 * @brief Handles digital mobile wallet transactions (e.g. JazzCash, Nayapay, SadaPay).
 */
class WalletPayment : public IPaymentMethod {
public:
    order::PaymentMode mode() const override { return order::PaymentMode::Wallet; }
    PaymentResult process(const PaymentRequest& request, double payable) const override;
};

/**
 * @class PaymentMethodRegistry
 * @brief Container and dispatcher managing registered payment processing strategies.
 *
 * Facilitates lookup and execution of the appropriate IPaymentMethod handler
 * based on the cashier's chosen payment mode.
 */
class PaymentMethodRegistry {
public:
    /**
     * @brief Factory creating a registry pre-populated with standard payment strategies (Cash, Card, Wallet).
     * @return Initialized PaymentMethodRegistry instance.
     */
    static PaymentMethodRegistry withDefaults();

    /**
     * @brief Registers an additional payment processor strategy into the registry.
     * @param method Owning pointer to an IPaymentMethod implementation.
     */
    void add(std::unique_ptr<IPaymentMethod> method);

    /**
     * @brief Retrieves the payment processor corresponding to the requested payment mode.
     * @param mode Target payment mode.
     * @return Non-owning pointer to the matching processor, or nullptr if unsupported.
     */
    const IPaymentMethod* find(order::PaymentMode mode) const;

private:
    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<const IPaymentMethod>> methods_; ///< Strategy storage.
};

} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/include/billing/README.md
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Billing Module (Standard C++17)

**Author:** Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)  
**Deliverable:** Increment 2 — Billing & POS Subsystem

This module implements the business logic, financial calculations, payment processing strategies, and flat-file persistence for customer bills in **BarcodeHub**. It is written in pure standard C++17, decoupled from Qt GUI headers, enabling high testability, modularity, and rapid unit testing.

---

## 1. Architecture & Component Responsibilities

The subsystem follows the **Single Responsibility Principle (SRP)** and **Open-Closed Principle (OCP)** across six core components:

| Component | Header | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|
| **Domain Models** | `Bill.h` | Defines `BillLine`, `BillTotals`, and `Bill` structures matching project specifications. |
| **Calculation Engine** | `BillCalculator.h` | Pure arithmetic engine computing subtotals, taxes, discounts, and net payable with 2-decimal rounding. |
| **Payment Strategies** | `PaymentMethod.h` | Strategy pattern interface (`IPaymentMethod`) and implementations (`CashPayment`, `CardPayment`, `WalletPayment`, `PaymentMethodRegistry`). |
| **Record Codec** | `BillRecordCodec.h` | Pure string-level encoder/decoder translating between `Bill` domain models and `Bill.txt` flat-file format. |
| **Data Repository** | `BillRepository.h` | Data Access Object (DAO) providing atomic write-through-temp-file persistence and sequential 9-digit Bill ID generation. |
| **Application Service** | `BillingService.h` | Orchestrates order settlement, product metadata lookup, payment execution, and atomic order status synchronization. |

---

## 2. Bill.txt Format Specification

Each line in `data/Bill.txt` represents one purchased line item of a customer bill, ending with `#`. All monetary totals and status fields are repeated across lines sharing the same Bill ID:

```text
billId customer orderId acronym unitPrice discountPerUnit quantity totalWithoutTax totalTax totalDiscount totalPayable status #
```

### Field Breakdown:
1. **billId**: Exactly 9 numeric digits formatted as `YYMMDD###` (e.g., `261118001`).
2. **customer**: Customer name (encoded via `RecordText` to escape spaces).
3. **orderId**: 8-digit originating order identifier from `Order.txt`.
4. **acronym**: Product acronym (up to 8 characters, e.g., `MILK`, `BREAD`).
5. **unitPrice**: Base unit price in PKR.
6. **discountPerUnit**: Discount amount per unit in PKR.
7. **quantity**: Purchased quantity (count or weight).
8. **totalWithoutTax**: Bill subtotal before tax in PKR.
9. **totalTax**: Cumulative sales tax in PKR.
10. **totalDiscount**: Cumulative discounts in PKR.
11. **totalPayable**: Net amount payable (`withoutTax + tax - discount`) in PKR.
12. **status**: Payment status (e.g., `paid`).
13. **#**: Mandatory terminal character.

---

## 3. Financial Calculation Rules

`BillCalculator` enforces standard business accounting rules:
- $\text{subtotal} = \sum (\text{unitPrice} \times \text{quantity})$
- $\text{totalTax} = \sum (\text{taxPerUnit} \times \text{quantity})$
- $\text{totalDiscount} = \sum (\text{discountPerUnit} \times \text{quantity})$
- $\text{payable} = \text{roundMoney}(\text{subtotal} + \text{totalTax} - \text{totalDiscount})$

All numbers are verified for finite validity ($\text{non-NaN}$, $\text{non-Inf}$), strictly non-negative prices/taxes/discounts, and positive quantities.

---

## 4. Payment Strategy Pattern

Payment processing is architected with the Strategy Pattern:
- **Cash (`CashPayment`)**: Verifies tendered cash is finite, non-negative, and covers the payable amount. Calculates change returned to the customer.
- **Card (`CardPayment`)**: Validates acquiring bank name (must be non-empty and free of `#` delimiters).
- **Wallet (`WalletPayment`)**: Validates digital wallet provider (e.g., JazzCash, EasyPaisa, SadaPay).
- **Extensibility**: To add a new payment method (e.g., Crypto, Voucher), implement `IPaymentMethod` and register it with `PaymentMethodRegistry::add()` without modifying any existing service logic.

---

## 5. Verification & Testing

Unit tests are implemented in `tests/billing_tests.cpp`. To execute the billing test suite:

```sh
# Using CMake / CTest
ctest --test-dir build/test-build -R billing --output-on-failure
```

#pragma once

#include <string>
#include <vector>

// Small helpers for the '#'-terminated text records used by Order.txt, OrderDetail.txt and Bill.txt.
// One place for the file rules, so every module reads and writes them the same way (reusability).
//   record  = field ' ' field ' ' ... ' ' '#'
//   a text field has ' ' written as '_', and '_' '%' '#' escaped; an empty field is written as '-'
// (the same encoding the Order module uses, so names look identical in every file).
namespace barcodehub::common {

std::string encodeField(const std::string& text);
bool decodeField(const std::string& token, std::string& out);

// Splits one record line into its fields. False when the line does not end with '#'.
bool splitRecord(const std::string& rawLine, std::vector<std::string>& tokens);

bool parseNumber(const std::string& token, double& value);   // whole token, finite numbers only
std::string formatMoney(double value);                        // 2 decimals, '.' always
std::string formatQuantity(double value);                     // 3 decimals, trailing zeros removed
double roundMoney(double value);                              // to 2 decimals

std::string currentDate();                                    // YYYY-MM-DD, local time

} // namespace barcodehub::common

#pragma once

// A small transfer object. Product Management supplies this data after barcode lookup.

struct ProductSnapshot {
    std::string productId;
    std::string barcode;
    std::string acronym;
    std::string name;
    std::string measurementUnit; // "count" or "weight"
    double unitPrice{0.0};
    double taxPerUnit{0.0};
    double discountPerUnit{0.0};
    double availableQuantity{0.0};
};

    ProductSnapshot product;
    double quantity{0.0};
};

class OrderManager {
public:
    OrderManager(std::filesystem::path orderFile,
                 std::filesystem::path orderDetailFile);

    // Adds a product returned by Product Management. Repeated products are combined.
    bool addToCart(const ProductSnapshot& product, double quantity,
                   std::string* error = nullptr);

    bool setCartQuantity(const std::string& productId, double newQuantity,
                         std::string* error = nullptr);
    bool removeFromCart(const std::string& productId);

    double subtotalBeforeTax() const noexcept;
    double totalTax() const noexcept;
    double totalDiscount() const noexcept;
    double totalPayable() const noexcept;

    // Returns the saved Order through createdOrder when successful.
    bool createOrder(const std::string& customerName,
                     const std::string& customerContact,
                     Order& createdOrder,
                     std::string* error = nullptr);

    // Update payment/status after the cashier completes payment or holds the order.
    bool updateOrder(const Order& order, std::string* error = nullptr);

    // The next order ID (preview only; createOrder allocates it at save time).
    std::string nextOrderId(std::string* error = nullptr) const;
    // Remove a newly created, unpaid order if checkout fails.
    bool discardOrder(const std::string& orderId, std::string* error = nullptr);

    // Read persisted data for UI/history screens.
    bool loadOrders(std::vector<Order>& orders, std::string* error = nullptr) const;
    bool loadOrderDetails(std::vector<OrderDetail>& details,
                          std::string* error = nullptr) const;
    // Only the detail rows that belong to one order (for Billing / order history).
    bool loadOrderDetails(const std::string& orderId,
                          std::vector<OrderDetail>& details,
                          std::string* error = nullptr) const;
    bool findOrder(const std::string& orderId, Order& order,
                   std::string* error = nullptr) const;

private:
    std::filesystem::path orderFile_;
    std::filesystem::path orderDetailFile_;
    std::vector<CartItem> cart_;

    bool validateProduct(const ProductSnapshot& product, double quantity,
                         std::string* error) const;
    std::string generateOrderId(const std::string& date,
                                const std::vector<Order>& existing,
                                std::string* error) const;
};

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/include/product/Ean8.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
// EAN-8 helpers (the project's barcode symbology). Header-only, no dependencies.
#include <string>

namespace ean8
{
    inline bool isEightDigits(const std::string &s)
    {
        if (s.size() != 8)
            return false;
        for (char c : s)
            if (c < '0' || c > '9')
                return false;
        return true;
    }

    // Check digit for the first 7 digits (weights 3,1,3,1,3,1,3).
    inline int checkDigit(const std::string &sevenDigits)
    {
        int sum = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 7; ++i)
            sum += (sevenDigits[i] - '0') * (i % 2 == 0 ? 3 : 1);
        return (10 - sum % 10) % 10;
    }

    inline bool hasValidCheckDigit(const std::string &code)
    {
        return isEightDigits(code) && (code[7] - '0') == checkDigit(code.substr(0, 7));
    }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/include/product/Money.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
// Currency formatting shared by the description and (later) bills: "PKR 1,234.50".
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <string>

inline std::string formatPKR(double amount)
{
    char buf[64];
    std::snprintf(buf, sizeof buf, "%.2f", std::fabs(amount));
    std::string s = buf;
    const std::size_t dot = s.find('.');
    for (int i = static_cast<int>(dot) - 3; i > 0; i -= 3)
        s.insert(static_cast<std::size_t>(i), ",");
    return std::string(amount < 0 ? "-" : "") + "PKR " + s;
}

#pragma once
// ProductModel: the core data type (Product) plus small helpers it needs
// (measurement unit, PKR money formatting, EAN-8 barcode checks) and the
// classes that turn a Product into human-readable text.
#include <string>

#include <vector>

// ---------- Measurement unit ----------
enum class MeasurementUnit
{
    Count,
    Weight
};
std::string toString(MeasurementUnit unit);
bool parseMeasurementUnit(const std::string &text, MeasurementUnit &out);

// ---------- Money ----------
std::string formatPKR(double amount);

// ---------- EAN-8 barcode helpers ----------
namespace ean8
{
    bool isEightDigits(const std::string &code);
    bool hasValidCheckDigit(const std::string &code);
}

// ---------- Product ----------
class Product
{
public:
    Product() = default;
    Product(int id, std::string barcode, std::string acronym, std::string name, MeasurementUnit unit,
            double unitPrice, double tax, double discount, int availableQuantity,
            std::string barcodeFilePath);

    int getProductID() const { return id_; }
    const std::string &getBarcode() const { return barcode_; }
    const std::string &getAcronym() const { return acronym_; }
    const std::string &getProductName() const { return name_; }
    MeasurementUnit getMeasurementUnit() const { return unit_; }
    std::string getMeasurementUnitName() const { return toString(unit_); }
    double getUnitPrice() const { return unitPrice_; }
    double getTax() const { return tax_; }
    double getDiscount() const { return discount_; }
    int getAvailableQuantity() const { return availableQuantity_; }
    const std::string &getBarcodeFilePath() const { return barcodeFilePath_; }

    void deductStock(int qty); // throws std::invalid_argument if qty is invalid

private:
    int id_ = 0;
    std::string barcode_;
    std::string acronym_;
    std::string name_;
    MeasurementUnit unit_ = MeasurementUnit::Count;
    double unitPrice_ = 0;
    double tax_ = 0;
    double discount_ = 0;
    int availableQuantity_ = 0;
    std::string barcodeFilePath_;
};

{

};

#pragma once
// ProductStorage: everything that moves products between the text file and memory —
// parsing lines, writing lines, loading a whole file with error reporting,
// and the ProductManager that holds the catalogue and searches it.
#include "ProductValidation.h"
#include <cstddef>
#include <iosfwd>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

// ---------- Parsing one line (multi-word names allowed) ----------
struct ParsedProduct
{
    std::optional<Product> product;
    std::string error;
    bool ok() const { return product.has_value() && error.empty(); }
};

class ProductParser
{
public:
    static constexpr char kEndMarker = '#';
    ParsedProduct parseLine(const std::string &rawLine) const;
};

// ---------- Older strict codec (exactly 10 fields, single-word name) ----------
class ProductRecordCodec
{
public:
    struct ParseResult
    {
        bool ok = false;
        std::string error;
        Product product;
    };
    static ParseResult parse(const std::string &rawLine);
    static std::string format(const Product &p);
};

// ---------- Writing the file ----------
class ProductFileWriter
{
public:
    std::string serialize(const Product &p) const;
    // Writes to "<path>.tmp" then swaps it in, so a crash never leaves a half-written file.
    bool save(const std::string &path, const std::vector<Product> &products, std::string *error = nullptr) const;
};

// ---------- Loading the file ----------
enum class IssueSeverity
{
    Error,
    Warning
};

struct LoadIssue
{
    int line;
    IssueSeverity severity;
    std::string message;
};

struct ProductLoadResult
{
    bool fileOpened = false;
    std::vector<Product> products;
    std::vector<LoadIssue> issues;
    int rejectedLines = 0;

    int errorCount() const;
    int warningCount() const;
    bool hasErrors() const { return errorCount() > 0; }
    std::string summary() const;
};

class ProductFileLoader
{
public:
    ProductLoadResult load(const std::string &path) const;
    ProductLoadResult loadFrom(std::istream &in) const;

private:
    ProductParser parser_;
    ProductValidator validator_ = ProductValidator::standard();
};

// ---------- In-memory catalogue ----------
class ProductManager
{
public:
    bool loadProducts(const std::string &filename);
    bool saveProducts(std::string *error = nullptr) const;
    void clearProducts();

    const Product *searchByBarcode(const std::string &barcode) const;
    const Product *searchByID(int id) const;
    const Product *searchByCode(const std::string &code) const; // barcode, then id, then acronym
    std::vector<const Product *> searchByText(const std::string &query) const;

    bool deductStock(int productId, int quantity);

    const std::vector<Product> &getProducts() const { return products_; }
    const ProductLoadResult &getLastLoad() const { return lastLoad_; }

private:
    void rebuildIndex();
    Product *mutableById(int id);

    ProductFileLoader loader_;
    ProductLoadResult lastLoad_;
    std::string path_;
    std::vector<Product> products_;
    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::size_t> byBarcode_;
    std::unordered_map<int, std::size_t> byId_;
};

#pragma once
// ProductValidation: rules that check a Product's data is sane (ProductValidator)
// and checks that an order quantity can be met from stock (StockValidator).
#include "ProductModel.h"
#include <cstddef>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

// ---------- Product data validation ----------
struct ValidationReport
{
    std::vector<std::string> errors;   // line is rejected
    std::vector<std::string> warnings; // line is kept, but flagged
    bool ok() const { return errors.empty(); }
};

class IProductRule
{
public:
    virtual ~IProductRule() = default;
    virtual void check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const = 0;
};

class ProductIdRule : public IProductRule
{
public:
    void check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const override;
};

class BarcodeRule : public IProductRule
{
public:
    void check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const override;
};

class AcronymRule : public IProductRule
{
public:
    explicit AcronymRule(std::size_t maxLength = 10) : maxLength_(maxLength) {}
    void check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const override;

private:
    std::size_t maxLength_;
};

class ProductNameRule : public IProductRule
{
public:
    void check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const override;
};

class PriceRule : public IProductRule
{
public:
    void check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const override;
};

class QuantityRule : public IProductRule
{
public:
    void check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const override;
};

class BarcodePathRule : public IProductRule
{
public:
    void check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const override;
};

class ProductValidator
{
public:
    static ProductValidator standard(); // all built-in rules
    ProductValidator &addRule(std::unique_ptr<IProductRule> rule);
    ValidationReport validate(const Product &product) const;

private:
    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<IProductRule>> rules_;
};

// ---------- Stock checks ----------
enum class StockStatus
{
    Ok,
    InvalidQuantity,
    OutOfStock,
    InsufficientStock
};

struct StockCheck
{
    StockStatus status = StockStatus::Ok;
    int requested = 0;
    int available = 0;
    std::string message;
    bool ok() const { return status == StockStatus::Ok; }
};

// Decides whether a requested quantity is allowed given what's in stock.
class IStockPolicy
{
public:
    virtual ~IStockPolicy() = default;
    virtual bool allows(int requested, int available) const = 0;
    virtual std::string describe() const = 0;
};

// Default policy: you can't order more than is in stock.
class StrictStockPolicy : public IStockPolicy
{
public:
    bool allows(int requested, int available) const override { return requested <= available; }
    std::string describe() const override { return "cannot exceed available stock"; }
};

class StockValidator
{
public:
    explicit StockValidator(std::shared_ptr<const IStockPolicy> policy = std::make_shared<StrictStockPolicy>())
        : policy_(std::move(policy)) {}

    StockCheck check(const Product &p, int requested) const;
    StockCheck checkAdd(const Product &p, int alreadyInOrder, int toAdd) const;

private:
    std::shared_ptr<const IStockPolicy> policy_;
};


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/include/product/README.md
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Product module (standard C++17)

This module uses only the C++ standard library. It does not include Qt headers and can be compiled/tested independently of the GUI.

- `Product`: product data and validation rules.
- `ProductManager`: catalogue loading, lookup, uniqueness checks, and stock reservation.
- `FileIO`: text-file persistence for product, order, order-detail, and bill records.

## Product.txt format

Each record is exactly ten space-separated fields followed by `#`:

`productId barcode acronym name measurementUnit unitPrice tax discount availableQuantity barcodeFilePath#`

`acronym` is limited to 8 characters. `measurementUnit` must be `count` or `weight`. Because the assignment specifies space-separated fields without quoting/escaping, `name` and `barcodeFilePath` must not contain spaces. Use a relative path where practical, for example `assets/barcodes/12345678.png`.

The project archive's `data/Product.txt` was empty, so no sample product rows have been fabricated. Add the actual product records supplied by your team/instructor.

## Standalone compile check

From the repository root (G++):

```sh
g++ -std=c++17 -Wall -Wextra -Wpedantic -I Backend/include \
  Backend/src/product/Product.cpp \
  Backend/src/product/ProductManager.cpp \
  Backend/src/product/FileIO.cpp \
  path/to/your_product_test.cpp -o product_test
```

FILE: /Backend/include/scanner/decoder.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
#include "GrayImage.h"
#include <string>
#include <vector>

// Reads an EAN-8 barcode from a grayscale picture (pure C++17).
// Scans several horizontal lines, then several vertical lines (barcode held sideways),
// and accepts a barcode printed upside down.
class Decoder
{
public:
    // On success `ean8` is the full 8-digit code with a valid check digit - the same key
    // that Product.txt stores - and true is returned.
    // `lineFraction` (0..1) is the height of the aiming line: the rows around it are tried first.
    bool decodeCropping(const GrayImage &gray, std::string &ean8, double lineFraction = 0.5) const;
    // True when the last decode saw a complete barcode whose check digit was wrong.
    bool isBadCheck() const { return badCheckDigit; }

    // Where the last successful decode found the barcode, in picture pixels (for drawing a box).
    struct Hit { double x0 = 0, y0 = 0, x1 = 0, y1 = 0; bool valid = false; };
    Hit lastHit() const { return hit_; }

    // Pieces below are public so they can be unit-tested one by one.
    static const char *const patterns[10]; // 7-module left-hand pattern of digits 0..9
    bool decodeModules(const std::string &bits, std::string &ean8) const;
    static bool hasValidCheckDigit(const std::string &eight);

private:
    // measure every dark/light stripe along one line of pixels (sub-pixel edge positions)
    std::vector<double> lineRuns(const std::vector<float> &line, double &firstEdge) const;
    // turn the 43 stripe widths into 67 modules ('1' dark, '0' light)
    std::string runsToModules(const std::vector<double> &runs) const;
    static int digitMatch(const std::string &seven, bool right);
    std::string modulesToDigits(const std::string &bits) const;
    bool scanLines(const GrayImage &g, bool vertical, std::string &ean8, int preferredPercent) const;

    static constexpr int stripes = 43;
    static constexpr int modules = 67;
    mutable bool badCheckDigit = false;
    mutable Hit hit_;
};


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/include/scanner/GrayImage.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
// A plain 8-bit grayscale picture (row-major). The scanner works on this type only, so the
// decoder has no dependency on Qt or OpenCV and is easy to unit-test.
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

struct GrayImage {
    int width = 0;
    int height = 0;
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> pixels; // width * height values, 0 = black, 255 = white

    bool empty() const { return width <= 0 || height <= 0 || pixels.size() != static_cast<std::size_t>(width) * height; }
    std::uint8_t at(int x, int y) const { return pixels[static_cast<std::size_t>(y) * width + x]; }
};

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/billing/BillCalculator.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "billing/BillCalculator.h"

#include "common/RecordText.h"

#include <cmath>

namespace barcodehub::billing {

bool BillCalculator::calculate(const std::vector<BillLine>& lines, BillTotals& totals,
                               std::string* error) {
    totals = BillTotals{};
    if (lines.empty()) {
        if (error) *error = "A bill needs at least one product.";
        return false;
    }
    for (const auto& line : lines) {
        const bool bad = !std::isfinite(line.unitPrice) || line.unitPrice < 0.0 ||
                         !std::isfinite(line.taxPerUnit) || line.taxPerUnit < 0.0 ||
                         !std::isfinite(line.discountPerUnit) || line.discountPerUnit < 0.0 ||
                         !std::isfinite(line.quantity) || line.quantity <= 0.0;
        if (bad) {
            if (error) *error = "Invalid price, tax, discount or quantity for " + line.productAcronym + ".";
            return false;
        }
        totals.withoutTax += line.unitPrice * line.quantity;
        totals.tax += line.taxPerUnit * line.quantity;
        totals.discount += line.discountPerUnit * line.quantity;
    }
    totals.withoutTax = common::roundMoney(totals.withoutTax);
    totals.tax = common::roundMoney(totals.tax);
    totals.discount = common::roundMoney(totals.discount);
    totals.payable = common::roundMoney(totals.withoutTax + totals.tax - totals.discount);
    if (totals.payable < 0.0) {
        if (error) *error = "Discount is larger than the bill.";
        totals = BillTotals{};
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

} // namespace barcodehub::billing

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/billing/BillingService.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "billing/BillingService.h"

#include "billing/BillCalculator.h"
#include "common/RecordText.h"

namespace barcodehub::billing {
namespace {

void clearError(std::string* error) {
    if (error) error->clear();
}

bool fail(std::string* error, const std::string& why) {
    if (error) *error = why;
    return false;
}

} // namespace

BillingService::BillingService(order::OrderManager& orders, BillRepository& bills,
                               ProductLookup lookup, PaymentMethodRegistry methods)
    : orders_(orders), bills_(bills), lookup_(std::move(lookup)), methods_(std::move(methods)) {}

bool BillingService::preview(const std::string& orderId, Bill& bill, std::string* error) const {
    clearError(error);
    order::Order order;
    if (!orders_.findOrder(orderId, order, error)) return false;

    std::vector<order::OrderDetail> details;
    if (!orders_.loadOrderDetails(orderId, details, error)) return false;
    if (details.empty()) return fail(error, "Order " + orderId + " has no products.");

    Bill result;
    result.customerName = order.customerName;
    result.orderId = order.id;
    result.paymentStatus = order::toString(order.status);
    for (const auto& detail : details) {
        order::ProductSnapshot product;
        if (!lookup_ || !lookup_(detail.productId, product))
            return fail(error, "Product " + detail.productId + " was not found.");
        BillLine line;
        line.productAcronym = product.acronym;
        line.unitPrice = product.unitPrice;
        line.taxPerUnit = product.taxPerUnit;
        line.discountPerUnit = product.discountPerUnit;
        line.quantity = detail.quantity;
        result.lines.push_back(line);
    }
    if (!BillCalculator::calculate(result.lines, result.totals, error)) return false;

    bill = std::move(result);
    return true;
}

bool BillingService::checkout(const std::string& orderId, const PaymentRequest& payment,
                              Bill& bill, double* change, std::string* error) {
    clearError(error);
    if (change) *change = 0.0;

    order::Order order;
    if (!orders_.findOrder(orderId, order, error)) return false;
    if (order.status == order::OrderStatus::Paid) return fail(error, "Order " + orderId + " is already paid.");
    if (order.status == order::OrderStatus::Archived) return fail(error, "Order " + orderId + " is archived.");

    Bill result;
    if (!preview(orderId, result, error)) return false;

    // 1. payment rules of the chosen mode
    const IPaymentMethod* method = methods_.find(payment.mode);
    if (!method) return fail(error, "This payment mode is not available.");
    const PaymentResult paid = method->process(payment, result.totals.payable);
    if (!paid.ok) return fail(error, paid.error);

    // 2. Bill ID
    std::vector<Bill> existing;
    if (!bills_.loadAll(existing, error)) return false;
    result.id = BillRepository::nextBillId(common::currentDate(), existing, error);
    if (result.id.empty()) return false;

    // 3. order becomes paid (Order.txt)
    order::Order paidOrder = order;
    paidOrder.status = order::OrderStatus::Paid;
    paidOrder.paymentAmount = result.totals.payable;
    paidOrder.paymentMode = payment.mode;
    paidOrder.paymentMerchant = payment.mode == order::PaymentMode::Cash ? std::string() : payment.merchant;
    if (!orders_.updateOrder(paidOrder, error)) return false;
    result.paymentStatus = order::toString(paidOrder.status);

    // 4. bill is saved (Bill.txt); if that fails the order goes back to how it was
    if (!bills_.append(result, error)) {
        std::string ignored;
        orders_.updateOrder(order, &ignored);
        return false;
    }

    bill = std::move(result);
    if (change) *change = paid.change;
    return true;
}

} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/billing/BillRecordCodec.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "billing/BillRecordCodec.h"

#include "common/RecordText.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <vector>

namespace barcodehub::billing {

std::string BillRecordCodec::format(const Bill& bill, const BillLine& line) {
    using common::encodeField;
    using common::formatMoney;
    using common::formatQuantity;
    return bill.id + ' ' +
           encodeField(bill.customerName) + ' ' +
           bill.orderId + ' ' +
           encodeField(line.productAcronym) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(line.unitPrice) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(line.discountPerUnit) + ' ' +
           formatQuantity(line.quantity) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(bill.totals.withoutTax) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(bill.totals.tax) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(bill.totals.discount) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(bill.totals.payable) + ' ' +
           encodeField(bill.paymentStatus) + " #";
}

bool BillRecordCodec::parse(const std::string& record, Bill& header, BillLine& line,
                            std::string* error) {
    const auto fail = [&](const std::string& why) {
        if (error) *error = why;
        return false;
    };

    std::vector<std::string> t;
    if (!common::splitRecord(record, t)) return fail("record must end with '#'.");
    if (t.size() != 12) return fail("expected 12 fields, found " + std::to_string(t.size()) + ".");

    const bool nineDigits = t[0].size() == 9 &&
        std::all_of(t[0].begin(), t[0].end(), [](unsigned char c) { return std::isdigit(c) != 0; });
    if (!nineDigits) return fail("Bill ID must contain exactly 9 digits.");

    Bill h;
    BillLine l;
    h.id = t[0];
    h.orderId = t[2];
    if (!common::decodeField(t[1], h.customerName) ||
        !common::decodeField(t[3], l.productAcronym) ||
        !common::decodeField(t[11], h.paymentStatus)) return fail("bad text encoding.");

    if (!common::parseNumber(t[4], l.unitPrice) || !common::parseNumber(t[5], l.discountPerUnit) ||
        !common::parseNumber(t[6], l.quantity) || !common::parseNumber(t[7], h.totals.withoutTax) ||
        !common::parseNumber(t[8], h.totals.tax) || !common::parseNumber(t[9], h.totals.discount) ||
        !common::parseNumber(t[10], h.totals.payable)) return fail("a numeric field is not a number.");

    header = h;
    line = l;
    return true;
}

} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/billing/BillRepository.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "billing/BillRepository.h"

#include "billing/BillRecordCodec.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <fstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <sstream>
#include <system_error>

namespace barcodehub::billing {

BillRepository::BillRepository(std::filesystem::path billFile) : file_(std::move(billFile)) {}

bool BillRepository::loadAll(std::vector<Bill>& bills, std::string* error) const {
    bills.clear();
    std::error_code ec;
    if (!std::filesystem::exists(file_, ec)) return true;

    std::ifstream in(file_);
    if (!in) {
        if (error) *error = "Cannot open bill file: " + file_.string();
        return false;
    }

    std::string text;
    int lineNo = 0;
    while (std::getline(in, text)) {
        ++lineNo;
        if (text.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n") == std::string::npos) continue;   // blank line

        Bill header;
        BillLine line;
        std::string why;
        if (!BillRecordCodec::parse(text, header, line, &why)) {
            if (error) *error = file_.filename().string() + " line " + std::to_string(lineNo) + ": " + why;
            return false;
        }
        const auto same = std::find_if(bills.begin(), bills.end(),
                                       [&](const Bill& b) { return b.id == header.id; });
        if (same == bills.end()) {
            header.lines.push_back(line);
            bills.push_back(std::move(header));
        } else {
            same->lines.push_back(line);
        }
    }
    return true;
}

bool BillRepository::append(const Bill& bill, std::string* error) {
    if (bill.lines.empty()) {
        if (error) *error = "Cannot save a bill without products.";
        return false;
    }
    std::vector<Bill> all;
    if (!loadAll(all, error)) return false;
    for (const auto& existing : all) {
        if (existing.id == bill.id) {
            if (error) *error = "Bill ID " + bill.id + " already exists.";
            return false;
        }
    }
    all.push_back(bill);

    std::error_code ec;
    if (!file_.parent_path().empty()) std::filesystem::create_directories(file_.parent_path(), ec);
    const auto temp = file_.string() + ".tmp";
    {
        std::ofstream out(temp, std::ios::trunc);
        if (!out) {
            if (error) *error = "Cannot write temporary bill file: " + temp;
            return false;
        }
        for (const auto& b : all)
            for (const auto& line : b.lines) out << BillRecordCodec::format(b, line) << '\n';
        out.flush();
        if (!out) {
            if (error) *error = "Failed while writing bills (disk full or no permission?).";
            std::filesystem::remove(temp, ec);
            return false;
        }
    }
    std::filesystem::rename(temp, file_, ec);
    if (ec) {   // some platforms refuse to rename over an existing file
        ec.clear();
        std::filesystem::copy_file(temp, file_, std::filesystem::copy_options::overwrite_existing, ec);
        std::error_code ignored;
        std::filesystem::remove(temp, ignored);
        if (ec) {
            if (error) *error = "Cannot replace bill file: " + ec.message();
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

std::string BillRepository::nextBillId(const std::string& date, const std::vector<Bill>& existing,
                                       std::string* error) {
    if (date.size() != 10 || date[4] != '-' || date[7] != '-') {
        if (error) *error = "Cannot generate a Bill ID from an invalid date.";
        return {};
    }
    const std::string prefix = date.substr(2, 2) + date.substr(5, 2) + date.substr(8, 2);   // YYMMDD
    int highest = 0;
    for (const auto& bill : existing) {
        if (bill.id.size() == 9 && bill.id.compare(0, 6, prefix) == 0) {
            try {
                highest = std::max(highest, std::stoi(bill.id.substr(6, 3)));
            } catch (...) {
            }
        }
    }
    if (highest >= 999) {
        if (error) *error = "Daily Bill ID limit reached (999 bills for this date).";
        return {};
    }
    std::ostringstream id;
    id << prefix << std::setw(3) << std::setfill('0') << (highest + 1);
    return id.str();
}

} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/billing/PaymentMethod.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "billing/PaymentMethod.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <cmath>

namespace barcodehub::billing {
namespace {

bool validMerchant(const std::string& name) {
    const auto first = name.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n");
    if (first == std::string::npos) return false;
    return name.find('#') == std::string::npos;
}

PaymentResult electronic(const PaymentRequest& request, const char* what) {
    PaymentResult result;
    if (!validMerchant(request.merchant)) {
        result.error = std::string("Enter the ") + what + " name (for example ABL or JazzCash).";
        return result;
    }
    result.ok = true;
    return result;
}

} // namespace

PaymentResult CashPayment::process(const PaymentRequest& request, double payable) const {
    PaymentResult result;
    if (!std::isfinite(request.amountTendered) || request.amountTendered < 0.0) {
        result.error = "Invalid cash amount tendered.";
        return result;
    }
    if (request.amountTendered + 1e-9 < payable) {
        result.error = "Cash received is less than the amount payable.";
        return result;
    }
    result.ok = true;
    result.change = std::round((request.amountTendered - payable) * 100.0) / 100.0;
    return result;
}

PaymentResult CardPayment::process(const PaymentRequest& request, double) const {
    return electronic(request, "bank");
}

PaymentResult WalletPayment::process(const PaymentRequest& request, double) const {
    return electronic(request, "wallet");
}

PaymentMethodRegistry PaymentMethodRegistry::withDefaults() {
    PaymentMethodRegistry registry;
    registry.add(std::make_unique<CashPayment>());
    registry.add(std::make_unique<CardPayment>());
    registry.add(std::make_unique<WalletPayment>());
    return registry;
}

void PaymentMethodRegistry::add(std::unique_ptr<IPaymentMethod> method) {
    methods_.push_back(std::shared_ptr<const IPaymentMethod>(std::move(method)));
}

const IPaymentMethod* PaymentMethodRegistry::find(order::PaymentMode mode) const {
    const auto it = std::find_if(methods_.begin(), methods_.end(),
                                 [&](const auto& m) { return m->mode() == mode; });
    return it == methods_.end() ? nullptr : it->get();
}

} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/common/RecordText.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "common/RecordText.h"

#include <cctype>
#include <cmath>
#include <ctime>
#include <iomanip>
#include <locale>
#include <sstream>

namespace barcodehub::common {

std::string encodeField(const std::string& text) {
    if (text.empty()) return "-";
    if (text == "-") return "%2D";
    std::string out;
    for (const unsigned char c : text) {
        if (c == '%') out += "%25";
        else if (c == '_') out += "%5F";
        else if (c == '#') out += "%23";
        else if (std::isspace(c)) out += '_';
        else out += static_cast<char>(c);
    }
    return out;
}

bool decodeField(const std::string& token, std::string& out) {
    out.clear();
    if (token == "-") return true;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < token.size(); ++i) {
        const char c = token[i];
        if (c == '_') {
            out += ' ';
        } else if (c == '%') {
            if (i + 2 >= token.size()) return false;   // truncated %XX
            const std::string hex = token.substr(i + 1, 2);
            if (!std::isxdigit(static_cast<unsigned char>(hex[0])) ||
                !std::isxdigit(static_cast<unsigned char>(hex[1]))) return false;
            out += static_cast<char>(std::stoi(hex, nullptr, 16));
            i += 2;
        } else {
            out += c;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

bool splitRecord(const std::string& rawLine, std::vector<std::string>& tokens) {
    tokens.clear();
    std::string line = rawLine;
    while (!line.empty() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(line.back()))) line.pop_back();
    if (line.empty() || line.back() != '#') return false;
    line.pop_back();
    std::istringstream in(line);
    for (std::string token; in >> token;) tokens.push_back(token);
    return true;
}

bool parseNumber(const std::string& token, double& value) {
    try {
        std::size_t used = 0;
        value = std::stod(token, &used);
        return used == token.size() && std::isfinite(value);
    } catch (...) {
        return false;
    }
}

std::string formatMoney(double value) {
    std::ostringstream out;
    out.imbue(std::locale::classic());
    out << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << value;
    return out.str();
}

std::string formatQuantity(double value) {
    std::ostringstream out;
    out.imbue(std::locale::classic());
    out << std::fixed << std::setprecision(3) << value;
    std::string text = out.str();
    while (!text.empty() && text.back() == '0') text.pop_back();
    if (!text.empty() && text.back() == '.') text.pop_back();
    return text.empty() ? "0" : text;
}

double roundMoney(double value) {
    return std::round(value * 100.0) / 100.0;
}

std::string currentDate() {
    const std::time_t now = std::time(nullptr);
    std::tm local{};
#ifdef _WIN32
    localtime_s(&local, &now);
#else
    localtime_r(&now, &local);
#endif
    std::ostringstream out;
    out << std::put_time(&local, "%Y-%m-%d");
    return out.str();
}

} // namespace barcodehub::common
