--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/Dialogs.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

/**
 * @file Dialogs.h
 * @brief Modal dialog windows and receipt generation utilities for the POS GUI.
 * @author Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)
 *
 * Implements the interactive dialog workflows assigned for Increment 2:
 * - Payment selection modal (supporting Cash, Card, and Mobile Wallet).
 * - Receipt viewer modal (displaying finalized bill details and printable format).
 * - Product Description modal (displaying specifications, stock, and barcode graphic).
 * - Order details inspector.
 */

#include "AppState.h"
#include "UserRole.h"
#include <QString>
#include <QVector>

class QWidget;

namespace Dialogs
{
    /** What the payment dialog needs to know about the order being settled. */
    struct PaymentRequest
    {
        QString orderId;                // e.g. "26110122"
        QString customer, contact;
        QVector<ReceiptLine> lines;     // cart lines; per-unit discount = the product's own discount only (Pricing.h adds the rest)
    };

    /** What the cashier chose in the payment dialog. */
    struct PaymentResult
    {
        QString mode;              // "Cash" | "Card" | "Wallet"
        QString merchant;          // gateway / wallet name for Card and Wallet
        double tendered = 0;       // cash received (Cash only)
        double specialPercent = 0; // special discount typed in the dialog, 0..8 (Pricing::clampSpecial)
    };

    /**
     * @brief "Complete Order Payment": billing breakdown (item / seasonal / special discount, tax), tender mode,
     *        cash numpad with quick-tender keys, balance to return. Returns false when cancelled.
     */
    bool payment(QWidget *parent, const PaymentRequest &req, PaymentResult &res);

    /**
     * @brief "Manager Approval Required": a paid order may only be edited (within 7 days of payment) after a manager
     *        signs it off. Returns true when approved; fills the manager's name and the audit reason.
     */
    bool managerApproval(QWidget *parent, const OrderRecord &order, QString *managerName, QString *reason);

    /**
     * @brief "Add New Product to Catalog" (existing == nullptr) or edit an existing product: category, specifications,
     *        EAN-8 barcode generator. Saves through AppState::addProduct / updateProduct. Returns true if saved.
     */
    bool editProduct(QWidget *parent, const ProductInfo *existing);

    /**
     * @brief Displays a clean printable-style receipt preview dialog for a finalized order.
     * @param parent Owning parent widget.
     * @param order Fully recorded order with items, customer details, and payment metadata.
     */
    void receipt(QWidget *parent, const OrderRecord &order);

    /**
     * @brief Displays detailed line items, timestamps, and customer metadata for an order.
     * @param parent Owning parent widget.
     * @param order Target OrderRecord to inspect.
     */
    void orderDetails(QWidget *parent, const OrderRecord &order);

    /**
     * @brief Displays the Product Description popup dialog.
     *
     * Fulfills Increment 2 requirement: Cashier can inspect comprehensive product
     * attributes (acronym, description, pricing, tax, discount, available inventory)
     * along with rendered barcode graphic.
     *
     * @param parent Owning parent widget.
     * @param productId Numeric identifier of the product to query.
     */
    void productDescription(QWidget *parent, int productId);

    /**
     * @brief Displays any syntax or data integrity issues detected during Product.txt parsing.
     * @param parent Owning parent widget.
     */
    void loadIssues(QWidget *parent);

    /**
     * @brief Generates formatted monospace text representation of an order's finalized bill receipt.
     *
     * Contains business header, itemized quantities, unit prices, discounts, taxes,
     * total payable, amount tendered, change returned, and payment method details.
     *
     * @param order Recorded order data.
     * @return Formatted multi-line receipt text.
     */
    QString receiptText(const OrderRecord &order);

    /** @brief The bill as formatted HTML (used on screen and for the PDF). */
    QString receiptHtml(const OrderRecord &order);

    /** @brief Saves the bill as a PDF file; returns false if it could not be written. */
    bool saveReceiptPdf(const OrderRecord &order, const QString &path);
    /**
     * @brief Sign-in screen: the user types a name and presses Cashier or Manager.
     * @param[out] role Chosen role.
     * @param[out] name Typed name (the role name if left empty).
     * @return False if the window was closed without signing in.
     */
    bool login(QWidget *parent, UserRole &role, QString &name);
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/OrderRules.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
// Order-editing rules (Increment 3, requirement 3). One place, no widgets (SRP):
//  - a PAID order may be edited within 7 days of its payment, and only after a manager approves it;
//  - an ON HOLD / IN PROGRESS order is simply resumed (no approval);
//  - an ARCHIVED order is never opened again.
// Front-end helper only: it works on the OrderRecord that AppState already loads from Order.txt.
#include "AppState.h"
#include <QDateTime>
#include <QString>

namespace OrderRules
{
    constexpr int kEditWindowDays = 7;

    // Moment the edit window closes (payment time + 7 days).
    inline QDateTime editDeadline(const OrderRecord &o) { return o.time.addDays(kEditWindowDays); }

    inline bool isPaid(const OrderRecord &o) { return o.status == "Paid"; }
    inline bool isResumable(const OrderRecord &o) { return o.status == "On Hold" || o.status == "In Progress"; }

    // Whole days still left to edit a paid order (rounded up: 6 days 3 h left -> 7). 0 = window closed.
    inline int editDaysLeft(const OrderRecord &o, const QDateTime &now = QDateTime::currentDateTime())
    {
        if (!isPaid(o))
            return 0;
        const qint64 secs = now.secsTo(editDeadline(o));
        if (secs <= 0)
            return 0;
        return int((secs + 86399) / 86400);
    }

    inline bool paidEditable(const OrderRecord &o, const QDateTime &now = QDateTime::currentDateTime())
    {
        return editDaysLeft(o, now) > 0;
    }

    // "Editable for 5 days" / "Editable for 1 day"
    inline QString editBadgeText(int daysLeft)
    {
        return QString("Editable for %1 day%2").arg(daysLeft).arg(daysLeft == 1 ? "" : "s");
    }

    // Badge colour: green while there is plenty of time, amber for the last three days (W::badge kinds).
    inline QString editBadgeKind(int daysLeft) { return daysLeft <= 3 ? "amber" : "green"; }

    // Whole days since the order was paid (for the "window closed" message).
    inline int daysSincePaid(const OrderRecord &o, const QDateTime &now = QDateTime::currentDateTime())
    {
        return int(o.time.daysTo(now));
    }
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/OrdersPage.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
// Orders Management: search/filter every order, open one for editing, manager approval for paid orders.
#include "AppState.h"
#include "UserRole.h"
#include <QWidget>

class QLabel;
class QLineEdit;
class QTableWidget;
class QPushButton;

class OrdersPage : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit OrdersPage(QWidget *parent = nullptr);
    void setSearch(const QString &text);
    void setRole(UserRole role); // Manager approves paid-order edits itself; Cashier must ask for approval
signals:
    void toast(const QString &message);
    void resumeOrder(const QString &orderId); // open an in-progress order from Order.txt in Record Order
    void resumeParked(const QString &holdCode); // resume an order parked with F2 ("HLD-108") in Record Order
    void editOrder(const QString &orderId);   // open a (paid, approved) order for editing in Record Order
    void showBill(const QString &orderId);    // jump to Bills for this order

private:
    void rebuild();
    void openPaid(const OrderRecord &order); // 7-day window + manager approval, then editOrder()
    QLineEdit *m_orderSearch = nullptr, *m_custSearch = nullptr;
    QTableWidget *m_table = nullptr;
    QString m_filter = "All";
    QVector<QPushButton *> m_tabs;
    QLabel *m_showing = nullptr;
    UserRole m_role = UserRole::Cashier;
};

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/Pricing.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
// Seasonal + special discount arithmetic for the checkout screens.
//
// The Increment 2 backend stores one "discount per unit" for every bill line. The seasonal discount (active season
// from the Discounts page) and the special discount (cashier, at most 8 percent) are therefore *folded into that
// per-unit discount* before the order is handed to the backend, so Order.txt / Bill.txt totals always equal what the
// customer paid. The breakdown (item / seasonal / special) is kept on the OrderRecord for the receipt of this session.
#include "AppState.h"
#include <QDate>

namespace Pricing
{
    struct Quote
    {
        double gross = 0;        // sum of unit price * qty (no tax)
        double itemDiscount = 0; // product discounts from Product.txt
        double seasonal = 0;     // seasonal discount (active season, restricted to its categories)
        double special = 0;      // special discount (cashier typed percent, capped at 8)
        double tax = 0;
        double payable = 0;      // gross - itemDiscount - seasonal - special + tax
        double seasonalPercent = 0;
        double specialPercent = 0; // after capping
        QString seasonName;
        int units = 0, lines = 0;
        double totalDiscount() const { return itemDiscount + seasonal + special; }
    };

    // Cap a typed special-discount percent to 0..8 (UiStore::kSpecialDiscountCapPercent).
    double clampSpecial(double percent);

    // `lines` carry only the product's own discount per unit. Returns the quote for `on` (default today).
    Quote quote(const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines, double specialPercent = 0, const QDate &on = QDate::currentDate());

    // Same lines with the seasonal and special amounts folded into each line's per-unit discount (2 decimals).
    QVector<ReceiptLine> fold(const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines, double specialPercent = 0,
                              const QDate &on = QDate::currentDate());

    // Copy the quote's breakdown into an OrderRecord (subtotal/discount/tax/total + seasonal/special parts).
    void apply(OrderRecord &order, const Quote &q);
}

#pragma once

/**
 * @file RecordOrderPage.h
 * @brief Primary interactive POS cashier screen for recording orders and completing checkout.
 * @author Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)
 *
 * Implements the core Point of Sale GUI workflow for Increment 2:
 * - Dual-pane layout: Camera scanner & manual barcode entry on the left, shopping cart & totals on the right.
 * - Keyboard shortcuts: F9 (Camera Scan), F4 (Product Description), F10 (Checkout), F2 (Focus Barcode Input).
 * - Real-time stock validation and dynamic bill subtotal calculation.
 * - Integration with Dialogs::payment() and Dialogs::receipt().
 */

#include "AppState.h"
#include "UserRole.h"
#include <QVector>
#include <QWidget>
#include <optional>

class QHBoxLayout;
class QLabel;
class QLineEdit;
class QPushButton;
class QTableWidget;
class CameraFeedWidget;

/**
 * @class RecordOrderPage
 * @brief Main cashier interface managing active order drafts, shopping cart items, and billing settlement.
 */
class RecordOrderPage : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    /**
     * @brief Constructs the RecordOrderPage and builds its user interface components.
     * @param parent Optional parent widget.
     */
    explicit RecordOrderPage(QWidget* parent = nullptr);

public slots:
    /** @brief Shifts keyboard focus to the barcode input field (F2). */
    void focusInput();

    /** @brief Initiates camera barcode scanning or simulates a scan input (F9 shortcut). */
    void simulateScan();

    /** @brief Opens the Product Description modal for the currently highlighted or entered item (F4 shortcut). */
    void quickLookup();

    /** @brief Triggers order checkout and launches the payment settlement dialog (F10 shortcut). */
    void checkout();

    /**
     * @brief Processes a scanned or typed barcode string, looking up matching inventory.
     * @param code Decoded barcode string.
     * @param fromScanner True if emitted from camera scanner; false if typed manually.
     */
    void showCode(const QString& code, bool fromScanner);

    /** @brief Discards the current cart and resets the interface for a new customer transaction. */
    void newDraft();

    /** @brief Opens the Product Description for the product on screen (F4). */
    void describeCurrent();

    /** @brief Puts the current order on hold (parked in UiStore) and starts a fresh one (F2). One press, no dialog. */
    void holdOrder();

    /** @brief Resumes a parked (on-hold) order by its code, e.g. "HLD-108" (click on the parked strip). */
    void resumeParked(const QString& code);

    /**
     * @brief Opens an existing order in this screen for editing. Called after the manager approved the edit of a paid
     *        order (Orders page) or when resuming an in-progress order. The screen shows an "Editing order" banner.
     */
    void loadOrderForEdit(const QString& orderId);

    /** @brief Role of the signed-in user (Manager may also approve edits on the spot). */
    void setRole(UserRole role);

signals:
    /** @brief Emits a transient user notification toast message on the main window. */
    void toast(const QString& message);

    /** @brief A bill was just created for this order: the shell opens it on the Bills page ("New Order [Space]" there). */
    void billReady(const QString& orderId);

private:
    /**
     * @struct CartLine
     * @brief In-memory shopping cart item tracking product metadata and selected quantity.
     */
    struct CartLine
    {
        ProductInfo p;  ///< Cached snapshot of product details.
        int qty = 1;    ///< Quantity added to cart.
    };

    QWidget* buildCustomerStrip();
    QWidget* buildParkedStrip(); ///< "Parked (n)" chips of the orders on hold
    QWidget* buildLeft();
    QWidget* buildRight();
    void setPreview(const std::optional<ProductInfo>& p);
    void addToCart(const ProductInfo& p, int qty = 1);
    void changeQty(int row, int delta);
    void removeRow(int row);
    void rebuildTable();
    void updateTotals();
    void loadDemoDraft();
    void rebuildParked();        ///< redraws the on-hold chips (also keeps the "4m ago" texts fresh)
    QVector<ReceiptLine> cartLines() const;

    UserRole m_role = UserRole::Cashier;
    QString m_editingId; ///< order opened for editing from the Orders page; empty while recording a new order
    QVector<CartLine> m_cart;
    std::optional<ProductInfo> m_preview;
    int m_nextCustomLine = 1;

    QLineEdit* m_customer;
    QLineEdit* m_contact;
    QLineEdit* m_manual;
    QLabel* m_sessionId;

    CameraFeedWidget* m_feed;
    QPushButton* m_camToggle;
    QPushButton* m_addBtn;
    QPushButton* m_descBtn;
    QLabel* m_pName;
    QLabel* m_pPrice;
    QLabel* m_pMeta;
    QLabel* m_pStock;
    QLabel* m_pThumb;
    QLabel* m_pBadge;

    QTableWidget* m_table;
    QLabel* m_itemsBadge;
    QLabel* m_gross;
    QLabel* m_disc;
    QLabel* m_tax;
    QLabel* m_total;
    QPushButton* m_checkout;
    QPushButton* m_hold;
    QLabel* m_parkTitle;
    QLabel* m_parkEmpty;
    QHBoxLayout* m_parkChips;
};

#pragma once
// Business Reports (manager): six tabular reports with summary tiles, totals, CSV export and PDF print.
//   1 Incomplete orders      2 Completed orders     3 Cash sales (daily / weekly / monthly / yearly / custom range)
//   4 All sales              5 Top 10 most sold     6 Top 10 by remaining stock
// Front end only: every report is computed from what AppState / UiStore already hold, so no new data file is needed.
#include <QDate>
#include <QStringList>
#include <QVector>
#include <QWidget>

class QDateEdit;
class QFrame;
class QLabel;
class QPushButton;
class QTableWidget;

class ReportsPage : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit ReportsPage(QWidget *parent = nullptr);
    void refresh();
signals:
    void toast(const QString &message);

private:
    // One computed report, independent of widgets (so a new report only adds a function, see ReportsPage.cpp).
    struct Report
    {
        QString title, subtitle;
        QString captions[3], values[3], subs[3]; // summary tiles
        QStringList headers;
        QVector<QStringList> rows;
        QVector<int> rightCols;  // right-aligned numeric columns
        int statusCol = -1;      // column drawn as an order-status badge
        int barCol = -1;         // column drawn as a horizontal bar (Top 10 reports)
        QVector<double> bars;    // 0..1 per row
        QString footerLabel, footerValue, footerExtra;
        QString emptyText;
    };
    using Builder = Report (ReportsPage::*)() const;

    Report incomplete() const;
    Report completed() const;
    Report cashSales() const;
    Report allSales() const;
    Report topSold() const;
    Report topStock() const;

    void selectTab(int index);
    void selectPeriod(const QString &name);
    void applyCustomRange();
    void render();
    void exportCsv();
    void printPdf();

    QVector<QPushButton *> m_tabs, m_periods;
    QFrame *m_periodCard = nullptr;
    QDateEdit *m_from = nullptr, *m_to = nullptr;
    QLabel *m_cap[3] = {}, *m_val[3] = {}, *m_sub[3] = {};
    QLabel *m_tableTitle = nullptr, *m_tableSub = nullptr, *m_empty = nullptr;
    QLabel *m_footLabel = nullptr, *m_footValue = nullptr, *m_footExtra = nullptr;
    QTableWidget *m_table = nullptr;
    int m_tab = 0;
    QDate m_rangeFrom, m_rangeTo;
    Report m_current; // what the table shows now (used by the exports)
};

    };

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/UiStore.h

#pragma once
// Front-end only state for the Increment 3 screens whose back-end is not built yet:
// product categories, promotional seasons, parked (on-hold) orders and the manager sign-off check.
// Nothing here touches the data files - when the back-end arrives (PCategory.txt, Seasons.txt, ...), replace the
// bodies of this class and keep its interface, so the pages do not change.
#include "AppState.h"
#include <QDate>
#include <QDateTime>
#include <QObject>
#include <QString>
#include <QStringList>
#include <QVector>

struct Category
{
    QString id;      // 2 digits, becomes the first two digits of every barcode in the category
    QString name;
    QString acronym; // up to 3 letters
    QString color;   // theme tag, e.g. "#2554d8"
};

struct Season
{
    QString id;   // "SEA-2026-01"
    QString name;
    QDate start, end;
    double percent = 0;
    bool active = false;       // isActive in Seasons.txt
    QStringList categories;    // empty = all categories
    // "active" | "scheduled" | "expired" for a given day (an inactive season is never "active")
    QString statusOn(const QDate &d) const;
};

struct ParkedOrder
{
    QString code; // "HLD-108"
    QString customer, contact;
    QDateTime parkedAt;
    QVector<ReceiptLine> lines;
    int itemCount() const;
    double total() const; // subtotal - discount + tax
};

class UiStore : public QObject
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    static UiStore &instance();
    static constexpr double kSpecialDiscountCapPercent = 8.0; // requirement 8: special discount is at most 8 percent

    // ---- categories ----
    const QVector<Category> &categories() const { return m_categories; }
    const Category *category(const QString &id) const;
    int productCount(const QString &id) const; // products whose barcode starts with this id (from Product.txt)
    QString rangeText(const QString &id) const; // "10000000 - 10999999"
    QStringList freeIds(int n = 3) const;       // unused ids from 10 to 99, e.g. {"80","85","90"}
    // Empty string when valid; otherwise the message to show (e.g. the "already in use" validation error).
    QString validateCategory(const QString &id, const QString &name, const QString &acronym,
                             const QString &ignoreId = QString()) const;
    bool addCategory(const Category &c, QString *error = nullptr);
    bool updateCategory(const QString &oldId, const Category &c, QString *error = nullptr);
    bool removeCategory(const QString &id, QString *error = nullptr); // refused while products use it

    // ---- seasons (Seasons.txt: start date, end date, percentage, isActive) ----
    const QVector<Season> &seasons() const { return m_seasons; }
    void addSeason(const Season &s);
    void updateSeason(const QString &id, const Season &s);
    void removeSeason(const QString &id);
    void setSeasonActive(const QString &id, bool on);
    QString nextSeasonId() const;
    const Season *activeSeason(const QDate &d = QDate::currentDate()) const; // first active season covering d
    QString validateSeason(const QString &name, const QDate &start, const QDate &end, double percent) const;

    // ---- on-hold orders ----
    const QVector<ParkedOrder> &parked() const { return m_parked; }
    QString park(const QString &customer, const QString &contact, const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines);
    bool takeParked(const QString &code, ParkedOrder &out); // removes it from the list
    void discardParked(const QString &code);

    // ---- manager sign-off (needed to edit a paid order) ----
    // Demo check only: replace with real staff accounts when the back-end has them.
    static QString demoManagerId() { return QStringLiteral("MGR-004"); }
    static QString demoManagerName() { return QStringLiteral("Salman Tariq"); }
    static QString demoManagerPin() { return QStringLiteral("1234"); }
    bool verifyManager(const QString &staffId, const QString &pin, QString *managerName = nullptr) const;

signals:
    void categoriesChanged();
    void seasonsChanged();
    void parkedChanged();

private:
    UiStore();
    QVector<Category> m_categories;
    QVector<Season> m_seasons;
    QVector<ParkedOrder> m_parked;
    int m_nextHold = 109;
};


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/UserRole.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
#include <QString>

// Who is using the terminal. Chosen once at sign-in.
enum class UserRole
{
    Cashier, // records orders, edits orders (paid ones after manager approval), views products, prints bills
    Manager  // everything a cashier can, plus categories, products, inventory, discounts and reports
};

// Sidebar pages, in sidebar order. MainWindow::Page has exactly the same values.
namespace Pages
{
    enum Id
    {
        Dashboard,
        RecordOrder,
        Orders,
        Inventory,
        Products,
        Categories,
        Scanner,
        Bills,
        Reports,
        Discounts,
        Settings,
        Count
    };
}

// Single place that decides what each role may open (SRP).
// Adding a role or changing a permission only touches this file (OCP for the rest of the UI).
namespace RolePolicy
{
inline bool isManagerOnly(int page)
{
    return page == Pages::Inventory || page == Pages::Categories || page == Pages::Reports ||
           page == Pages::Discounts;
}

inline bool canOpen(UserRole role, int page)
{
    if (page < 0 || page >= Pages::Count)
        return false;
    return role == UserRole::Manager || !isManagerOnly(page);
}

// Only a manager may add or edit products, generate barcodes and change stock (Products page is view-only for cashiers).
inline bool canEditCatalogue(UserRole role) { return role == UserRole::Manager; }

// First page shown after signing in.
inline int startPage(UserRole role)
{
    return role == UserRole::Manager ? int(Pages::Dashboard) : int(Pages::RecordOrder);
}

inline QString name(UserRole role)
{
    return role == UserRole::Manager ? QStringLiteral("Manager") : QStringLiteral("Cashier");
}

// "Terminal Manager" / "Terminal Cashier" (sidebar user card)
inline QString title(UserRole role)
{
    return role == UserRole::Manager ? QStringLiteral("Terminal Manager") : QStringLiteral("Terminal Cashier");
}
} // namespace RolePolicy

                           QWidget *rightWidget = nullptr);

class QVideoFrame;

// Live camera feed (Qt Multimedia) with barcode detection by the pure-C++ Decoder.
// Without Qt Multimedia the widget shows a clear "camera unavailable" message.
class CameraFeedWidget : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit CameraFeedWidget(QWidget *parent = nullptr);
    ~CameraFeedWidget() override;
    bool running() const { return m_running; }
    void setRunning(bool on);
    void setCode(const QString &code); // barcode digits shown in the viewfinder
    // Green confirmation banner on the live picture (e.g. "40001029 - Olpers Milk"); fades after ~3 s.
    void showResult(const QString &text, bool ok = true);
signals:
    void barcodeDetected(const QString &ean);
    void scanError(const QString &error);

public:
    QSize sizeHint() const override { return {420, 236}; }
    QSize minimumSizeHint() const override { return {260, 150}; }

protected:
    void paintEvent(QPaintEvent *) override;
    void mousePressEvent(QMouseEvent *e) override;
    void mouseMoveEvent(QMouseEvent *e) override;
    void mouseReleaseEvent(QMouseEvent *e) override;

private:
    bool nearLine(const QPoint &p) const;
    void moveLine(const QPoint &p);
    double m_lineFrac = 0.5; // aiming line height, 0..1 of the picture (drag it with the mouse)
    bool m_drag = false;
    double m_sweepPhase = 0;
    QString m_result;
    bool m_resultOk = true;
    QElapsedTimer m_resultClock;
    bool m_running = false;
    QString m_code;
    QString m_lastDetected;
    QElapsedTimer m_lastEmit; // last time any barcode was seen in the picture
    bool m_armed = true;      // false after a read, until the barcode leaves the picture
    QImage m_frame;
    QRectF m_hitBox;       // where the barcode was found, in camera-picture pixels
    QString m_hitText;     // the code that was read
    QElapsedTimer m_hitClock;
    QTimer m_timer;
#ifdef WITH_CAMERA
    struct CameraRig;
    void onImage(const QImage &image); // show + decode one camera picture
    std::unique_ptr<CameraRig> m_rig;
    QElapsedTimer m_decodeClock;
#endif
};

#include "CameraWidget.h"
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QPixmap>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <opencv2/imgcodecs.hpp>
#include <opencv2/imgproc.hpp>
CameraWidget::CameraWidget(QWidget *parent)
    : QWidget(parent), view(new QLabel(this)), status(new QLabel("Camera is off", this)),
      toggle(new QPushButton("Start camera", this)), upload(new QPushButton("Upload image", this))
{
    auto *Layout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
    Layout->addWidget(view);
    view->setMinimumSize(640, 480);
    view->setScaledContents(true);
    // NEW: buttons + status label under the picture
    auto *buttons = new QHBoxLayout();
    buttons->addWidget(toggle);
    buttons->addWidget(upload);
    Layout->addLayout(buttons);
    Layout->addWidget(status);
    connect(&timer, &QTimer::timeout, this, &CameraWidget::onTick);
    connect(toggle, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &CameraWidget::onToggle);
    connect(upload, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &CameraWidget::onUpload);
}
void CameraWidget::start()
{
    if (scanner.openCamera())
    {
        timer.start(30);
        toggle->setText("Stop camera");
        setStatus("Point a barcode at the camera");
    }
    else
    {
        // NEW: error handling - tell the user, and offer the upload instead
        const QString message = QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage());
        setStatus(message + " - use Upload image");
        emit scanError(message);
    }
}
void CameraWidget::stop()
{
    timer.stop();
    scanner.closeCamera(); // NEW: really release the camera, otherwise its light stays on
    toggle->setText("Start camera");
    setStatus("Camera is off");
}
// NEW: Start / Stop button
void CameraWidget::onToggle()
{
    if (timer.isActive())
        stop();
    else
        start();
}
// NEW: upload fallback, choose a picture of a barcode
void CameraWidget::onUpload()
{
    const QString path = QFileDialog::getOpenFileName(this, "Choose a barcode picture", QString(),
                                                      "Pictures (*.png *.jpg *.jpeg *.bmp)");
    if (!path.isEmpty())
        scanImage(path);
}
void CameraWidget::scanImage(const QString &path)
{
    QFile file(path); // read the bytes ourselves, so file names with special letters also work on Windows
    if (!file.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly))
    {
        setStatus("Cannot open the picture");
        emit scanError("Cannot open the picture");
        return;
    }
    const QByteArray bytes = file.readAll();
    const cv::Mat picture = cv::imdecode(cv::Mat(1, bytes.size(), CV_8UC1, (void *)bytes.constData()), cv::IMREAD_COLOR);
    if (picture.empty())
    {
        setStatus("This file is not a picture");
        emit scanError("This file is not a picture");
        return;
    }
    std::string six;
    cv::Rect box;
    if (scanner.frameRead(six, box, picture))
    {
        setStatus("Scanned " + QString::fromStdString(six));
        emit barcodeScan(QString::fromStdString(six));
    }
    else
    {
        const QString message = scanner.badCheckDigit() ? "Wrong check digit in the picture" : "No barcode found in the picture";
        setStatus(message);
        emit scanError(message);
    }
}
void CameraWidget::setStatus(const QString &text)
{
    status->setText(text);
}
QImage CameraWidget::toQImage(const cv::Mat &BGR)
{
    cv::Mat RGB;
    cv::cvtColor(BGR, RGB, cv::COLOR_BGR2RGB);
    QImage img(RGB.data, RGB.cols, RGB.rows, (int)RGB.step, QImage::Format_RGB888);
    return img.copy();
}
void CameraWidget::onTick()
{
    cv::Mat frame;

    if (!scanner.readFrame(frame))
    {
        stop();
        setStatus(QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage()) + " - use Upload image");
        emit scanError(QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage()));
        return;
    }
    std::string six;
    cv::Rect Box;
    bool found = scanner.frameRead(six, Box, frame);
    if (Box.area() > 0)
        cv::rectangle(frame, Box, cv::Scalar(0, 255, 0), 2);
    view->setPixmap(QPixmap::fromImage(toQImage(frame)));
    if (found)
    {
        // NEW: duplicate-scan prevention - the same barcode still in front of the camera is ignored for 2 seconds
        const QString code = QString::fromStdString(six);
        if (code == lastCode && lastClock.isValid() && lastClock.elapsed() < 2000)
            return;
        lastCode = code;
        lastClock.restart();
        setStatus("Scanned " + code);
        emit barcodeScan(code);
    }
    else if (scanner.badCheckDigit())
        setStatus("Wrong check digit - hold the barcode steady");
}

#pragma once
#include <QWidget>
#include <QTimer>
#include <QString>
#include <QImage>
#include <opencv2/core.hpp>
#include "cameraScanner.h"
class QLabel;
class CameraWidget : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit CameraWidget(QWidget *parent = nullptr);
    void start();
    void stop();
signals:
    void barcodeScan(const QString &six);
    void scanError(const QString &message);
private slots:
    void onTick();

private:
    // OpenCV picture to Qt picture
    static QImage toQImage(constcv::Mat &BGR);
    // backend work of decoding and scanning
    cameraScanner scanner;
    QLabel *view;
    QTimer timer;
};

FILE: /Frontend/legacy-camera/main.cpp

#include <QApplication>
#include "CameraWidget.h"

int main(int argc, char *argv[])
{
    QApplication app(argc, argv); // starts Qt
    CameraWidget cam;             // your widget, as its own window
    QObject::connect(&cam, &CameraWidget::barcodeScan, [&cam](const QString &six)
                     {
                         cam.setWindowTitle("Scanned: " + six); // show the digits in the title bar
                     });
    QObject::connect(&cam, &CameraWidget::scanError, [&cam](const QString &msg)
                     {
                         cam.setWindowTitle("Error: " + msg); // show the problem instead
                     });
    cam.show();        // open the window
    cam.start();       // open the camera (connect first, start last)
    return app.exec(); // keep the window alive
}

// "Manager Approval Required": a paid order can be edited (within 7 days of payment) only after a manager signs it off.
// Frontend only: the check goes through UiStore::verifyManager (demo staff id / PIN until the backend has accounts).
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiKit.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QAction>
#include <QComboBox>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace Dialogs
{
    bool managerApproval(QWidget *parent, const OrderRecord &order, QString *managerName, QString *reason)
    {
        auto &store = UiStore::instance();
        Ui::Modal d(parent, "shield", "Manager Approval Required", "SECURITY GATE  •  AUDIT TRAIL", QString(), 560);

        // info banner
        auto *info = W::card(nullptr, "subCard");
        auto *ih = new QHBoxLayout(info);
        ih->setContentsMargins(12, 10, 12, 10);
        ih->setSpacing(10);
        ih->addWidget(W::iconLabel("info", Theme::primary, 20), 0, Qt::AlignTop);
        auto *msg = W::label(QString("This paid order (<b>#%1 – %2 • %3</b>) can only be edited with an authorized manager's "
                                     "approval. Modifying finalized sales alters fiscal records and inventory ledgers.")
                                 .arg(order.id.toHtmlEscaped(),
                                      (order.customer.isEmpty() ? QString("Walk-in Customer") : order.customer).toHtmlEscaped(),
                                      W::money(order.total)));
        msg->setTextFormat(Qt::RichText);
        msg->setWordWrap(true);
        ih->addWidget(msg, 1);
        d.body()->addWidget(info);

        // staff id
        d.body()->addWidget(W::label("MANAGER USERNAME / STAFF ID", "caption"));
        auto *staff = new QLineEdit(UiStore::demoManagerId());
        staff->setMinimumHeight(40);
        staff->addAction(Theme::icon("user", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
        auto *staffOk = staff->addAction(Theme::icon("check", Theme::tertiary, 18), QLineEdit::TrailingPosition);
        d.body()->addWidget(staff);

        // PIN with show / hide
        d.body()->addWidget(W::label("SECURITY PASSWORD / PIN", "caption"));
        auto *pin = new QLineEdit;
        pin->setEchoMode(QLineEdit::Password);
        pin->setPlaceholderText("Manager PIN");
        pin->setMinimumHeight(40);
        pin->addAction(Theme::icon("lock", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
        auto *eye = pin->addAction(Theme::icon("eye", Theme::outline, 18), QLineEdit::TrailingPosition);
        eye->setCheckable(true);
        eye->setToolTip("Show / hide the PIN");
        QObject::connect(eye, &QAction::toggled, &d, [pin](bool on)
                         { pin->setEchoMode(on ? QLineEdit::Normal : QLineEdit::Password); });
        d.body()->addWidget(pin);
        d.body()->addWidget(W::label(QString("Demo account: %1 / PIN %2").arg(UiStore::demoManagerId(), UiStore::demoManagerPin()), "muted"));

        // reason (goes to the audit log)
        d.body()->addWidget(W::label("REASON FOR EDIT / AUDIT LOG ENTRY", "caption"));
        auto *why = new QComboBox;
        why->setEditable(true);
        why->setMinimumHeight(40);
        why->addItems({"Customer item exchange / barcode error", "Wrong quantity entered", "Price or discount correction",
                       "Customer request", "Other"});
        d.body()->addWidget(why);

        auto *err = W::label("", "muted");
        err->setWordWrap(true);
        err->setStyleSheet("color:#ba1a1a; font-weight:700; background:#ffdad6; border-radius:8px; padding:8px 10px;");
        err->hide();
        d.body()->addWidget(err);

        // staff id check mark: only shown while the id is a known manager
        auto idKnown = [&]
        { return staff->text().trimmed().compare(UiStore::demoManagerId(), Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0; };
        staffOk->setVisible(idKnown());
        QObject::connect(staff, &QLineEdit::textChanged, &d, [&](const QString &)
                         { staffOk->setVisible(idKnown()); err->hide(); });
        QObject::connect(pin, &QLineEdit::textChanged, &d, [&](const QString &)
                         { err->hide(); });

        auto *cancel = W::button("Cancel  [Esc]");
        auto *ok = W::button("Approve && Unlock Order  [Enter]", "primary", "unlock");
        ok->setMinimumHeight(40);
        ok->setDefault(true);
        d.footer()->addWidget(W::label("Approval is stored with the order", "muted"));
        d.footer()->addStretch();
        d.footer()->addWidget(cancel);
        d.footer()->addWidget(ok);
        QObject::connect(cancel, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::reject);
        QObject::connect(ok, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&]
                         {
            QString who;
            if (!store.verifyManager(staff->text(), pin->text(), &who))
            {
                err->setText("Staff ID or PIN is not correct. Ask a manager to sign in.");
                err->show();
                pin->selectAll();
                pin->setFocus();
                return;
            }
            if (managerName)
                *managerName = who;
            if (reason)
                *reason = why->currentText().trimmed().isEmpty() ? QString("Customer request") : why->currentText().trimmed();
            d.accept(); });

        pin->setFocus();
        return d.exec() == QDialog::Accepted;
    }
}

#include "AppState.h"
#include "BarcodeArt.h"
#include "integration/ProductAdapter.h"
#include "billing/BillingService.h"
#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QFile>
#include <QSaveFile>
#include <QtMath>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <map>

#ifndef BARCODEHUB_SOURCE_DIR
#define BARCODEHUB_SOURCE_DIR ""
#endif

ProductInfo ProductInfo::from(const Product& p, const QString& rootDir)
{
    ProductInfo i;
    i.id = p.getProductID();
    i.sku = QString::fromStdString(p.getAcronym());
    i.ean = QString::fromStdString(p.getBarcode());
    i.name = QString::fromStdString(p.getProductName());
    i.unit = QString::fromStdString(p.getMeasurementUnitName());
    i.price = p.getUnitPrice();
    i.tax = p.getTax();
    i.discount = p.getDiscount();
    i.stock = p.getAvailableQuantity();
    const QString path = QString::fromStdString(p.getBarcodeFilePath());
    i.imagePath = QFileInfo(path).isAbsolute() ? path : QDir(rootDir).filePath(path);
    return i;
}

int OrderRecord::itemCount() const
{
    int n = 0;
    for (const auto& l : lines)
        n += l.qty;
    return n;
}

void OrderRecord::recompute()
{
    subtotal = discount = tax = 0;
    for (const auto& l : lines)
    {
        subtotal += l.price * l.qty;
        discount += l.discount * l.qty;
        tax += l.tax * l.qty;
    }
    total = subtotal - discount + tax;
}

QString OrderRecord::summary() const
{
    if (lines.isEmpty())
        return "-";
    QStringList names;
    for (const auto& l : lines)
        names << l.name;
    QString s = QString("%1 item%2  ").arg(itemCount()).arg(itemCount() == 1 ? "" : "s");
    s += names.mid(0, 2).join(", ");
    if (names.size() > 2)
        s += QString(", +%1").arg(names.size() - 2);
    return s;
}

AppState& AppState::instance()
{
    static AppState s;
    return s;
}

QString AppState::locateDataDir()
{
    QStringList candidates;
    const QByteArray env = qgetenv("BARCODEHUB_DATA");
    if (!env.isEmpty())
        candidates << QString::fromLocal8Bit(env);
    const QString app = QCoreApplication::applicationDirPath();
    candidates << app + "/data" << app + "/../data" << app + "/../../data" << app + "/../../../data"
        << QDir::currentPath() + "/data" << QString(BARCODEHUB_SOURCE_DIR) + "/data";
    for (const auto& c : candidates)
        if (QFileInfo::exists(c + "/Product.txt"))
            return QDir::cleanPath(c);
    return QString();
}

AppState::AppState()
{
    const QString dir = locateDataDir();
    if (!dir.isEmpty())
        loadProducts(dir + "/Product.txt");
    if (!dir.isEmpty())
    {
        const std::string root = dir.toStdString();
        m_orderManager = std::make_unique<barcodehub::order::OrderManager>(root + "/Order.txt", root + "/OrderDetail.txt");
        m_billRepository = std::make_unique<barcodehub::billing::BillRepository>(root + "/Bill.txt");
        refreshOrders();
    }
}

bool AppState::loadProducts(const QString& path)
{
    m_root = QDir::cleanPath(QFileInfo(path).absolutePath() + "/..");
    m_productPath = path;
    const bool ok = m_manager.loadProducts(path.toStdString());
    refreshCache();
    emit changed();
    return ok;
}

void AppState::refreshCache()
{
    m_products.clear();
    for (const auto& p : m_manager.getProducts())
        m_products.push_back(ProductInfo::from(p, m_root));
}

QStringList AppState::productIssues() const
{
    QStringList out;
    const auto& r = m_manager.getLastLoad();
    if (!r.fileOpened)
        out << QString::fromStdString(r.summary());
    for (const auto& i : r.issues)
        if (i.line > 0)
            out << QString("%1 line %2: %3")
            .arg(i.severity == IssueSeverity::Error ? "Error  " : "Warning")
            .arg(i.line)
            .arg(QString::fromStdString(i.message));
    return out;
}

std::optional<ProductInfo> AppState::findByCode(const QString& code) const
{
    if (const Product* p = m_manager.searchByCode(code.trimmed().toStdString()))
        return ProductInfo::from(*p, m_root);
    return std::nullopt;
}

QVector<ProductInfo> AppState::searchText(const QString& query) const
{
    QVector<ProductInfo> out;
    for (const Product* p : m_manager.searchByText(query.trimmed().toStdString()))
        out.push_back(ProductInfo::from(*p, m_root));
    return out;
}

ProductDescriptionView AppState::describe(int productId) const
{
    ProductDescriptionView v;
    const Product* p = m_manager.searchByID(productId);
    if (!p)
        return v;
    const ProductDescription d = ProductDescriber(m_settings.lowStockThreshold).describe(*p);
    v.title = QString::fromStdString(d.title);
    v.level = QString::fromStdString(d.stockLevel);
    for (const auto& f : d.fields)
        v.fields.push_back({ QString::fromStdString(f.first), QString::fromStdString(f.second) });
    v.imagePath = ProductInfo::from(*p, m_root).imagePath;
    return v;
}

QString AppState::stockProblem(int productId, int alreadyInOrder, int toAdd) const
{
    const Product* p = m_manager.searchByID(productId);
    if (!p)
        return QString(); // custom lines are not stock-controlled
    const StockCheck c = m_stock.checkAdd(*p, alreadyInOrder, toAdd);
    return c.ok() ? QString() : QString::fromStdString(c.message);
}

QVector<ProductInfo> AppState::lowStock() const
{
    QVector<ProductInfo> v;
    for (const auto& p : m_products)
        if (p.stock <= m_settings.lowStockThreshold)
            v << p;
    std::sort(v.begin(), v.end(), [](const ProductInfo& a, const ProductInfo& b) { return a.stock < b.stock; });
    return v;
}

void AppState::reorder(int id)
{
    m_reordered.insert(id);
    emit changed();
}

void AppState::reorderAllLow()
{
    for (const auto& p : lowStock())
        m_reordered.insert(p.id);
    emit changed();
}

QString AppState::nextOrderId()
{
    if (!m_orderManager)
        return "--------";
    std::string error;
    const std::string id = m_orderManager->nextOrderId(&error);
    return id.empty() ? QString("--------") : QString::fromStdString(id);
}

bool AppState::addOrder(OrderRecord& o, QString* error)
{
    auto fail = [&](const QString& why) {
        if (error) *error = why;
        return false;
        };
    if (error) error->clear();
    if (!m_orderManager || !m_billRepository)
        return fail("Data directory is unavailable. Check BARCODEHUB_DATA and Product.txt.");
    if (o.lines.isEmpty())
        return fail("The order is empty.");

    namespace bh = barcodehub;
    std::string why;
    bh::order::PaymentMode mode = bh::order::PaymentMode::Cash;
    if (!bh::order::paymentModeFromString(o.payment.toLower().toStdString(), mode))
        return fail("Unsupported payment method.");
    bh::billing::PaymentRequest payment;
    payment.mode = mode;
    payment.merchant = o.merchant.toStdString();
    payment.amountTendered = o.tendered;
    const auto methods = bh::billing::PaymentMethodRegistry::withDefaults();
    const auto* method = methods.find(mode);
    if (!method)
        return fail("Payment method unavailable.");

    m_orderManager->clearCart();
    std::map<std::string, bh::order::ProductSnapshot> manualProducts;
    std::map<std::string, double> lineDiscount; // product id -> per-unit discount incl. seasonal/special (Pricing.h)
    std::map<int, int> stockToDeduct;
    for (const auto& line : o.lines)
    {
        bh::order::ProductSnapshot snapshot;
        if (line.productId > 0)
        {
            const Product* p = m_manager.searchByID(line.productId);
            if (!p || QString::fromStdString(p->getAcronym()) != line.acronym)
                return fail("A product in the order no longer exists. Refresh and retry.");
            snapshot = bh::integration::toSnapshot(*p);
            snapshot.discountPerUnit = line.discount; // product discount + folded seasonal/special discount
            lineDiscount[snapshot.productId] = line.discount;
            stockToDeduct[line.productId] += line.qty;
        }
        else
        {
            // Manual items have no inventory entry. Their snapshot remains valid for this
            // checkout and their price/quantity is saved to Bill.txt for future receipts.
            snapshot.productId = "CUSTOM-" + line.acronym.toStdString();
            snapshot.barcode = snapshot.productId;
            snapshot.acronym = line.acronym.toStdString();
            snapshot.name = line.name.toStdString();
            snapshot.measurementUnit = "count";
            snapshot.unitPrice = line.price;
            snapshot.taxPerUnit = line.tax;
            snapshot.discountPerUnit = line.discount;
            snapshot.availableQuantity = 1000000;
            manualProducts[snapshot.productId] = snapshot;
        }
        if (!m_orderManager->addToCart(snapshot, line.qty, &why))
            return fail(QString::fromStdString(why));
    }
    for (const auto& [id, qty] : stockToDeduct)
        if (const auto* p = m_manager.searchByID(id); !p || qty > p->getAvailableQuantity())
            return fail("Stock changed while preparing checkout. Refresh and retry.");

    // Validate the same arithmetic/payment rules used by Billing before touching disk.
    o.recompute();
    const double payable = m_orderManager->totalPayable();
    if (std::fabs(o.total - payable) > 0.011)
        return fail("Cart totals differ from backend totals. Please refresh the order.");
    const auto accepted = method->process(payment, payable);
    if (!accepted.ok)
        return fail(QString::fromStdString(accepted.error));

    bh::order::Order created;
    const std::string name = o.customer.trimmed().isEmpty() ? "Walk-in Customer" : o.customer.toStdString();
    const std::string contact = o.contact == "-" ? "" : o.contact.toStdString();
    if (!m_orderManager->createOrder(name, contact, created, &why))
        return fail(QString::fromStdString(why));

    // Capture the original inventory before changing stock, so a failed checkout can
    // restore Product.txt instead of leaving stock deducted for an unpaid order.
    QFile stockFile(m_productPath);
    if (!stockFile.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly))
    {
        std::string ignored;
        m_orderManager->discardOrder(created.id, &ignored);
        return fail("Cannot back up Product.txt before checkout.");
    }
    const QByteArray originalStock = stockFile.readAll();
    stockFile.close();
    auto restoreStock = [&]() -> bool {
        QSaveFile output(m_productPath);
        if (!output.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly)) return false;
        if (output.write(originalStock) != originalStock.size()) return false;
        if (!output.commit()) return false;
        m_manager.loadProducts(m_productPath.toStdString());
        refreshCache();
        return true;
        };
    for (const auto& [id, qty] : stockToDeduct)
        m_manager.deductStock(id, qty);
    if (!m_manager.saveProducts(&why))
    {
        const bool restored = restoreStock();
        std::string ignored;
        const bool discarded = m_orderManager->discardOrder(created.id, &ignored);
        return fail("Could not save stock: " + QString::fromStdString(why) +
            (!restored || !discarded ? " (rollback needs manual attention)" : ""));
    }

    bh::billing::BillingService service(*m_orderManager, *m_billRepository,
        [this, &manualProducts, &lineDiscount](const std::string& id, bh::order::ProductSnapshot& snapshot) {
            const auto custom = manualProducts.find(id);
            if (custom != manualProducts.end()) { snapshot = custom->second; return true; }
            try {
                const Product* p = m_manager.searchByID(std::stoi(id));
                if (p) {
                    snapshot = bh::integration::toSnapshot(*p);
                    const auto d = lineDiscount.find(id);
                    if (d != lineDiscount.end()) snapshot.discountPerUnit = d->second;
                    return true;
                }
            }
            catch (...) {}
            return false;
        });
    bh::billing::Bill bill;
    double change = 0;
    if (!service.checkout(created.id, payment, bill, &change, &why))
    {
        const bool restored = restoreStock();
        std::string ignored;
        const bool discarded = m_orderManager->discardOrder(created.id, &ignored);
        refreshCache();
        return fail("Checkout failed: " + QString::fromStdString(why) +
            (!restored || !discarded ? " (rollback needs manual attention)" : ""));
    }

    o.id = QString::fromStdString(created.id);
    o.billId = QString::fromStdString(bill.id);
    o.time = QDateTime::fromString(QString::fromStdString(created.date + " " + created.time), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
    o.status = "Paid";
    o.total = bill.totals.payable;
    o.subtotal = bill.totals.withoutTax;
    o.discount = bill.totals.discount;
    o.tax = bill.totals.tax;
    o.status = "Paid";
    refreshCache();
    refreshOrders();
    // The current session knows the manual item descriptions even if the catalogue doesn't.
    for (auto& history : m_orders)
        if (history.id == o.id) { history = o; break; }
    emit changed();
    return true;
}

void AppState::refreshOrders()
{
    m_orders.clear();
    m_baseSales = 0;
    m_baseOrders = 0;
    if (!m_orderManager || !m_billRepository) return;
    std::vector<barcodehub::order::Order> orders;
    std::vector<barcodehub::order::OrderDetail> details;
    std::vector<barcodehub::billing::Bill> bills;
    std::string error;
    if (!m_orderManager->loadOrders(orders, &error) ||
        !m_orderManager->loadOrderDetails(details, &error) ||
        !m_billRepository->loadAll(bills, &error))
    {
        qWarning("Could not load persisted orders/bills: %s", error.c_str());
        return;
    }
    const QDate today = QDate::currentDate();
    for (const auto& entry : orders)
    {
        OrderRecord rec;
        rec.id = QString::fromStdString(entry.id);
        rec.time = QDateTime::fromString(QString::fromStdString(entry.date + " " + entry.time), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
        rec.customer = QString::fromStdString(entry.customerName);
        rec.contact = entry.customerContact.empty() ? "-" : QString::fromStdString(entry.customerContact);
        rec.tier = "Guest";
        rec.shift = "POS #01";
        rec.status = QString::fromStdString(barcodehub::order::toString(entry.status));
        if (entry.status == barcodehub::order::OrderStatus::InProgress) rec.status = "In Progress";
        if (entry.status == barcodehub::order::OrderStatus::OnHold) rec.status = "On Hold";
        if (entry.status == barcodehub::order::OrderStatus::Paid) rec.status = "Paid";
        if (entry.status == barcodehub::order::OrderStatus::Archived) rec.status = "Archived";
        rec.payment = QString::fromStdString(barcodehub::order::toString(entry.paymentMode));
        rec.merchant = QString::fromStdString(entry.paymentMerchant);
        const auto bill = std::find_if(bills.begin(), bills.end(), [&](const auto& b) { return b.orderId == entry.id; });
        if (bill != bills.end()) rec.billId = QString::fromStdString(bill->id);
        int detailIndex = 0;
        for (const auto& detail : details)
        {
            if (detail.orderId != entry.id) continue;
            const Product* p = nullptr;
            try { p = m_manager.searchByID(std::stoi(detail.productId)); }
            catch (...) {}
            ReceiptLine line;
            line.name = p ? QString::fromStdString(p->getProductName()) : QString::fromStdString(detail.productId);
            line.acronym = p ? QString::fromStdString(p->getAcronym()) : QString::fromStdString(detail.productId);
            line.productId = p ? p->getProductID() : 0;
            line.qty = static_cast<int>(detail.quantity);
            line.price = p ? p->getUnitPrice() : 0;
            line.tax = p ? p->getTax() : 0;
            line.discount = p ? p->getDiscount() : 0;
            if (bill != bills.end() && detailIndex < static_cast<int>(bill->lines.size()))
            {
                const auto& saved = bill->lines[detailIndex];
                line.acronym = QString::fromStdString(saved.productAcronym);
                if (!p) line.name = line.acronym;
                line.price = saved.unitPrice;
                line.discount = saved.discountPerUnit;
            }
            rec.lines.push_back(line);
            ++detailIndex;
        }
        rec.recompute();
        if (bill != bills.end())
        {
            rec.subtotal = bill->totals.withoutTax;
            rec.discount = bill->totals.discount;
            rec.tax = bill->totals.tax;
            rec.total = bill->totals.payable;
        }
        if (rec.time.date() == today)
        {
            ++m_baseOrders;
            if (rec.status == "Paid") m_baseSales += rec.total;
        }
        m_orders.push_back(std::move(rec));
    }
    std::sort(m_orders.begin(), m_orders.end(), [](const OrderRecord& a, const OrderRecord& b) { return a.time > b.time; });
}

// ======================================================================== data directory / reload
QString AppState::dataDir() const
{
    return m_productPath.isEmpty() ? QString() : QFileInfo(m_productPath).absolutePath();
}

void AppState::reloadAll()
{
    if (!m_productPath.isEmpty())
        m_manager.loadProducts(m_productPath.toStdString());
    refreshCache();
    refreshOrders();
    emit changed();
}

// ======================================================================== catalogue (manager)
namespace
{
    Product toProduct(const ProductInfo& p, const QString& barcodeFile)
    {
        MeasurementUnit unit = MeasurementUnit::Count;
        parseMeasurementUnit(p.unit.toStdString(), unit);
        return Product(p.id, p.ean.toStdString(), p.sku.toStdString(), p.name.toStdString(), unit, p.price, p.tax,
                       p.discount, p.stock, barcodeFile.toStdString());
    }
}

int AppState::nextProductId() const
{
    int maxId = 0;
    for (const auto& p : m_products)
        maxId = qMax(maxId, p.id);
    return maxId + 1;
}

int AppState::nextSerial(const QString& categoryId) const
{
    int maxSerial = 0;
    for (const auto& p : m_products)
        if (p.ean.size() == 8 && p.ean.left(2) == categoryId)
            maxSerial = qMax(maxSerial, p.ean.mid(2, 5).toInt());
    return maxSerial + 1;
}

QString AppState::nextBarcode(const QString& categoryId) const
{
    if (categoryId.size() != 2)
        return QString();
    int serial = nextSerial(categoryId);
    while (serial <= 99999)
    {
        const QString code = BarcodeArt::makeCode(categoryId, serial);
        bool taken = false;
        for (const auto& p : m_products)
            if (p.ean == code) { taken = true; break; }
        if (!taken)
            return code;
        ++serial;
    }
    return QString();
}

bool AppState::backendAcceptsPrefix(const QString& categoryId) const
{
    const QString code = BarcodeArt::makeCode(categoryId, 1);
    if (code.isEmpty())
        return false;
    const Product probe(1, code.toStdString(), "PROBE", "Probe", MeasurementUnit::Count, 1, 0, 0, 0, "data/barcodes/probe.png");
    return ProductValidator::standard().validate(probe).ok();
}

QString AppState::validateProduct(const ProductInfo& p, bool isNew) const
{
    if (p.name.trimmed().isEmpty())
        return "Product name is required.";
    if (p.sku.trimmed().isEmpty())
        return "Acronym is required.";
    if (p.ean.isEmpty())
        return "Generate a barcode first.";
    const Product candidate = toProduct(p, "data/barcodes/" + p.ean + ".png");
    const ValidationReport report = ProductValidator::standard().validate(candidate);
    if (!report.ok())
        return QString::fromStdString(report.errors.front());
    for (const auto& o : m_products)
    {
        if (!isNew && o.id == p.id)
            continue;
        if (o.ean == p.ean)
            return QString("Barcode %1 already belongs to \"%2\".").arg(p.ean, o.name);
        if (o.sku.compare(p.sku, Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0)
            return QString("Acronym %1 is already used by \"%2\".").arg(p.sku.toUpper(), o.name);
    }
    return QString();
}

namespace
{
    // Saves `products` to Product.txt; on failure puts the previous file content back.
    bool saveCatalogue(const std::string& path, const std::vector<Product>& products, QString* error)
    {
        QFile f(QString::fromStdString(path));
        QByteArray backup;
        if (f.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly)) { backup = f.readAll(); f.close(); }
        std::string why;
        if (ProductFileWriter().save(path, products, &why))
            return true;
        if (!backup.isEmpty())
        {
            QSaveFile restore(QString::fromStdString(path));
            if (restore.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly)) { restore.write(backup); restore.commit(); }
        }
        if (error) *error = "Could not save Product.txt: " + QString::fromStdString(why);
        return false;
    }
}

bool AppState::addProduct(ProductInfo& p, QString* error)
{
    if (m_productPath.isEmpty())
    {
        if (error) *error = "Product.txt was not found.";
        return false;
    }
    if (p.id <= 0)
        p.id = nextProductId();
    p.sku = p.sku.trimmed().toUpper();
    p.name = p.name.trimmed();
    const QString problem = validateProduct(p, true);
    if (!problem.isEmpty())
    {
        if (error) *error = problem;
        return false;
    }
    const QString rel = "data/barcodes/" + p.ean + ".png";
    QString imgError;
    if (!BarcodeArt::savePng(p.ean, QDir(m_root).filePath(rel), &imgError))
    {
        if (error) *error = imgError;
        return false;
    }
    std::vector<Product> all = m_manager.getProducts();
    all.push_back(toProduct(p, rel));
    if (!saveCatalogue(m_productPath.toStdString(), all, error))
        return false;
    m_manager.loadProducts(m_productPath.toStdString());
    refreshCache();
    refreshOrders();
    emit changed();
    return true;
}

bool AppState::updateProduct(const ProductInfo& p, QString* error)
{
    ProductInfo q = p;
    q.sku = q.sku.trimmed().toUpper();
    q.name = q.name.trimmed();
    const QString problem = validateProduct(q, false);
    if (!problem.isEmpty())
    {
        if (error) *error = problem;
        return false;
    }
    std::vector<Product> all = m_manager.getProducts();
    bool found = false;
    for (auto& e : all)
        if (e.getProductID() == q.id)
        {
            e = toProduct(q, QString::fromStdString(e.getBarcodeFilePath()));
            found = true;
        }
    if (!found)
    {
        if (error) *error = "That product no longer exists.";
        return false;
    }
    if (!saveCatalogue(m_productPath.toStdString(), all, error))
        return false;
    m_manager.loadProducts(m_productPath.toStdString());
    refreshCache();
    refreshOrders();
    emit changed();
    return true;
}

bool AppState::setStockLevels(const QMap<int, int>& levels, QString* error)
{
    std::vector<Product> all = m_manager.getProducts();
    for (auto& e : all)
    {
        const auto it = levels.constFind(e.getProductID());
        if (it == levels.constEnd())
            continue;
        if (it.value() < 0)
        {
            if (error) *error = "Quantity cannot be negative.";
            return false;
        }
        ProductInfo info = ProductInfo::from(e, m_root);
        info.stock = it.value();
        e = toProduct(info, QString::fromStdString(e.getBarcodeFilePath()));
    }
    if (!saveCatalogue(m_productPath.toStdString(), all, error))
        return false;
    m_manager.loadProducts(m_productPath.toStdString());
    refreshCache();
    emit changed();
    return true;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/src/ui/BarcodeArt.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "BarcodeArt.h"
#include <QDir>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QFont>
#include <QPainter>

namespace
{
    // EAN-8: left half uses the "L" codes, right half the "R" codes (R = bitwise complement of L).
    const char *kL[10] = {"0001101", "0011001", "0010011", "0111101", "0100011",
                          "0110001", "0101111", "0111011", "0110111", "0001011"};
    const char *kR[10] = {"1110010", "1100110", "1101100", "1000010", "1011100",
                          "1001110", "1010000", "1000100", "1001000", "1110100"};
    bool eightDigits(const QString &s)
    {
        if (s.size() != 8)
            return false;
        for (QChar c : s)
            if (!c.isDigit())
                return false;
        return true;
    }
}

namespace BarcodeArt
{
    QString checkDigit(const QString &seven)
    {
        if (seven.size() != 7)
            return QString();
        int sum = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 7; ++i)
            sum += seven[i].digitValue() * (i % 2 == 0 ? 3 : 1);
        return QString::number((10 - sum % 10) % 10);
    }

    QString makeCode(const QString &categoryId, int serial)
    {
        if (categoryId.size() != 2 || serial < 0 || serial > 99999)
            return QString();
        const QString seven = categoryId + QString::number(serial).rightJustified(5, '0');
        return seven + checkDigit(seven);
    }

    QString modules(const QString &code)
    {
        if (!eightDigits(code))
            return QString();
        QString m = "101";
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
            m += kL[code[i].digitValue()];
        m += "01010";
        for (int i = 4; i < 8; ++i)
            m += kR[code[i].digitValue()];
        m += "101";
        return m;
    }

    QImage render(const QString &code, int moduleWidth, int barHeight, bool withDigits)
    {
        const QString m = modules(code);
        if (m.isEmpty())
            return QImage();
        const int quiet = moduleWidth * 7;
        const int digitsH = withDigits ? moduleWidth * 8 : 0;
        const int w = quiet * 2 + m.size() * moduleWidth;
        const int h = quiet / 2 + barHeight + digitsH + quiet / 2;
        QImage img(w, h, QImage::Format_RGB32);
        img.fill(Qt::white);
        QPainter p(&img);
        p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
        p.setBrush(Qt::black);
        for (int i = 0; i < m.size(); ++i)
            if (m[i] == '1')
                p.drawRect(quiet + i * moduleWidth, quiet / 2, moduleWidth, barHeight);
        if (withDigits)
        {
            QFont f("Courier New");
            f.setStyleHint(QFont::Monospace);
            f.setPixelSize(moduleWidth * 6);
            p.setFont(f);
            p.setPen(Qt::black);
            p.drawText(QRect(0, quiet / 2 + barHeight, w, digitsH + quiet / 3), Qt::AlignHCenter | Qt::AlignVCenter, code);
        }
        return img;
    }

    bool savePng(const QString &code, const QString &path, QString *error)
    {
        const QImage img = render(code);
        if (img.isNull())
        {
            if (error) *error = "Barcode must be exactly 8 digits.";
            return false;
        }
        QDir().mkpath(QFileInfo(path).absolutePath());
        if (!img.save(path, "PNG"))
        {
            if (error) *error = "Could not write " + path;
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }
}

#include "BillsPage.h"

// =================================================================== BillsPage
BillsPage::BillsPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root;
    pageShell(this, root);
    auto* card = W::card(this);
    auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(card);
    v->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 10);
    v->setSpacing(10);
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout;
    h->addWidget(W::iconLabel("bills", Theme::primary, 20));
    h->addWidget(W::label("Bills", "h1"));
    h->addStretch();
    m_search = new QLineEdit;
    m_search->setPlaceholderText("Search bill or order ID...");
    m_search->setMinimumWidth(280);
    m_search->addAction(Theme::icon("search", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
    h->addWidget(m_search);
    v->addLayout(h);
    m_table = makeTable({ "BILL NO.", "ORDER", "DATE & TIME", "CUSTOMER", "PAYMENT", "TOTAL", "ACTIONS" });
    m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(6, QHeaderView::Fixed);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(6, 160);
    v->addWidget(m_table, 1);
    root->addWidget(card, 1);
    connect(m_search, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this]
        { rebuild(); });
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &BillsPage::rebuild);
    rebuild();
}

void BillsPage::rebuild()
{
    const QString q = m_search->text().trimmed();
    QVector<OrderRecord> rows;
    for (const auto& o : AppState::instance().orders())
        if (o.status == "Paid" && !o.billId.isEmpty() &&
            (q.isEmpty() || o.id.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive) || o.billId.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive) || o.customer.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive)))
            rows << o;
    m_table->setRowCount(rows.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < rows.size(); ++r)
    {
        const auto& o = rows[r];
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 46);
        m_table->setItem(r, 0, item(o.billId, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "#004ac6", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, item(o.id));
        m_table->setItem(r, 2, item(o.time.toString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm")));
        m_table->setItem(r, 3, item(o.customer));
        m_table->setItem(r, 4, item(o.payment));
        m_table->setItem(r, 5, item(W::money(o.total), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter, "", true));
        auto* w = new QWidget;
        auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(w);
        h->setContentsMargins(4, 0, 4, 0);
        auto* view = W::button("View Bill", "mini");
        h->addWidget(view);
        h->addStretch();
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 6, w);
        const OrderRecord copy = o;
        connect(view, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, copy]
            { Dialogs::receipt(this, copy); });
    }
}

void BillsPage::showOrder(const QString& orderId) { m_search->setText(orderId); }

#include "CameraWidget.h"
#include <QLabel>
#include <QPixmap>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <opencv2/imgproc.hpp>
CameraWidget::CameraWidget(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent), view(new QLabel(this))
{
    auto *Layout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
    Layout->addWidget(view);
    view->setMinimumSize(640, 480);
    view->setScaledContents(true);
    connect(&timer, &QTimer::timeout, this, &CameraWidget::onTick);
}
void CameraWidget::start()
{
    if (scanner.openCamera())
        timer.start(30);
    else
        emit scanError(QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage()));
}
void CameraWidget::stop()
{
    timer.stop();
}
QImage CameraWidget::toQImage(const cv::Mat &BGR)
{
    cv::Mat RGB;
    cv::cvtColor(BGR, RGB, cv::COLOR_BGR2RGB);
    QImage img(RGB.data, RGB.cols, RGB.rows, (int)RGB.step, QImage::Format_RGB888);
    return img.copy();
}
void CameraWidget::onTick()
{
    cv::Mat frame;

    if (!scanner.readFrame(frame))
    {
        timer.stop();
        emit scanError(QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage()));
        return;
    }
    std::string six;
    cv::Rect Box;
    bool found = scanner.frameRead(six, Box, frame);
    if (Box.area() > 0)
        cv::rectangle(frame, Box, cv::Scalar(0, 255, 0), 2);
    view->setPixmap(QPixmap::fromImage(toQImage(frame)));
    if (found)
    {
        emit barcodeScan(QString::fromStdString(six));
    }
}

    auto *form = W::card(this);
    auto *fv = new QVBoxLayout(form);
    fv->setContentsMargins(18, 16, 18, 16);
    fv->setSpacing(8);
    fv->addWidget(W::label("NEW DEPARTMENT ENTRY", "eyebrow"));
    m_formTitle = W::label("Create New Category", "h1");
    fv->addWidget(m_formTitle);
    fv->addWidget(W::label("Assign a 2-digit numeric prefix for automatic barcode binding.", "muted"));

    fv->addWidget(W::label("CATEGORY ID (2 DIGITS)", "caption"));
    m_id = new QLineEdit;
    m_id->setMaxLength(2);
    m_id->setValidator(new QRegularExpressionValidator(QRegularExpression("\\d{0,2}"), m_id));
    m_id->setMinimumHeight(42);
    m_id->setPlaceholderText("e.g. 80");
    fv->addWidget(m_id);

    m_errorBox = W::card(form, "notice");
    m_errorBox->setStyleSheet("#notice{background:#ffefed; border:1px solid #ffc3bd; border-radius:12px;}");
    auto *eh = new QHBoxLayout(m_errorBox);
    eh->setContentsMargins(12, 8, 12, 8);
    eh->addWidget(W::iconLabel("warn", Theme::error, 18), 0, Qt::AlignTop);
    m_error = W::label("");
    m_error->setWordWrap(true);
    m_error->setStyleSheet("color:#93000a; font-weight:600;");
    eh->addWidget(m_error, 1);
    fv->addWidget(m_errorBox);
    m_suggest = W::label("", "muted");
    fv->addWidget(m_suggest);

    fv->addWidget(W::label("CATEGORY NAME", "caption"));
    m_name = new QLineEdit;
    m_name->setMinimumHeight(42);
    m_name->setPlaceholderText("e.g. Frozen Foods");
    fv->addWidget(m_name);
    fv->addWidget(W::label("DEPARTMENT ACRONYM  (MAX 3 CHARACTERS)", "caption"));
    m_acr = new QLineEdit;
    m_acr->setMaxLength(3);
    m_acr->setMinimumHeight(42);
    m_acr->setPlaceholderText("e.g. FRZ");
    fv->addWidget(m_acr);
    m_range = W::label("Calculated range: -", "mono");
    fv->addWidget(m_range);
    fv->addStretch();

    auto *btns = new QHBoxLayout;
    auto *reset = W::button("Reset Form", "soft");
    m_submit = W::button("Add Category", "primary", "plus");
    m_submit->setMinimumHeight(44);
    btns->addWidget(reset);
    btns->addWidget(m_submit, 1);
    fv->addLayout(btns);
    row->addWidget(form, 38);
    root->addLayout(row, 1);

    connect(m_filter, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this] { rebuild(); });
    for (auto *e : {m_id, m_name, m_acr})
        connect(e, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this] { validate(); });
    connect(reset, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &CategoriesPage::resetForm);
    connect(m_submit, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &CategoriesPage::submit);
    connect(&UiStore::instance(), &UiStore::categoriesChanged, this, &CategoriesPage::refresh);
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &CategoriesPage::refresh);
    resetForm();
    refresh();
}

void CategoriesPage::refresh() { rebuild(); validate(); }

void CategoriesPage::rebuild()
{
    auto &store = UiStore::instance();
    const QString q = m_filter->text().trimmed();
    QVector<Category> rows;
    int skus = 0;
    for (const auto &c : store.categories())
    {
        skus += store.productCount(c.id);
        if (!q.isEmpty() && !c.id.contains(q) && !c.name.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive) &&
            !c.acronym.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive))
            continue;
        rows << c;
    }
    m_summary->setText(QString("%1 categories  -  %2 products").arg(store.categories().size()).arg(skus));
    m_table->setRowCount(0);
    m_table->setRowCount(rows.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < rows.size(); ++r)
    {
        const Category c = rows[r];
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 52);
        m_table->setItem(r, 0, item(c.id, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "#004ac6", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, item(c.name, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "", true));
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 2, wrapW(W::badge(c.acronym, "blue")));
        m_table->setItem(r, 3, item(QString::number(store.productCount(c.id)) + " products"));
        m_table->setItem(r, 4, item(store.rangeText(c.id)));
        auto *cell = new QWidget;
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(cell);
        h->setContentsMargins(4, 0, 4, 0);
        h->setSpacing(4);
        auto *e = W::button("", "flat", "edit");
        auto *x = W::button("", "flat", "trash");
        e->setToolTip("Edit");
        x->setToolTip("Delete");
        h->addWidget(e);
        h->addWidget(x);
        h->addStretch();
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 5, cell);
        const QString id = c.id;
        connect(e, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, id] { edit(id); });
        connect(x, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, id]
                {
            QString err;
            if (QMessageBox::question(this, "Delete category", "Delete category " + id + "?") != QMessageBox::Yes)
                return;
            if (!UiStore::instance().removeCategory(id, &err)) { emit toast(err); return; }
            emit toast("Category " + id + " deleted"); });
    }
}

void CategoriesPage::validate()
{
    const QString id = m_id->text().trimmed();
    m_range->setText("Calculated range: " + (id.size() == 2 ? UiStore::instance().rangeText(id) : QString("-")));
    const bool pristine = id.isEmpty() && m_name->text().isEmpty() && m_acr->text().isEmpty();
    QString why;
    if (!pristine)
        why = UiStore::instance().validateCategory(id, m_name->text(), m_acr->text(), m_editing);
    // do not nag about fields the user has not reached yet
    if (id.size() == 2 && m_name->text().trimmed().isEmpty() && m_acr->text().trimmed().isEmpty() && why.startsWith("Category name"))
        why.clear();
    if (why.startsWith("Acronym must") && m_acr->text().isEmpty())
        why.clear();
    m_errorBox->setVisible(!why.isEmpty());
    m_error->setText(why);
    const bool idTaken = why.contains("already in use");
    m_suggest->setVisible(idTaken);
    m_suggest->setText("Suggested free ids:  " + UiStore::instance().freeIds(3).join("   "));
    m_submit->setEnabled(!pristine && UiStore::instance().validateCategory(id, m_name->text(), m_acr->text(), m_editing).isEmpty());
}

void CategoriesPage::resetForm()
{
    m_editing.clear();
    m_id->setEnabled(true);
    m_id->clear();
    m_name->clear();
    m_acr->clear();
    m_formTitle->setText("Create New Category");
    m_submit->setText("Add Category");
    validate();
}

void CategoriesPage::edit(const QString &id)
{
    const Category *c = UiStore::instance().category(id);
    if (!c)
        return;
    m_editing = id;
    m_id->setText(c->id);
    m_name->setText(c->name);
    m_acr->setText(c->acronym);
    m_formTitle->setText("Edit Category " + id);
    m_submit->setText("Save Category");
    validate();
}

void CategoriesPage::submit()
{
    Category c;
    c.id = m_id->text().trimmed();
    c.name = m_name->text().trimmed();
    c.acronym = m_acr->text().trimmed().toUpper();
    c.color = "#2554d8";
    QString err;
    const bool editing = !m_editing.isEmpty();
    const bool ok = editing ? UiStore::instance().updateCategory(m_editing, c, &err)
                            : UiStore::instance().addCategory(c, &err);
    if (!ok)
    {
        emit toast(err);
        return;
    }
    emit toast(QString("Category %1 %2").arg(c.id, editing ? "updated" : "added to PCategory.txt"));
    resetForm();
}
