FILE: /Frontend/src/ui/PaymentDialog.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// "Complete Order Payment" (redesigned): billing breakdown with item / seasonal / special discount and tax on the
// left; tender mode, cash numpad shortcuts and balance to return on the right.
// Frontend only: the numbers come from Pricing::quote (the same function the order is saved with), so what the
// cashier sees here is exactly what lands in Order.txt / Bill.txt.
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "Pricing.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiKit.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QButtonGroup>
#include <QComboBox>
#include <QDoubleSpinBox>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QStackedWidget>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <QtMath>

namespace Dialogs
{
    bool payment(QWidget *parent, const PaymentRequest &req, PaymentResult &res)
    {
        const QString who = req.customer.trimmed().isEmpty() ? QString("Walk-in Customer") : req.customer.trimmed();
        QString sub = QString("Order #%1  •  %2").arg(req.orderId, who);
        const QString phone = req.contact.trimmed();
        if (!phone.isEmpty() && phone != "-")
            sub += QString("  (%1)").arg(phone);
        Ui::Modal d(parent, "cash", "Complete Order Payment", sub, "POS DESK", 880);

        Pricing::Quote q = Pricing::quote(req.lines, 0);

        auto *cols = new QHBoxLayout;
        cols->setSpacing(14);

        // ============================================================ left: billing breakdown
        auto *bill = W::card(nullptr, "subCard");
        auto *bv = new QVBoxLayout(bill);
        bv->setContentsMargins(16, 14, 16, 14);
        bv->setSpacing(8);
        auto *itemsBadge = W::badge("", "blue");
        W::headerRow(bv, "bills", "Billing Breakdown", itemsBadge);

        QLabel *gross = nullptr, *itemDisc = nullptr, *seasonal = nullptr, *special = nullptr, *tax = nullptr;
        QLabel *seasonalName = nullptr;
        auto row = [&](const QString &k, QLabel *&val, QLabel **keyOut = nullptr)
        {
            auto *r = new QHBoxLayout;
            auto *kl = W::label(k, "muted");
            kl->setWordWrap(true);
            r->addWidget(kl, 1);
            val = W::label("PKR 0.00");
            val->setStyleSheet("font-weight:800; font-size:14px;");
            r->addWidget(val);
            bv->addLayout(r);
            if (keyOut)
                *keyOut = kl;
        };
        row("Gross Subtotal", gross);
        row("Item Discount", itemDisc);
        row("Seasonal Discount", seasonal, &seasonalName);

        // special discount: typed percent, at most 8 percent (requirement 8)
        auto *spBox = W::card(nullptr, "field");
        auto *sh = new QHBoxLayout(spBox);
        sh->setContentsMargins(10, 8, 10, 8);
        sh->setSpacing(8);
        sh->addWidget(W::label("Special Discount", "muted"));
        auto *pct = new QDoubleSpinBox;
        pct->setRange(0, UiStore::kSpecialDiscountCapPercent);
        pct->setDecimals(1);
        pct->setSingleStep(0.5);
        pct->setSuffix(" %");
        pct->setToolTip("Special discount, at most 8 percent");
        pct->setMinimumWidth(84);
        sh->addWidget(pct);
        sh->addWidget(W::badge(QString("CAPPED AT %1% MAX").arg(UiStore::kSpecialDiscountCapPercent, 0, 'f', 0), "grey"));
        sh->addStretch();
        special = W::label("PKR 0.00");
        special->setStyleSheet("font-weight:800; font-size:14px;");
        sh->addWidget(special);
        bv->addWidget(spBox);

        row("Sales Tax", tax);
        bv->addStretch();

        auto *totalBox = W::card(nullptr, "totalBox");
        auto *tv = new QVBoxLayout(totalBox);
        tv->setContentsMargins(16, 12, 16, 14);
        tv->setSpacing(2);
        tv->addWidget(W::label("NET PAYABLE AMOUNT", "caption"));
        auto *payable = W::label("PKR 0.00");
        payable->setStyleSheet("font-size:32px; font-weight:800; color:white;");
        tv->addWidget(payable);
        bv->addWidget(totalBox);
        cols->addWidget(bill, 5);

        // ============================================================ right: tender
        auto *right = new QVBoxLayout;
        right->setSpacing(8);
        right->addWidget(W::label("SELECT TENDER MODE", "caption"));
        auto *modeRow = new QHBoxLayout;
        modeRow->setSpacing(8);
        auto *modes = new QButtonGroup(&d);
        const QStringList modeNames = {"Cash", "Card", "Wallet"};
        const QStringList modeSub = {"CASH IN HAND", "SWIPE / TAP", "QR CODE"};
        const QStringList modeIcons = {"cash", "card", "wallet"};
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
        {
            auto *b = W::button(modeNames[i] + "\n" + modeSub[i], "tab", modeIcons[i]);
            b->setCheckable(true);
            b->setMinimumHeight(58);
            modes->addButton(b, i);
            modeRow->addWidget(b, 1);
        }
        modes->button(0)->setChecked(true);
        right->addLayout(modeRow);

        // page 0: cash, page 1: card / wallet gateway
        auto *pages = new QStackedWidget;
        auto *cashPage = new QWidget;
        auto *cv = new QVBoxLayout(cashPage);
        cv->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        cv->setSpacing(8);
        cv->addWidget(W::label("TENDER AMOUNT RECEIVED", "caption"));
        auto *tender = new QDoubleSpinBox;
        tender->setPrefix("PKR  ");
        tender->setDecimals(2);
        tender->setRange(0, 100000000);
        tender->setGroupSeparatorShown(true);
        tender->setAlignment(Qt::AlignRight);
        tender->setMinimumHeight(54);
        tender->setStyleSheet("font-size:24px; font-weight:800;");
        tender->setButtonSymbols(QAbstractSpinBox::NoButtons);
        cv->addWidget(tender);
        cv->addWidget(W::label("FAST CASH TENDER SHORTCUTS", "caption"));
        auto *quick = new QHBoxLayout;
        quick->setSpacing(8);
        QPushButton *exact = nullptr;
        bool replaceOnFirstKey = true; // the pre-filled exact amount is replaced by the first note the cashier adds
        bool updating = false;
        for (int add : {500, 1000, 5000})
        {
            auto *b = W::button(QString("+%L1").arg(add), "soft");
            b->setMinimumHeight(40);
            b->setToolTip("Adds to the amount received (the first key replaces the pre-filled exact amount)");
            QObject::connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&, add]
                             {
                const double base = replaceOnFirstKey ? 0.0 : tender->value();
                replaceOnFirstKey = false;
                tender->setValue(base + add); });
            quick->addWidget(b, 1);
        }
        exact = W::button("Exact", "primary");
        exact->setMinimumHeight(40);
        quick->addWidget(exact, 1);
        cv->addLayout(quick);
        pages->addWidget(cashPage);

        auto *gatePage = new QWidget;
        auto *gv = new QVBoxLayout(gatePage);
        gv->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        gv->setSpacing(8);
        auto *gateCap = W::label("CARD / DIGITAL GATEWAY ROUTE", "caption");
        gv->addWidget(gateCap);
        auto *gate = new QComboBox;
        gate->setEditable(true);
        gate->setMinimumHeight(40);
        gv->addWidget(gate);
        auto *gateHint = W::label("Pick the bank or wallet, or type another name.", "muted");
        gv->addWidget(gateHint);
        gv->addStretch();
        pages->addWidget(gatePage);
        right->addWidget(pages);

        // balance to return (green) / short by (red)
        auto *balBox = new QFrame;
        auto *bh = new QHBoxLayout(balBox);
        bh->setContentsMargins(16, 10, 16, 10);
        auto *balCap = W::label("BALANCE CHANGE RETURN", "caption");
        auto *balVal = W::label("PKR 0.00");
        auto *balCol = new QVBoxLayout;
        balCol->setSpacing(0);
        balCol->addWidget(balCap);
        balCol->addWidget(balVal);
        bh->addLayout(balCol, 1);
        right->addStretch();
        right->addWidget(balBox);
        cols->addLayout(right, 6);
        d.body()->addLayout(cols);

        // ============================================================ footer
        auto *cancel = W::button("Cancel  [Esc]", "", "close");
        auto *ok = W::button("Confirm Payment && Print  [Enter]", "primary", "check");
        ok->setMinimumHeight(42);
        ok->setDefault(true);
        d.footer()->addWidget(W::label("Bill is saved as PDF from the receipt screen", "muted"));
        d.footer()->addStretch();
        d.footer()->addWidget(cancel);
        d.footer()->addWidget(ok);
        QObject::connect(cancel, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::reject);

        // ============================================================ behaviour
        auto fillGateways = [&]
        {
            gate->clear();
            if (modes->checkedId() == 2)
            {
                gate->addItems({"JazzCash", "Easypaisa", "SadaPay", "NayaPay"});
                gateCap->setText("WALLET");
            }
            else
            {
                gate->addItems({"HBL - POS Terminal #04", "Meezan Bank - POS", "UBL - POS Terminal", "Allied Bank - POS"});
                gateCap->setText("CARD / DIGITAL GATEWAY ROUTE");
            }
            gate->setCurrentIndex(0);
        };

        auto refresh = [&]
        {
            q = Pricing::quote(req.lines, pct->value());
            W::setBadge(itemsBadge, QString("%1 ITEM%2").arg(q.units).arg(q.units == 1 ? "" : "S"), "blue");
            gross->setText(W::money(q.gross));
            itemDisc->setText(W::money(-q.itemDiscount));
            seasonalName->setText(q.seasonName.isEmpty()
                                      ? QString("Seasonal Discount (none active)")
                                      : QString("Seasonal (%1% %2)").arg(q.seasonalPercent, 0, 'g', 4).arg(q.seasonName));
            seasonal->setText(W::money(-q.seasonal));
            special->setText(W::money(-q.special));
            tax->setText(W::money(q.tax));
            payable->setText(W::money(q.payable));
            exact->setText(QString("Exact (%1)").arg(W::money(q.payable)));

            const bool cash = modes->checkedId() == 0;
            pages->setCurrentIndex(cash ? 0 : 1);
            bool enough = true;
            if (cash)
            {
                const double diff = tender->value() - q.payable;
                enough = diff >= -0.005;
                balCap->setText(enough ? "BALANCE CHANGE RETURN" : "SHORT BY");
                balVal->setText(W::money(enough ? qMax(0.0, diff) : -diff));
            }
            else
            {
                balCap->setText(modes->checkedId() == 1 ? "CHARGE TO CARD" : "CHARGE TO WALLET");
                balVal->setText(W::money(q.payable));
            }
            const QString bg = enough ? "#0b7a4a" : "#ba1a1a";
            balBox->setStyleSheet(QString("QFrame{background:%1; border-radius:12px;} QLabel{background:transparent; color:white;}").arg(bg));
            balVal->setStyleSheet("font-size:26px; font-weight:800; color:white; background:transparent;");
            balCap->setStyleSheet("font-size:10.5px; font-weight:800; letter-spacing:1px; color:#d7f8e2; background:transparent;");
            ok->setEnabled(cash ? enough : !gate->currentText().trimmed().isEmpty());
        };

        QObject::connect(pct, qOverload<double>(&QDoubleSpinBox::valueChanged), &d, [&](double)
                         {
            // while the amount is still the pre-filled exact amount it follows the new payable
            const double before = q.payable;
            const Pricing::Quote nq = Pricing::quote(req.lines, pct->value());
            if (replaceOnFirstKey && qAbs(tender->value() - before) < 0.005)
            {
                updating = true;
                tender->setValue(nq.payable);
                updating = false;
            }
            refresh(); });
        QObject::connect(tender, qOverload<double>(&QDoubleSpinBox::valueChanged), &d, [&](double)
                         {
            if (!updating)
                replaceOnFirstKey = false;
            refresh(); });
        QObject::connect(exact, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&]
                         {
            replaceOnFirstKey = true;
            updating = true;
            tender->setValue(q.payable);
            updating = false;
            refresh(); });
        QObject::connect(modes, &QButtonGroup::idClicked, &d, [&](int)
                         {
            fillGateways();
            refresh(); });
        QObject::connect(gate, &QComboBox::currentTextChanged, &d, [&](const QString &) { refresh(); });
        QObject::connect(ok, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::accept);

        fillGateways();
        updating = true;
        tender->setValue(q.payable); // exact amount pre-filled: pressing Enter completes a cash sale at once
        updating = false;
        refresh();
        tender->setFocus();
        tender->selectAll();
        if (d.exec() != QDialog::Accepted)
            return false;

        const int id = modes->checkedId();
        res.mode = modeNames[id];
        res.merchant = id == 0 ? QString() : gate->currentText().trimmed();
        res.tendered = id == 0 ? tender->value() : 0;
        res.specialPercent = Pricing::clampSpecial(pct->value());
        return true;
    }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/src/ui/Pricing.cpp

#include "Pricing.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include <QtMath>

namespace
{
    double round2(double v) { return qRound64(v * 100.0) / 100.0; }

    // Category id of the product behind a bill line (first two digits of its barcode); empty for manual lines.
    QString categoryOfLine(const ReceiptLine &l)
    {
        if (l.productId <= 0)
            return QString();
        for (const auto &p : AppState::instance().products())
            if (p.id == l.productId)
                return p.ean.left(2);
        return QString();
    }

    bool seasonCovers(const Season &s, const ReceiptLine &l)
    {
        if (s.categories.isEmpty())
            return true;
        return s.categories.contains(categoryOfLine(l));
    }
}

namespace Pricing
{
    double clampSpecial(double percent)
    {
        if (percent < 0)
            return 0;
        return qMin(percent, UiStore::kSpecialDiscountCapPercent);
    }

    Quote quote(const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines, double specialPercent, const QDate &on)
    {
        Quote q;
        q.specialPercent = clampSpecial(specialPercent);
        const Season *season = UiStore::instance().activeSeason(on);
        if (season)
        {
            q.seasonalPercent = season->percent;
            q.seasonName = season->name;
        }
        for (const auto &l : lines)
        {
            const double base = l.price - l.discount; // per unit, after the product's own discount
            double sUnit = 0;
            if (season && seasonCovers(*season, l))
                sUnit = round2(base * season->percent / 100.0);
            const double xUnit = round2((base - sUnit) * q.specialPercent / 100.0);
            q.gross += l.price * l.qty;
            q.itemDiscount += l.discount * l.qty;
            q.seasonal += sUnit * l.qty;
            q.special += xUnit * l.qty;
            q.tax += l.tax * l.qty;
            q.units += l.qty;
            ++q.lines;
        }
        q.payable = q.gross - q.itemDiscount - q.seasonal - q.special + q.tax;
        return q;
    }

    QVector<ReceiptLine> fold(const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines, double specialPercent, const QDate &on)
    {
        const double sp = clampSpecial(specialPercent);
        const Season *season = UiStore::instance().activeSeason(on);
        QVector<ReceiptLine> out = lines;
        for (auto &l : out)
        {
            const double base = l.price - l.discount;
            double sUnit = 0;
            if (season && seasonCovers(*season, l))
                sUnit = round2(base * season->percent / 100.0);
            const double xUnit = round2((base - sUnit) * sp / 100.0);
            l.discount = round2(l.discount + sUnit + xUnit);
        }
        return out;
    }

    void apply(OrderRecord &o, const Quote &q)
    {
        o.subtotal = q.gross;
        o.discount = q.totalDiscount();
        o.tax = q.tax;
        o.total = q.payable;
        o.seasonal = q.seasonal;
        o.special = q.special;
        o.seasonalPercent = q.seasonalPercent;
        o.specialPercent = q.specialPercent;
        o.seasonName = q.seasonName;
    }
}

FILE: /Frontend/src/ui/ProductEditorDialog.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// "Add New Product to Catalog" / "Edit Product" (manager).
// Layout follows the Stitch design: 1 Category selection, 2 Product specifications, EAN-8 barcode engine on the right.
// Frontend only: everything is saved through the existing AppState::addProduct / updateProduct (Increment 2 backend).
#include "BarcodeArt.h"
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiKit.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QButtonGroup>
#include <QComboBox>
#include <QDir>
#include <QDoubleSpinBox>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QGridLayout>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QMarginsF>
#include <QMessageBox>
#include <QPageLayout>
#include <QPageSize>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPdfWriter>
#include <QPixmap>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QRegularExpression>
#include <QSpinBox>
#include <QStandardItemModel>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace
{
    // Round numbered badge "1" / "2" in front of a section title.
    QLabel *stepDot(int n)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(QString::number(n));
        l->setFixedSize(22, 22);
        l->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        l->setStyleSheet("background:#2554d8; color:white; border-radius:11px; font-weight:800; font-size:12px;");
        return l;
    }

    // Saves a one-page A4 sheet (3 x 8 labels) with this barcode; returns false when the user cancels or it fails.
    bool saveLabelSheet(QWidget *parent, const QString &code, const QString &name)
    {
        const QString suggested = QDir(AppState::instance().dataDir()).filePath("labels-" + code + ".pdf");
        const QString path = QFileDialog::getSaveFileName(parent, "Save barcode label sheet", suggested, "PDF (*.pdf)");
        if (path.isEmpty())
            return false;
        QPdfWriter pdf(path);
        pdf.setPageSize(QPageSize(QPageSize::A4));
        pdf.setPageMargins(QMarginsF(10, 10, 10, 10), QPageLayout::Millimeter);
        pdf.setResolution(300);
        pdf.setCreator("Syntro-Check");
        QPainter p(&pdf);
        if (!p.isActive())
            return false;
        const QImage img = BarcodeArt::render(code, 4, 120, true);
        QFont f = p.font();
        f.setPixelSize(34);
        f.setBold(true);
        p.setFont(f);
        const int cols = 3, rows = 8;
        const QRect page = p.viewport();
        const int cw = page.width() / cols, ch = page.height() / rows;
        for (int r = 0; r < rows; ++r)
            for (int c = 0; c < cols; ++c)
            {
                const QRect cell = QRect(c * cw, r * ch, cw, ch).adjusted(12, 12, -12, -12);
                p.drawText(QRect(cell.left(), cell.top(), cell.width(), 44), Qt::AlignCenter, name.left(26));
                const QRect box = cell.adjusted(0, 48, 0, 0);
                const QSize s = img.size().scaled(box.size(), Qt::KeepAspectRatio);
                p.drawImage(QRect(box.center().x() - s.width() / 2, box.top(), s.width(), s.height()), img);
            }
        p.end();
        return true;
    }
}

namespace Dialogs
{
    bool editProduct(QWidget *parent, const ProductInfo *existing)
    {
        auto &app = AppState::instance();
        auto &store = UiStore::instance();
        const bool isNew = (existing == nullptr);
        const ProductInfo cur = existing ? *existing : ProductInfo();

        Ui::Modal d(parent, isNew ? "plus" : "edit",
                    isNew ? "Add New Product to Catalog" : "Edit Product",
                    isNew ? "Configure SKU specs, retail pricing and auto-generate the category-linked EAN-8 barcode"
                          : "Change the product details. The barcode stays the same.",
                    isNew ? "SKU ENTRY" : "EDIT", 900);

        auto *cols = new QHBoxLayout;
        cols->setSpacing(14);
        auto *left = new QVBoxLayout;
        left->setSpacing(12);

        // ------------------------------------------------ 1. category
        auto *c1 = W::card(nullptr, "subCard");
        auto *v1 = new QVBoxLayout(c1);
        v1->setContentsMargins(14, 12, 14, 12);
        v1->setSpacing(8);
        auto *h1 = new QHBoxLayout;
        h1->setSpacing(8);
        h1->addWidget(stepDot(1));
        h1->addWidget(W::label("Category Selection *", "h2"));
        h1->addStretch();
        h1->addWidget(W::label("DEFINES BARCODE PREFIX", "caption"));
        v1->addLayout(h1);

        auto *catBox = new QComboBox;
        const QString curCat = cur.ean.left(2);
        bool curListed = false;
        for (const auto &c : store.categories())
        {
            // The Increment 2 backend only accepts some 2-digit prefixes; those are listed but cannot be picked.
            const bool usable = app.backendAcceptsPrefix(c.id) || c.id == curCat;
            catBox->addItem(QString("%1 - %2%3").arg(c.id, c.name, usable ? QString() : QString("   (prefix not accepted by Product.txt rules yet)")),
                            c.id);
            if (!usable)
                if (auto *m = qobject_cast<QStandardItemModel *>(catBox->model()))
                    if (auto *it = m->item(catBox->count() - 1))
                        it->setEnabled(false);
            if (c.id == curCat)
                curListed = true;
        }
        if (!isNew && !curListed && curCat.size() == 2)
            catBox->addItem(curCat + " - (category not configured)", curCat);
        if (isNew)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < catBox->count(); ++i)
            {
                auto *m = qobject_cast<QStandardItemModel *>(catBox->model());
                if (!m || m->item(i)->isEnabled())
                {
                    catBox->setCurrentIndex(i);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        else
        {
            const int idx = catBox->findData(curCat);
            if (idx >= 0)
                catBox->setCurrentIndex(idx);
            catBox->setEnabled(false); // the category is part of the barcode, which is fixed
        }
        catBox->setMinimumHeight(38);
        v1->addWidget(catBox);
        auto *note = W::label("The first two digits of every barcode come automatically from the Category ID "
                              "(e.g. 10 for Dairy). Categories are managed on the Categories page.", "muted");
        note->setWordWrap(true);
        v1->addWidget(note);
        left->addWidget(c1);

        // ------------------------------------------------ 2. specifications
        auto *c2 = W::card(nullptr, "subCard");
        auto *v2 = new QVBoxLayout(c2);
        v2->setContentsMargins(14, 12, 14, 12);
        v2->setSpacing(8);
        auto *h2 = new QHBoxLayout;
        h2->setSpacing(8);
        h2->addWidget(stepDot(2));
        h2->addWidget(W::label("Product Specifications", "h2"));
        h2->addStretch();
        v2->addLayout(h2);

        auto *g = new QGridLayout;
        g->setHorizontalSpacing(10);
        g->setVerticalSpacing(4);
        g->setColumnStretch(0, 3);
        g->setColumnStretch(1, 3);
        g->setColumnStretch(2, 3);

        auto *idField = new QLineEdit(QString("PRD-%1").arg(isNew ? app.nextProductId() : cur.id, 4, 10, QChar('0')));
        idField->setReadOnly(true);
        idField->setMinimumHeight(36);
        auto *name = new QLineEdit(cur.name);
        name->setPlaceholderText("e.g. Olpers Full Cream Milk 1.5L");
        name->setMinimumHeight(36);
        g->addWidget(W::label("PRODUCT ID", "caption"), 0, 0);
        g->addWidget(W::label("PRODUCT NAME *", "caption"), 0, 1, 1, 2);
        g->addWidget(idField, 1, 0);
        g->addWidget(name, 1, 1, 1, 2);

        auto *acr = new QLineEdit(cur.sku);
        acr->setMaxLength(8);
        acr->setPlaceholderText("e.g. OLPMILK");
        acr->setMinimumHeight(36);
        auto *acrCount = W::label("", "caption");
        auto *acrHead = new QHBoxLayout;
        acrHead->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        acrHead->addWidget(W::label("ACRONYM *", "caption"));
        acrHead->addStretch();
        acrHead->addWidget(acrCount);
        auto *acrHeadW = new QWidget;
        acrHeadW->setLayout(acrHead);
        g->addWidget(acrHeadW, 2, 0);
        g->addWidget(W::label("UNIT TYPE", "caption"), 2, 1, 1, 2);
        g->addWidget(acr, 3, 0);

        auto *unitRow = new QHBoxLayout;
        unitRow->setSpacing(6);
        auto *countBtn = W::button("Count / Unit", "tab");
        auto *weightBtn = W::button("Weight / Kg", "tab");
        countBtn->setCheckable(true);
        weightBtn->setCheckable(true);
        countBtn->setMinimumHeight(36);
        weightBtn->setMinimumHeight(36);
        auto *unitGroup = new QButtonGroup(&d);
        unitGroup->setExclusive(true);
        unitGroup->addButton(countBtn);
        unitGroup->addButton(weightBtn);
        (cur.unit == "weight" ? weightBtn : countBtn)->setChecked(true);
        unitRow->addWidget(countBtn, 1);
        unitRow->addWidget(weightBtn, 1);
        auto *unitW = new QWidget;
        unitW->setLayout(unitRow);
        unitRow->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        g->addWidget(unitW, 3, 1, 1, 2);

        auto money = [&](double value)
        {
            auto *s = new QDoubleSpinBox;
            s->setPrefix("PKR ");
            s->setDecimals(2);
            s->setRange(0, 10000000);
            s->setValue(value);
            s->setMinimumHeight(36);
            return s;
        };
        auto *price = money(cur.price);
        auto *tax = money(cur.tax);
        auto *disc = money(cur.discount);
        g->addWidget(W::label("RETAIL PRICE (NO TAX)", "caption"), 4, 0);
        g->addWidget(W::label("TAX PER UNIT", "caption"), 4, 1);
        g->addWidget(W::label("DISCOUNT / UNIT", "caption"), 4, 2);
        g->addWidget(price, 5, 0);
        g->addWidget(tax, 5, 1);
        g->addWidget(disc, 5, 2);
        v2->addLayout(g);

        auto *gst = W::button("Set tax to 5% of price", "mini");
        QObject::connect(gst, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [=] { tax->setValue(qRound64(price->value() * 5.0) / 100.0); });
        v2->addWidget(gst, 0, Qt::AlignLeft);

        // stock stepper
        auto *stockRow = new QHBoxLayout;
        stockRow->setSpacing(8);
        auto *stockTxt = new QVBoxLayout;
        stockTxt->setSpacing(0);
        stockTxt->addWidget(W::label(isNew ? "Initial Stock Volume" : "Stock on hand", "h2"));
        stockTxt->addWidget(W::label(isNew ? "Register the starting quantity" : "Available quantity", "muted"));
        stockRow->addLayout(stockTxt, 1);
        auto *minus = W::button("−", "step");
        auto *plus = W::button("+", "step");
        auto *stock = new QSpinBox;
        stock->setRange(0, 1000000);
        stock->setValue(cur.stock);
        stock->setButtonSymbols(QAbstractSpinBox::NoButtons);
        stock->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        stock->setMinimumWidth(80);
        stock->setMinimumHeight(36);
        QObject::connect(minus, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [=] { stock->setValue(stock->value() - 1); });
        QObject::connect(plus, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [=] { stock->setValue(stock->value() + 1); });
        stockRow->addWidget(minus);
        stockRow->addWidget(stock);
        stockRow->addWidget(plus);
        v2->addLayout(stockRow);
        left->addWidget(c2);
        left->addStretch();
        cols->addLayout(left, 6);

        // ------------------------------------------------ barcode engine (right)
        auto *c3 = W::card(nullptr, "subCard");
        auto *v3 = new QVBoxLayout(c3);
        v3->setContentsMargins(14, 12, 14, 12);
        v3->setSpacing(8);
        auto *h3 = new QHBoxLayout;
        h3->setSpacing(8);
        h3->addWidget(W::label("EAN-8 Barcode Engine", "h2"));
        h3->addStretch();
        auto *engineBadge = W::badge("Ready", "green");
        h3->addWidget(engineBadge);
        v3->addLayout(h3);

        auto *gen = W::button(isNew ? "Generate Barcode" : "Barcode locked", "primary", "bolt");
        gen->setMinimumHeight(40);
        gen->setEnabled(isNew);
        v3->addWidget(gen);

        auto *preview = new QLabel;
        preview->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        preview->setMinimumHeight(96);
        preview->setStyleSheet("background:white; border:1px solid #dbe4f7; border-radius:10px;");
        v3->addWidget(preview);

        auto *digits = new QHBoxLayout;
        digits->setSpacing(6);
        digits->addStretch();
        auto *dPre = W::label("", "mono");
        auto *dSer = W::label("", "mono");
        auto *dChk = W::label("", "mono");
        dPre->setStyleSheet("background:#dce1ff; color:#1d46b4; font-weight:800; font-size:17px; padding:3px 8px; border-radius:6px;");
        dSer->setStyleSheet("font-weight:800; font-size:17px; padding:3px 4px;");
        dChk->setStyleSheet("background:#85f8c4; color:#005137; font-weight:800; font-size:17px; padding:3px 8px; border-radius:6px;");
        digits->addWidget(dPre);
        digits->addWidget(dSer);
        digits->addWidget(dChk);
        digits->addStretch();
        v3->addLayout(digits);

        auto kv = [&](const QString &k, QLabel *&val)
        {
            auto *r = new QHBoxLayout;
            r->addWidget(W::label(k, "muted"));
            r->addStretch();
            val = W::label("—", "mono");
            val->setStyleSheet("font-weight:700;");
            r->addWidget(val);
            v3->addLayout(r);
        };
        QLabel *kPrefix = nullptr, *kSerial = nullptr, *kCheck = nullptr;
        kv("Category prefix", kPrefix);
        kv("Item serial", kSerial);
        kv("Mod-10 check digit", kCheck);
        auto *pathLbl = W::label("", "muted");
        pathLbl->setWordWrap(true);
        v3->addWidget(pathLbl);

        auto *sheet = W::button("Print Barcode Label Sheet", "soft", "print");
        sheet->setMinimumHeight(38);
        v3->addWidget(sheet);
        v3->addStretch();
        cols->addWidget(c3, 4);
        d.body()->addLayout(cols);

        auto *errLbl = W::label("", "muted");
        errLbl->setWordWrap(true);
        errLbl->setStyleSheet("color:#ba1a1a; font-weight:700; background:#ffdad6; border-radius:8px; padding:8px 10px;");
        errLbl->hide();
        d.body()->addWidget(errLbl);

        // ------------------------------------------------ behaviour
        QString code = isNew ? QString() : cur.ean;
        auto showCode = [&]
        {
            const bool have = code.size() == 8;
            dPre->setText(have ? code.left(2) : "--");
            dSer->setText(have ? code.mid(2, 5) : "-----");
            dChk->setText(have ? code.right(1) : "-");
            kPrefix->setText(have ? code.left(2) : "—");
            kSerial->setText(have ? code.mid(2, 5) : "—");
            kCheck->setText(have ? code.right(1) + "  (valid)" : "—");
            pathLbl->setText(have ? "Saved as data/barcodes/" + code + ".png" : "No barcode yet - pick a category.");
            sheet->setEnabled(have);
            if (have)
                preview->setPixmap(QPixmap::fromImage(BarcodeArt::render(code, 2, 70, false)));
            else
                preview->setText("—");
            W::setBadge(engineBadge, have ? "Ready" : "Waiting", have ? "green" : "grey");
        };
        auto generate = [&]
        {
            if (!isNew)
                return;
            code = app.nextBarcode(catBox->currentData().toString());
            if (code.isEmpty())
            {
                errLbl->setText("No free barcode left in this category (serial 00001 - 99999 is used up).");
                errLbl->show();
            }
            else
                errLbl->hide();
            showCode();
        };
        auto suggestAcronym = [&]
        {
            QString s = name->text().toUpper();
            s.remove(QRegularExpression("[^A-Z0-9]"));
            return s.left(8);
        };
        bool acrTouched = !isNew;
        auto updateCount = [&] { acrCount->setText(QString("[ %1 / 8 max ]").arg(acr->text().size())); };
        QObject::connect(acr, &QLineEdit::textEdited, &d, [&](const QString &) { acrTouched = true; });
        QObject::connect(acr, &QLineEdit::textChanged, &d, [&](const QString &) { updateCount(); });
        QObject::connect(name, &QLineEdit::textChanged, &d, [&](const QString &)
                         {
            if (!acrTouched)
                acr->setText(suggestAcronym()); });
        QObject::connect(catBox, qOverload<int>(&QComboBox::currentIndexChanged), &d, [&](int) { generate(); });
        QObject::connect(gen, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&] { generate(); });
        QObject::connect(sheet, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&]
                         {
            if (code.size() == 8 && saveLabelSheet(&d, code, name->text().trimmed().isEmpty() ? acr->text() : name->text().trimmed()))
                errLbl->hide(); });

        // footer: Cancel [Esc] ... hint ... Save [Enter]
        auto *cancel = W::button("Cancel  [Esc]", "", "close");
        auto *hint = W::label("Press Enter to save instantly", "muted");
        auto *save = W::button(isNew ? "Save Product to Inventory" : "Save Changes", "primary", "check");
        save->setMinimumHeight(40);
        save->setDefault(true);
        d.footer()->addWidget(cancel);
        d.footer()->addStretch();
        d.footer()->addWidget(hint);
        d.footer()->addWidget(save);
        QObject::connect(cancel, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::reject);
        QObject::connect(save, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&]
                         {
            ProductInfo p = cur;
            p.name = name->text().trimmed();
            p.sku = acr->text().trimmed().toUpper();
            p.unit = weightBtn->isChecked() ? "weight" : "count";
            p.price = price->value();
            p.tax = tax->value();
            p.discount = disc->value();
            p.stock = stock->value();
            p.ean = code;
            QString err;
            const bool ok = isNew ? app.addProduct(p, &err) : app.updateProduct(p, &err);
            if (!ok)
            {
                errLbl->setText(err.isEmpty() ? QString("The product could not be saved.") : err);
                errLbl->show();
                return;
            }
            d.accept(); });

        updateCount();
        if (isNew)
            generate();
        else
            showCode();
        name->setFocus();
        return d.exec() == QDialog::Accepted;
    }
}

#include "ProductsPage.h"
#include "Dialogs.h"

using namespace Ui; // makeTable, item, wrapW, pageShell, writeCsv (see UiKit.h)


// =================================================================== ProductsPage
ProductsPage::ProductsPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root;
    pageShell(this, root);
    auto* card = W::card(this);
    auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(card);
    v->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 10);
    v->setSpacing(10);
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout;
    h->addWidget(W::iconLabel("products", Theme::primary, 20));
    h->addWidget(W::label("Product Catalog", "h1"));
    m_count = W::badge("", "blue");
    h->addWidget(m_count);
    h->addStretch();
    m_search = new QLineEdit;
    m_search->setPlaceholderText("Search name, acronym, id or barcode...");
    m_search->setMinimumWidth(280);
    m_search->addAction(Theme::icon("search", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
    h->addWidget(m_search);
    v->addLayout(h);
    m_issues = W::button("", "soft", "warn");
    connect(m_issues, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this] { Dialogs::loadIssues(this); });
    h->insertWidget(3, m_issues);
    m_newBtn = W::button("New Product", "primary", "plus");
    m_newBtn->setVisible(false);
    connect(m_newBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ProductsPage::addNew);
    h->addWidget(m_newBtn);
    m_table = makeTable({ "ID", "ACRONYM", "BARCODE", "PRODUCT", "UNIT", "PRICE", "TAX", "DISC.", "STOCK", "STATUS", "" });
    m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView::Stretch);
    for (int c : {0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9})
        m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(c, QHeaderView::ResizeToContents);
    m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(10, QHeaderView::Fixed);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(10, 250);
    v->addWidget(m_table, 1);
    root->addWidget(card, 1);
    connect(m_search, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this]
        { rebuild(); });
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &ProductsPage::rebuild);
    rebuild();
}

void ProductsPage::setSearch(const QString& text) { m_search->setText(text); }

void ProductsPage::rebuild()
{
    const QString q = m_search->text().trimmed();
    auto& st = AppState::instance();
    const QVector<ProductInfo> rows = q.isEmpty() ? st.products() : st.searchText(q);
    m_count->setText(QString("%1 products").arg(rows.size()));
    const int nIssues = st.productIssues().size();
    m_issues->setVisible(nIssues > 0);
    m_issues->setText(QString("Data issues (%1)").arg(nIssues));
    m_table->setRowCount(rows.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < rows.size(); ++r)
    {
        const auto& p = rows[r];
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 44);
        const int limit = st.settings().lowStockThreshold;
        m_table->setItem(r, 0, item(QString::number(p.id)));
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, item(p.sku, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "#004ac6", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 2, item(p.ean));
        m_table->setItem(r, 3, item(p.name, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 4, item(p.unit));
        m_table->setItem(r, 5, item(W::money(p.price), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter, "", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 6, item(W::money(p.tax), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter));
        m_table->setItem(r, 7, item(W::money(p.discount), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter));
        const bool low = p.stock <= limit;
        m_table->setItem(r, 8, item(QString::number(p.stock), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter, low ? "#ba1a1a" : "", low));
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 9, wrapW(W::badge(p.stock == 0 ? "Out" : low ? "Low" : "OK", p.stock == 0 ? "red" : low ? "amber" : "green")));
        auto* d = W::button("Details", "soft");
        auto* cell = new QWidget;
        auto* cl = new QHBoxLayout(cell);
        cl->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 4, 0);
        cl->setSpacing(6);
        cl->addWidget(d);
        const QString ean = p.ean;
        const int pid = p.id;
        if (RolePolicy::canEditCatalogue(m_role)) // manager: edit the product in the same dialog as "New Product"
        {
            auto* e = W::button("Edit", "mini", "edit");
            cl->addWidget(e);
            const ProductInfo copy = p;
            connect(e, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, copy]
                {
                    // open after the click handler returns: saving rebuilds this table (and this button)
                    QTimer::singleShot(0, this, [this, copy]
                        {
                            if (Dialogs::editProduct(this, &copy))
                                emit toast("Product " + copy.sku + " updated in Product.txt");
                        });
                });
        }
        if (!m_viewOnly) // Manager views products only: no "Add" button
        {
            auto* b = W::button("Add", "mini");
            b->setEnabled(p.stock > 0);
            cl->addWidget(b);
            connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, ean]
                { emit addToOrder(ean); });
        }
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 10, cell);
        connect(d, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, pid]
            { Dialogs::productDescription(this, pid); });
    }
}
void ProductsPage::setViewOnly(bool on)
{
    m_viewOnly = on;
    m_table->setRowCount(0); // drop the old rows (with their buttons) before rebuilding
    rebuild();
}

void ProductsPage::setRole(UserRole role)
{
    m_role = role;
    m_newBtn->setVisible(RolePolicy::canEditCatalogue(role));
    m_table->setRowCount(0);
    rebuild();
}

void ProductsPage::addNew()
{
    if (RolePolicy::canEditCatalogue(m_role) && Dialogs::editProduct(this, nullptr))
        emit toast("Product saved to Product.txt");
}

#include "RecordOrderPage.h"
#include "AppState.h"
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "Pricing.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QDateTime>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHeaderView>
#include <QInputDialog>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QScrollArea>
#include <QShortcut>
#include <QTimer>
#include <QTableWidget>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <QtMath>

namespace
{
    QWidget* twoLine(const QString& top, const QString& bottom)
    {
        auto* w = new QWidget;
        auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(w);
        v->setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4);
        v->setSpacing(1);
        auto* t = W::label(top);
        t->setStyleSheet("font-weight:700; font-size:13.5px;");
        t->setWordWrap(true);
        auto* b = W::label(bottom);
        b->setStyleSheet("color:#8a94ab; font-size:11.5px;");
        v->addWidget(t);
        v->addWidget(b);
        return w;
    }
    QTableWidgetItem* cell(const QString& text, const QString& color = "", bool bold = false)
    {
        auto* i = new QTableWidgetItem(text);
        i->setTextAlignment(Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter);
        if (!color.isEmpty())
            i->setForeground(Theme::readable(QColor(color)));
        if (bold)
        {
            QFont f = i->font();
            f.setBold(true);
            i->setFont(f);
        }
        return i;
    }
}

RecordOrderPage::RecordOrderPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    auto* root = W::pageLayout(this);
    root->addWidget(buildCustomerStrip());
    auto* body = new QHBoxLayout;
    body->setSpacing(0);
    body->addWidget(buildLeft(), 36);
    body->addWidget(buildRight(), 64);
    root->addLayout(body, 1);

    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::scanned, this, [this](const QString& ean)
        { showCode(ean, true); });
    connect(m_feed, &CameraFeedWidget::barcodeDetected, this, [this](const QString& ean) {
        const auto product = AppState::instance().findByCode(ean);
        if (product) m_feed->showResult(ean + "  -  " + product->name, true);
        else m_feed->showResult(ean + "  -  not in Product.txt", false);
        AppState::instance().emitScan(ean);
        });
    connect(m_feed, &CameraFeedWidget::scanError, this, &RecordOrderPage::toast);
    loadDemoDraft();

    // keyboard: F2 hold, F10 checkout, F4 description, F9 camera (only while this page is visible)
    auto key = [this](const QKeySequence& k)
        {
            auto* s = new QShortcut(k, this);
            s->setContext(Qt::WidgetWithChildrenShortcut);
            return s;
        };
    connect(key(QKeySequence(Qt::Key_F2)), &QShortcut::activated, this, &RecordOrderPage::holdOrder);
    connect(key(QKeySequence(Qt::Key_F10)), &QShortcut::activated, this, &RecordOrderPage::checkout);
    connect(key(QKeySequence(Qt::Key_F4)), &QShortcut::activated, this, &RecordOrderPage::describeCurrent);
    connect(key(QKeySequence(Qt::Key_F9)), &QShortcut::activated, m_camToggle, &QPushButton::click);

    // on-hold chips: redraw when the list changes and every 30 s (the "4m ago" text)
    connect(&UiStore::instance(), &UiStore::parkedChanged, this, &RecordOrderPage::rebuildParked);
    auto* ageTimer = new QTimer(this);
    ageTimer->setInterval(30000);
    connect(ageTimer, &QTimer::timeout, this, &RecordOrderPage::rebuildParked);
    ageTimer->start();
    rebuildParked();
}

QWidget* RecordOrderPage::buildCustomerStrip()
{
    auto* strip = W::card(this, "strip");
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(strip);
    h->setContentsMargins(14, 8, 14, 8);
    h->setSpacing(12);
    h->addWidget(W::iconTile("user", Qt::white, "#7fb0ff", "#2b5fe8", 40));

    auto field = [&](const QString& cap, QWidget* content, int stretch)
        {
            auto* f = new QFrame;
            f->setObjectName("field");
            auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(f);
            v->setContentsMargins(12, 5, 12, 6);
            v->setSpacing(0);
            v->addWidget(W::label(cap, "caption"));
            v->addWidget(content);
            h->addWidget(f, stretch);
        };
    m_customer = new QLineEdit("Marcus Vance");
    m_customer->setProperty("flat", true);
    m_customer->setPlaceholderText("Walk-in customer");
    field("CUSTOMER", m_customer, 3);
    m_contact = new QLineEdit("+1 (555) 019-2834");
    m_contact->setProperty("flat", true);
    m_contact->setPlaceholderText("Phone (optional)");
    field("PHONE", m_contact, 3);


    h->addStretch(1);
    auto* sv = new QVBoxLayout;
    sv->setSpacing(0);
    sv->addWidget(W::label("ORDER", "caption"));
    m_sessionId = W::label("#" + AppState::instance().nextOrderId(), "mono");
    m_sessionId->setStyleSheet("font-weight:800; font-size:14px;");
    sv->addWidget(m_sessionId);
    h->addLayout(sv);
    return strip;
}

{
    auto* col = new QWidget;

    v->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    v->setSpacing(0);

    auto* cam = W::card(col);

    cv->setSpacing(10);

    m_feed->setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Expanding);
    cv->addWidget(m_feed, 1);

    cv->addLayout(btns);
    connect(m_camToggle, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        {
            const bool on = !m_feed->running();
            m_feed->setRunning(on);
            const bool running = m_feed->running();
            m_camToggle->setText(running ? "Stop camera" : "Start camera  [F9]");
            m_camToggle->setIcon(Theme::icon(running ? "stop" : "play", QColor("white"), 16)); });

    auto* mr = new QHBoxLayout;
    mr->setSpacing(8);
    m_manual = new QLineEdit;
    m_manual->setPlaceholderText("Type a barcode and press Enter");
    m_manual->setMaxLength(12);
    m_manual->setMinimumHeight(40);
    m_manual->addAction(Theme::icon("search", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);

    mr->addWidget(m_manual, 1);
    mr->addWidget(browse);
    cv->addLayout(mr);
    connect(m_manual, &QLineEdit::returnPressed, this, [this]
        { showCode(m_manual->text(), false); });
    connect(browse, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &RecordOrderPage::quickLookup);
    v->addWidget(cam, 5);

    auto* pc = W::card(col);

    pv->setContentsMargins(16, 14, 16, 14);
    pv->setSpacing(10);
    m_pBadge = W::badge("—", "grey");

    auto* info = W::card(pc, "subCard");
    auto* ih = new QHBoxLayout(info);
    ih->setContentsMargins(12, 10, 12, 10);
    ih->setSpacing(12);
    m_pThumb = new QLabel;
    m_pThumb->setFixedSize(60, 72);
    m_pThumb->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    ih->addWidget(m_pThumb);
    auto* iv = new QVBoxLayout;
    iv->setSpacing(2);
    auto* nr = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_pName = W::label("Nothing scanned yet", "h1");
    m_pName->setWordWrap(true);
    m_pPrice = W::label("");
    m_pPrice->setStyleSheet("color:#2554d8; font-size:17px; font-weight:800;");
    nr->addWidget(m_pName, 1);
    nr->addWidget(m_pPrice);
    iv->addLayout(nr);
    m_pMeta = W::label("Scan a barcode or type one above.", "muted");
    m_pMeta->setWordWrap(true);
    iv->addWidget(m_pMeta);
    m_pStock = W::label("");
    m_pStock->setStyleSheet("font-weight:700;");
    iv->addWidget(m_pStock);
    ih->addLayout(iv, 1);
    pv->addWidget(info);

    m_addBtn = W::button("Add to order", "primary", "cart");
    m_addBtn->setMinimumHeight(42);
    m_addBtn->setEnabled(false);
    m_descBtn = W::button("Description", "soft", "search");
    m_descBtn->setEnabled(false);
    pv->addWidget(m_descBtn);
    pv->addWidget(m_addBtn);
    connect(m_descBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        { if (m_preview && m_preview->id > 0) Dialogs::productDescription(this, m_preview->id); });
    connect(m_addBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        { if (m_preview) addToCart(*m_preview); });
    v->addWidget(pc, 3);
    return col;
}

QWidget* RecordOrderPage::buildRight()
{
    auto* col = new QWidget;
    auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(col);
    v->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    v->setSpacing(0);

    v->addWidget(buildParkedStrip());

    auto* oc = W::card(col);
    auto* ov = new QVBoxLayout(oc);
    ov->setContentsMargins(0, 14, 0, 10);
    ov->setSpacing(6);

    auto* head = new QHBoxLayout;
    head->setContentsMargins(18, 0, 14, 0);
    head->setSpacing(10);
    head->addWidget(W::iconLabel("orders", Theme::primary, 20));
    head->addWidget(W::label("Current order", "h1"));
    m_itemsBadge = W::badge("0 items", "grey");
    head->addWidget(m_itemsBadge);
    head->addStretch();
    auto* clear = W::button("Clear", "flat", "trash");
    head->addWidget(clear);
    ov->addLayout(head);
    connect(clear, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &RecordOrderPage::newDraft);

    m_table = new QTableWidget(0, 6);
    m_table->setHorizontalHeaderLabels({ "PRODUCT", "PRICE", "QTY", "DISCOUNT", "TOTAL", "" });
    m_table->verticalHeader()->hide();
    m_table->setShowGrid(false);
    m_table->setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView::NoSelection);
    m_table->setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView::NoEditTriggers);
    m_table->setFocusPolicy(Qt::NoFocus);
    m_table->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
    auto* hh = m_table->horizontalHeader();
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView::Fixed);
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView::Stretch);
    hh->setHighlightSections(false);
    hh->setDefaultAlignment(Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(1, 78);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(2, 112);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(3, 92);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(4, 96);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(5, 52);
    m_table->setWordWrap(false);
    ov->addWidget(m_table, 1);
    v->addWidget(oc, 1);

    auto* tc = W::card(col, "totals");
    auto* th = new QHBoxLayout(tc);
    th->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 14);
    th->setSpacing(18);
    auto* grid = new QVBoxLayout;
    grid->setSpacing(8);
    auto row = [&](const QString& k, QLabel*& val)
        {
            auto* r = new QHBoxLayout;
            r->addWidget(W::label(k, "muted"));
            r->addStretch();
            val = W::label("PKR 0.00");
            val->setStyleSheet("font-weight:800; font-size:14px;");
            r->addWidget(val);
            grid->addLayout(r);
        };
    row("Subtotal", m_gross);
    row("Discount", m_disc);
    row("Tax", m_tax);
    grid->addStretch();
    th->addLayout(grid, 1);

    auto* tb = W::card(tc, "totalBox");
    auto* tv = new QVBoxLayout(tb);
    tv->setContentsMargins(18, 12, 18, 14);
    tv->setSpacing(2);
    auto* cap = W::label("TOTAL", "caption");
    tv->addWidget(cap);
    m_total = W::label("PKR 0.00");
    m_total->setStyleSheet("font-size:34px; font-weight:800; color:white;");
    tv->addWidget(m_total);
    auto* actions = new QHBoxLayout;
    actions->setSpacing(8);
    m_hold = W::button("Hold  [F2]", "soft", "pause");
    m_hold->setMinimumHeight(44);
    m_hold->setToolTip("Put this order on hold and start a new one (one press, no questions)");
    m_checkout = W::button("Checkout  [F10]", "primary", "check");
    m_checkout->setMinimumHeight(44);
    actions->addWidget(m_hold, 2);
    actions->addWidget(m_checkout, 3);
    tv->addLayout(actions);
    connect(m_hold, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &RecordOrderPage::holdOrder);
    th->addWidget(tb, 1);
    connect(m_checkout, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &RecordOrderPage::checkout);
    v->addWidget(tc);
    return col;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- behaviour
void RecordOrderPage::focusInput()
{
    m_manual->setFocus();
    m_manual->selectAll();
}

void RecordOrderPage::simulateScan()
{
    if (!m_feed->running()) m_feed->setRunning(true);
    if (!m_feed->running()) emit toast("Unable to start camera scanning");
    else {
        m_camToggle->setText("Stop camera");
        m_camToggle->setIcon(Theme::icon("stop", QColor("white"), 16));
    }
}

void RecordOrderPage::quickLookup()
{
    QStringList items;
    for (const auto& p : AppState::instance().products())
        items << QString("%1  -  %2").arg(p.ean, p.name);
    bool ok = false;
    const QString pick = QInputDialog::getItem(this, "Browse products", "Choose a product:", items, 0, false, &ok);
    if (ok)
        showCode(pick.section("  -  ", 0, 0), false);
}

void RecordOrderPage::showCode(const QString& codeIn, bool fromScanner)
{
    const QString code = codeIn.trimmed();
    if (code.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast("Enter a barcode first");
        return;
    }
    const auto p = AppState::instance().findByCode(code);
    if (!p)
    {
        setPreview(std::nullopt);
        emit toast("No product found for \"" + code + "\"");
        return;
    }
    m_feed->setCode(p->ean);
    setPreview(p);
    m_manual->clear();
    if (fromScanner && AppState::instance().settings().autoAddScans)
        addToCart(*p);
}

void RecordOrderPage::setPreview(const std::optional<ProductInfo>& p)
{
    m_preview = p;
    m_addBtn->setEnabled(p.has_value() && p->stock > 0);
    m_descBtn->setEnabled(p.has_value() && p->id > 0);
    if (!p)
    {
        m_pName->setText("Nothing scanned yet");
        m_pPrice->clear();
        m_pMeta->setText("Scan a barcode or type one above.");
        m_pStock->clear();
        W::setBadge(m_pBadge, "—", "grey");
        m_pThumb->clear();
        return;
    }
    m_pName->setText(p->name);
    m_pPrice->setText(W::money(p->price));
    m_pMeta->setText(QString("%1  •  %2  •  tax %3  •  disc %4").arg(p->ean, p->unit, W::money(p->tax), W::money(p->discount)));
    const bool low = p->stock <= AppState::instance().settings().lowStockThreshold;
    m_pStock->setText(QString("%1 in stock").arg(p->stock));
    m_pStock->setStyleSheet(QString("font-weight:700; color:%1;").arg(low ? "#b3261e" : "#0b7a36"));
    W::setBadge(m_pBadge, p->stock == 0 ? "Out of stock" : low ? "Low stock" : "In stock",
        p->stock == 0 ? "red" : low ? "amber" : "green");

    // little 3D "box" thumbnail
    QPixmap pm(120, 144);
    pm.setDevicePixelRatio(2);
    pm.fill(Qt::transparent);
    QPainter pa(&pm);
    pa.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    pa.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    pa.setBrush(QColor(20, 40, 110, 40));
    pa.drawEllipse(QRectF(6, 60, 46, 8));
    QLinearGradient fg(10, 8, 10, 62);
    fg.setColorAt(0, QColor("#7fb0ff"));
    fg.setColorAt(1, QColor("#2b5fe8"));
    pa.setBrush(fg);
    pa.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(8, 14, 34, 46), 4, 4);
    pa.setBrush(QColor("#1a3fae"));
    pa.drawPolygon(QPolygonF({ {42, 14}, {50, 8}, {50, 54}, {42, 60} }));
    pa.setBrush(QColor("#a9ccff"));
    pa.drawPolygon(QPolygonF({ {8, 14}, {16, 8}, {50, 8}, {42, 14} }));
    pa.setBrush(QColor(255, 255, 255, 230));
    pa.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(13, 26, 24, 22), 3, 3);
    pa.setPen(QColor("#1a3fae"));
    QFont f = pa.font();
    f.setPixelSize(8);
    f.setBold(true);
    pa.setFont(f);
    pa.drawText(QRectF(13, 26, 24, 22), Qt::AlignCenter, p->name.section(' ', 0, 0).left(5).toUpper());
    pa.end();
    m_pThumb->setPixmap(pm);
}

void RecordOrderPage::addToCart(const ProductInfo& p, int qty)
{
    int have = 0;
    for (const auto& l : m_cart)
        if (l.p.sku == p.sku)
            have = l.qty;
    const QString problem = AppState::instance().stockProblem(p.id, have, qty);
    if (!problem.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast(problem);
        return;
    }
    bool found = false;
    for (auto& l : m_cart)
        if (l.p.sku == p.sku)
        {
            l.qty += qty;
            found = true;
        }
    if (!found)
        m_cart.push_back({ p, qty });
    rebuildTable();
    emit toast(QString("Added %1 × %2").arg(qty).arg(p.name));
}

void RecordOrderPage::changeQty(int row, int delta)
{
    if (row < 0 || row >= m_cart.size())
        return;
    auto& l = m_cart[row];
    const int nq = l.qty + delta;
    if (nq < 1)
    {
        removeRow(row);
        return;
    }
    const QString problem = AppState::instance().stockProblem(l.p.id, l.qty, delta);
    if (delta > 0 && !problem.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast(problem);
        return;
    }
    l.qty = nq;
    rebuildTable();
}

void RecordOrderPage::removeRow(int row)
{
    if (row < 0 || row >= m_cart.size())
        return;
    m_cart.removeAt(row);
    rebuildTable();
}

void RecordOrderPage::rebuildTable()
{
    m_table->setRowCount(0);
    m_table->setRowCount(m_cart.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < m_cart.size(); ++r)
    {
        const auto& l = m_cart[r];
        const double gross = l.p.price * l.qty;
        const double disc = l.p.discount * l.qty;
        const double tax = l.p.tax * l.qty;
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 62);
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 0, twoLine(l.p.name, l.p.sku + "  •  " + l.p.unit));
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, cell(W::money(l.p.price), "", true));
        auto* stp = new QtyStepper(l.qty);
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 2, stp);
        connect(stp, &QtyStepper::minus, this, [this, r]
            { changeQty(r, -1); });
        connect(stp, &QtyStepper::plus, this, [this, r]
            { changeQty(r, +1); });
        m_table->setItem(r, 3, cell(disc > 0 ? W::money(-disc) : "—", disc > 0 ? "#0b7a36" : "#8a94ab"));
        m_table->setItem(r, 4, cell(W::money(gross - disc + tax), "#2554d8", true));
        auto* del = new QPushButton;
        del->setProperty("kind", "flat");
        del->setIcon(Theme::icon("trash", Theme::error, 18));
        del->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
        del->setToolTip("Remove");
        auto* wrap = new QWidget;
        auto* wl = new QHBoxLayout(wrap);
        wl->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        wl->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        wl->addWidget(del);
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 5, wrap);
        connect(del, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, r]
            { removeRow(r); });
    }
    updateTotals();
}

void RecordOrderPage::updateTotals()
{
    double gross = 0, disc = 0, tax = 0;
    int items = 0;
    for (const auto& l : m_cart)
    {
        gross += l.p.price * l.qty;
        disc += l.p.discount * l.qty;
        tax += l.p.tax * l.qty;
        items += l.qty;
    }
    const double total = gross - disc + tax;
    W::setBadge(m_itemsBadge, QString("%1 item%2").arg(items).arg(items == 1 ? "" : "s"), items ? "blue" : "grey");
    m_gross->setText(W::money(gross));
    m_disc->setText(disc > 0 ? W::money(-disc) : "PKR 0.00");
    m_tax->setText(W::money(tax));
    m_total->setText(W::money(total));
    m_checkout->setEnabled(items > 0);
    m_hold->setEnabled(items > 0);
}

void RecordOrderPage::loadDemoDraft()
{
    // Start with an empty order; products come from Product.txt.
    rebuildTable();
    setPreview(std::nullopt);
}

void RecordOrderPage::newDraft()
{
    m_editingId.clear();
    m_cart.clear();
    setPreview(std::nullopt);
    rebuildTable();
    m_sessionId->setText("#" + AppState::instance().nextOrderId());
    emit toast("New order started");
}

void RecordOrderPage::checkout()
{
    if (m_cart.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast("The order is empty");
        return;
    }
    if (!m_editingId.isEmpty())
    {
        // Front-end only for now: saving edits to an existing order (Order.txt / Bill.txt / stock) arrives with the
        // Increment 3 back-end. Nothing is written, so no duplicate order is created.
        emit toast("Saving edits to order #" + m_editingId + " needs the Increment 3 back-end - nothing was changed");
        return;
    }
    OrderRecord o;
    for (const auto& l : m_cart)
        o.lines.push_back({ l.p.name, l.p.sku, l.qty, l.p.price, l.p.tax, l.p.discount, l.p.id });
    o.recompute();

    Dialogs::PaymentRequest req;
    req.orderId = m_sessionId->text().mid(1);
    req.customer = m_customer->text();
    req.contact = m_contact->text();
    req.lines = o.lines;
    Dialogs::PaymentResult res;
    if (!Dialogs::payment(this, req, res))
        return;
    const QString mode = res.mode, merchant = res.merchant;
    const double tendered = res.tendered;
    const auto quote = Pricing::quote(o.lines, res.specialPercent);
    o.lines = Pricing::fold(o.lines, res.specialPercent);
    Pricing::apply(o, quote);

    o.customer = m_customer->text().trimmed().isEmpty() ? "Walk-in Customer" : m_customer->text().trimmed();
    o.contact = m_contact->text().trimmed().isEmpty() ? "-" : m_contact->text().trimmed();
    o.payment = mode;
    o.merchant = merchant;
    o.tendered = tendered;
    o.status = "Paid";
    o.shift = "POS #04 • Shift A";
    QString checkoutError;
    if (!AppState::instance().addOrder(o, &checkoutError))
    {
        emit toast(checkoutError);
        return;
    }

    Dialogs::receipt(this, o);
    m_cart.clear();
    setPreview(std::nullopt);
    rebuildTable();
    m_sessionId->setText("#" + AppState::instance().nextOrderId());
    emit toast("Order " + o.id + " paid - " + W::money(o.total));
}
// ---- placeholder bodies for the Increment 3 actions (the redesigned screen replaces them) ----
void RecordOrderPage::setRole(UserRole role) { m_role = role; }
void RecordOrderPage::describeCurrent()
{
    if (m_preview && m_preview->id > 0)
        Dialogs::productDescription(this, m_preview->id);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- on hold (parked orders)
QVector<ReceiptLine> RecordOrderPage::cartLines() const
{
    QVector<ReceiptLine> lines;
    for (const auto& l : m_cart)
        lines.push_back({ l.p.name, l.p.sku, l.qty, l.p.price, l.p.tax, l.p.discount, l.p.id });
    return lines;
}

QWidget* RecordOrderPage::buildParkedStrip()
{
    auto* strip = W::card(this, "strip");
    strip->setFixedHeight(62);
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(strip);
    h->setContentsMargins(14, 6, 14, 6);
    h->setSpacing(10);
    h->addWidget(W::iconLabel("pause", Theme::primary, 18));
    m_parkTitle = W::label("ON HOLD", "caption");
    h->addWidget(m_parkTitle);

    auto* scroll = new QScrollArea;
    scroll->setWidgetResizable(true);
    scroll->setFrameShape(QFrame::NoFrame);
    scroll->setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
    scroll->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAsNeeded);
    scroll->setStyleSheet("QScrollArea, QScrollArea > QWidget > QWidget { background: transparent; border: none; }");
    auto* inner = new QWidget;
    m_parkChips = new QHBoxLayout(inner);
    m_parkChips->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    m_parkChips->setSpacing(8);
    scroll->setWidget(inner);
    h->addWidget(scroll, 1);
    m_parkEmpty = W::label("No orders on hold. Press F2 to park the current order.", "muted");
    h->addWidget(m_parkEmpty);
    return strip;
}

void RecordOrderPage::rebuildParked()
{
    // drop the old chips
    while (QLayoutItem* it = m_parkChips->takeAt(0))
    {
        if (QWidget* w = it->widget())
            w->deleteLater();
        delete it;
    }
    const auto& parked = UiStore::instance().parked();
    m_parkTitle->setText(QString("ON HOLD (%1)").arg(parked.size()));
    m_parkEmpty->setVisible(parked.isEmpty());
    const QDateTime now = QDateTime::currentDateTime();
    for (const auto& p : parked)
    {
        const qint64 secs = p.parkedAt.secsTo(now);
        const QString ago = secs < 60 ? QString("just now")
            : secs < 3600 ? QString("%1m ago").arg(secs / 60)
            : QString("%1h ago").arg(secs / 3600);
        const QString text = QString("#%1   %2  •  %3  •  %4   %5")
            .arg(p.code, p.customer.left(18), QString::number(p.itemCount()), W::money(p.total()), ago);
        auto* chip = W::button(text, "soft", "arrow-right");
        chip->setMinimumHeight(36);
        chip->setToolTip("Click to resume this order");
        const QString code = p.code;
        connect(chip, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, code] { resumeParked(code); });
        auto* del = W::button("", "flat", "trash");
        del->setToolTip("Discard this held order");
        connect(del, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, code]
            {
                UiStore::instance().discardParked(code);
                emit toast("Held order " + code + " discarded");
            });
        auto* cell = new QWidget;
        auto* cl = new QHBoxLayout(cell);
        cl->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        cl->setSpacing(2);
        cl->addWidget(chip);
        cl->addWidget(del);
        m_parkChips->addWidget(cell);
    }
    m_parkChips->addStretch();
}

void RecordOrderPage::holdOrder()
{
    if (m_cart.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast("Nothing to hold - the order is empty");
        return;
    }
    const QString code = UiStore::instance().park(m_customer->text(), m_contact->text(), cartLines());
    m_editingId.clear();
    m_cart.clear();
    m_customer->clear();
    m_contact->clear();
    setPreview(std::nullopt);
    rebuildTable();
    m_sessionId->setText("#" + AppState::instance().nextOrderId());
    focusInput(); // ready for the next customer's first scan
    emit toast("Order put on hold as " + code);
}

void RecordOrderPage::resumeParked(const QString& code)
{
    ParkedOrder po;
    if (!UiStore::instance().takeParked(code, po))
    {
        emit toast("That held order is no longer available");
        return;
    }
    // A cart that is not empty is parked first (swap), so nothing is lost and no question is asked.
    QString swapped;
    if (!m_cart.isEmpty() && m_editingId.isEmpty())
        swapped = UiStore::instance().park(m_customer->text(), m_contact->text(), cartLines());
    if (!m_editingId.isEmpty()) // leaving an order that was open for editing
    {
        m_editingId.clear();
        m_sessionId->setText("#" + AppState::instance().nextOrderId());
    }

    m_cart.clear();
    int adjusted = 0;
    for (const auto& line : po.lines)
    {
        std::optional<ProductInfo> prod;
        for (const auto& p : AppState::instance().products())
            if (line.productId > 0 ? p.id == line.productId : p.sku == line.acronym)
            {
                prod = p;
                break;
            }
        if (!prod)
        {
            ++adjusted;
            continue;
        }
        int qty = line.qty;
        while (qty > 0 && !AppState::instance().stockProblem(prod->id, 0, qty).isEmpty())
            --qty; // stock changed while the order was on hold
        if (qty != line.qty)
            ++adjusted;
        if (qty > 0)
            m_cart.push_back({ *prod, qty });
    }
    m_customer->setText(po.customer == "Walk-in Customer" ? QString() : po.customer);
    m_contact->setText(po.contact == "-" ? QString() : po.contact);
    setPreview(std::nullopt);
    rebuildTable();
    focusInput();

    QString msg = "Resumed " + code;
    if (!swapped.isEmpty())
        msg += "  (previous order held as " + swapped + ")";
    if (adjusted > 0)
        msg += QString("  -  %1 line%2 changed because of stock").arg(adjusted).arg(adjusted == 1 ? "" : "s");
    emit toast(msg);
}
// Opens an existing order (paid + approved from the Orders page, or in progress) in the cart.
void RecordOrderPage::loadOrderForEdit(const QString& orderId)
{
    const OrderRecord* found = nullptr;
    for (const auto& o : AppState::instance().orders())
        if (o.id == orderId)
        {
            found = &o;
            break;
        }
    if (!found)
    {
        emit toast("Order #" + orderId + " was not found");
        return;
    }
    // Nothing is lost: a cart that is not empty is parked first, like when resuming a held order.
    QString swapped;
    if (m_editingId.isEmpty() && !m_cart.isEmpty())
        swapped = UiStore::instance().park(m_customer->text(), m_contact->text(), cartLines());

    m_cart.clear();
    for (const auto& line : found->lines)
        for (const auto& p : AppState::instance().products())
            if (line.productId > 0 ? p.id == line.productId : p.sku == line.acronym)
            {
                m_cart.push_back({ p, line.qty });
                break;
            }
    m_editingId = orderId;
    m_customer->setText(found->customer == "Walk-in Customer" ? QString() : found->customer);
    m_contact->setText(found->contact == "-" ? QString() : found->contact);
    m_sessionId->setText("#" + orderId + "  (editing)");
    setPreview(std::nullopt);
    rebuildTable();
    focusInput();
    QString msg = "Editing order #" + orderId;
    if (!swapped.isEmpty())
        msg += "  (previous order held as " + swapped + ")";
    emit toast(msg);
}

FILE: /Frontend/src/ui/ReportsPage.cpp
