#include "ReportsPage.h"
#include "AppState.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiKit.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QDateEdit>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHeaderView>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QMap>
#include <QPageSize>
#include <QPdfWriter>
#include <QProgressBar>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QScrollArea>
#include <QShortcut>
#include <QTableWidget>
#include <QTextDocument>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace Ui; // makeTable, item, wrapW, sectionHeader, kbd, writeCsv, csvField (see UiKit.h)

namespace
{
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- data helpers (no widgets)
    bool isCompleted(const OrderRecord &o) { return o.status == "Paid" || o.status == "Archived"; }
    bool isCash(const OrderRecord &o) { return o.status == "Paid" && o.payment.compare("cash", Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0; }
    QString pct(double part, double whole) { return whole > 0 ? QString::number(part * 100.0 / whole, 'f', 1) + "%" : QString("0.0%"); }
    QString when(const QDateTime &t) { return t.toString("dd-MMM-yyyy HH:mm"); }
    QString customerOf(const OrderRecord &o) { return o.customer.isEmpty() ? QString("Walk-in Customer") : o.customer; }

    // Orders parked with F2 are incomplete orders too (they live in UiStore, not in Order.txt).
    QVector<OrderRecord> parkedAsOrders()
    {
        QVector<OrderRecord> out;
        for (const auto &p : UiStore::instance().parked())
        {
            OrderRecord h;
            h.id = p.code;
            h.time = p.parkedAt;
            h.customer = p.customer;
            h.contact = p.contact;
            h.status = "On Hold";
            h.lines = p.lines;
            h.recompute();
            out << h;
        }
        return out;
    }

    // One product's sales over all completed orders.
    struct Sold
    {
        QString name, acronym;
        int productId = 0;
        int qty = 0;
        double revenue = 0; // (price - discount) * qty, without tax
    };

    QVector<Sold> soldTotals()
    {
        QMap<QString, Sold> byKey;
        for (const auto &o : AppState::instance().orders())
        {
            if (!isCompleted(o))
                continue;
            for (const auto &l : o.lines)
            {
                const QString key = l.productId > 0 ? QString("#%1").arg(l.productId) : "n:" + l.name;
                Sold &s = byKey[key];
                s.name = l.name;
                s.acronym = l.acronym;
                s.productId = l.productId;
                s.qty += l.qty;
                s.revenue += (l.price - l.discount) * l.qty;
            }
        }
        QVector<Sold> out;
        for (auto it = byKey.cbegin(); it != byKey.cend(); ++it)
            out.push_back(it.value());
        return out;
    }

    const ProductInfo *productOf(int productId)
    {
        if (productId <= 0)
            return nullptr;
        for (const auto &p : AppState::instance().products())
            if (p.id == productId)
                return &p;
        return nullptr;
    }

    QString stockText(int stock)
    {
        const int low = AppState::instance().settings().lowStockThreshold;
        if (stock <= 0)
            return "0  (depleted)";
        if (stock <= low)
            return QString("%1  (low)").arg(stock);
        return QString::number(stock);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- widget helpers
    // Summary tile with a caption that changes per report.
    QFrame *tile(const QString &icon, const QString &tint, QLabel *&cap, QLabel *&val, QLabel *&sub)
    {
        auto *c = W::card(nullptr);
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(c);
        h->setContentsMargins(16, 12, 16, 12);
        h->setSpacing(10);
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout;
        v->setSpacing(2);
        cap = W::label("", "caption");
        val = W::label("", "bigValue");
        sub = W::label("", "muted");
        v->addWidget(cap);
        v->addWidget(val);
        v->addWidget(sub);
        h->addLayout(v, 1);
        QString top = "#eaf1ff", bottom = "#d9e6ff";
        QColor fg = Theme::primary;
        if (tint == "green") { top = "#e2fbec"; bottom = "#c9f3d9"; fg = Theme::tertiary; }
        else if (tint == "amber") { top = "#fff4d6"; bottom = "#ffe8a8"; fg = QColor("#8a5a00"); }
        h->addWidget(W::iconTile(icon, fg, top, bottom, 38), 0, Qt::AlignTop);
        return c;
    }

    QWidget *barCell(double fraction)
    {
        auto *bar = new QProgressBar;
        bar->setRange(0, 1000);
        bar->setValue(int(qBound(0.0, fraction, 1.0) * 1000));
        bar->setTextVisible(false);
        bar->setFixedHeight(8);
        bar->setMinimumWidth(130);
        bar->setStyleSheet("QProgressBar{background:#dfe8fb;border:none;border-radius:4px;}"
                           "QProgressBar::chunk{background:#2554d8;border-radius:4px;}");
        return wrapW(bar);
    }

    QString htmlEscape(const QString &s) { return s.toHtmlEscaped(); }
}

// ======================================================================= page
ReportsPage::ReportsPage(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    setObjectName("page");
    auto *outer = new QVBoxLayout(this);
    outer->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    auto *scroll = new QScrollArea;
    scroll->setWidgetResizable(true);
    scroll->setFrameShape(QFrame::NoFrame);
    scroll->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
    auto *inner = new QWidget;
    inner->setMinimumHeight(860);
    scroll->setWidget(inner);
    outer->addWidget(scroll);
    auto *root = W::pageLayout(inner);

    // ---- header: title + exports
    auto *csv = W::button("Export CSV Data", "soft", "export");
    auto *pdf = W::button("Export / Print as PDF", "primary", "print");
    root->addWidget(sectionHeader("MANAGER REPORTS", "Business Reports & Analytics",
                                  "Audit orders, cash sales and product turnover. Every figure comes from the saved orders and products.",
                                  {csv, pdf, kbd("Ctrl+P")}));
    connect(csv, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ReportsPage::exportCsv);
    connect(pdf, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ReportsPage::printPdf);
    auto *printKey = new QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+P"), this);
    printKey->setContext(Qt::WidgetWithChildrenShortcut);
    connect(printKey, &QShortcut::activated, this, &ReportsPage::printPdf);

    // ---- the six report tabs
    auto *tabCard = W::card(this);
    auto *th = new QHBoxLayout(tabCard);
    th->setContentsMargins(12, 10, 12, 10);
    th->setSpacing(6);
    const QStringList names = {"Incomplete Orders", "Completed Orders", "Cash Sales", "All Sales", "Top 10 Sold",
                               "Top 10 Remaining Stock"};
    for (int i = 0; i < names.size(); ++i)
    {
        auto *b = W::button(names[i], "tab");
        b->setCheckable(true);
        b->setChecked(i == 0);
        th->addWidget(b);
        m_tabs << b;
        connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, i] { selectTab(i); });
    }
    th->addStretch();
    root->addWidget(tabCard);

    // ---- three summary tiles (captions change with the report)
    auto *tiles = new QHBoxLayout;
    tiles->setSpacing(0);
    const char *icons[3] = {"orders", "cash", "percent"};
    const char *tints[3] = {"blue", "green", "amber"};
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
        tiles->addWidget(tile(icons[i], tints[i], m_cap[i], m_val[i], m_sub[i]));
    root->addLayout(tiles);

    // ---- time period (Cash Sales only): Daily / Weekly / Monthly / Yearly or a custom range
    m_periodCard = W::card(this);
    auto *ph = new QHBoxLayout(m_periodCard);
    ph->setContentsMargins(16, 10, 16, 10);
    ph->setSpacing(8);
    ph->addWidget(W::label("TIME PERIOD", "caption"));
    for (const QString &name : {"Daily", "Weekly", "Monthly", "Yearly"})
    {
        auto *b = W::button(name, "tab");
        b->setCheckable(true);
        ph->addWidget(b);
        m_periods << b;
        connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, name] { selectPeriod(name); });
    }
    ph->addSpacing(18);
    ph->addWidget(W::label("CUSTOM RANGE", "caption"));
    m_from = new QDateEdit(QDate::currentDate());
    m_to = new QDateEdit(QDate::currentDate());
    for (QDateEdit *d : {m_from, m_to})
    {
        d->setCalendarPopup(true);
        d->setDisplayFormat("dd-MMM-yyyy");
        d->setMinimumHeight(34);
    }
    ph->addWidget(m_from);
    ph->addWidget(W::label("to", "muted"));
    ph->addWidget(m_to);
    auto *apply = W::button("Filter", "primary", "filter");
    ph->addWidget(apply);
    ph->addStretch();
    connect(apply, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ReportsPage::applyCustomRange);
    root->addWidget(m_periodCard);

    // ---- the report table
    auto *card = W::card(this);
    auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(card);
    v->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 14);
    v->setSpacing(8);
    auto *head = new QVBoxLayout;
    head->setSpacing(0);
    m_tableTitle = W::label("", "h1");
    m_tableSub = W::label("", "muted");
    head->addWidget(m_tableTitle);
    head->addWidget(m_tableSub);
    v->addLayout(head);
    m_table = makeTable({"-"});
    v->addWidget(m_table, 1);
    m_empty = W::label("", "muted");
    m_empty->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    m_empty->setMinimumHeight(70);
    v->addWidget(m_empty);

    auto *foot = new QFrame;
    foot->setObjectName("reportFooter");
    foot->setStyleSheet("#reportFooter{background:#0f1b33;border-radius:12px;}"
                        "#reportFooter QLabel{background:transparent;}");
    auto *fh = new QHBoxLayout(foot);
    fh->setContentsMargins(18, 12, 18, 12);
    fh->setSpacing(12);
    m_footLabel = new QLabel;
    m_footLabel->setStyleSheet("color:#c9d3ee;font-weight:700;");
    m_footValue = new QLabel;
    m_footValue->setStyleSheet("color:#5be3a0;font-size:20px;font-weight:800;");
    m_footExtra = new QLabel;
    m_footExtra->setStyleSheet("color:#c9d3ee;");
    fh->addWidget(m_footLabel);
    fh->addWidget(m_footValue);
    fh->addStretch();
    fh->addWidget(m_footExtra);
    v->addWidget(foot);
    root->addWidget(card, 1);

    selectPeriod("Daily"); // also renders
    selectTab(0);
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &ReportsPage::refresh);
    connect(&UiStore::instance(), &UiStore::parkedChanged, this, &ReportsPage::refresh);
}

void ReportsPage::refresh() { render(); }

void ReportsPage::selectTab(int index)
{
    m_tab = index;
    for (int i = 0; i < m_tabs.size(); ++i)
        m_tabs[i]->setChecked(i == index);
    m_periodCard->setVisible(index == 2);
    render();
}

void ReportsPage::selectPeriod(const QString &name)
{
    const QDate today = QDate::currentDate();
    m_rangeTo = today;
    if (name == "Daily")
        m_rangeFrom = today;
    else if (name == "Weekly")
        m_rangeFrom = today.addDays(-6);
    else if (name == "Monthly")
        m_rangeFrom = QDate(today.year(), today.month(), 1);
    else
        m_rangeFrom = QDate(today.year(), 1, 1);
    for (auto *b : m_periods)
        b->setChecked(b->text() == name);
    m_from->setDate(m_rangeFrom);
    m_to->setDate(m_rangeTo);
    render();
}

void ReportsPage::applyCustomRange()
{
    QDate a = m_from->date(), b = m_to->date();
    if (b < a)
        std::swap(a, b);
    m_rangeFrom = a;
    m_rangeTo = b;
    for (auto *p : m_periods)
        p->setChecked(false);
    render();
}

// ======================================================================= the six reports
// 1. Orders that are not paid or archived (in progress, on hold, parked), with the total of all such bills.
ReportsPage::Report ReportsPage::incomplete() const
{
    Report r;
    r.title = "Incomplete Orders";
    r.subtitle = "Orders that are not paid or archived";
    QVector<OrderRecord> list = parkedAsOrders();
    for (const auto &o : AppState::instance().orders())
        if (!isCompleted(o))
            list << o;
    std::sort(list.begin(), list.end(), [](const OrderRecord &a, const OrderRecord &b) { return a.time > b.time; });

    double total = 0;
    int hold = 0, progress = 0;
    r.headers = {"ORDER ID", "DATE & TIME", "CUSTOMER", "ITEMS", "STATUS", "AMOUNT"};
    r.rightCols = {5};
    r.statusCol = 4;
    for (const auto &o : list)
    {
        total += o.total;
        (o.status == "On Hold" ? hold : progress)++;
        r.rows << QStringList{"#" + o.id, when(o.time), customerOf(o), o.summary(), o.status, W::money(o.total)};
    }
    r.captions[0] = "INCOMPLETE ORDERS";
    r.values[0] = QString::number(list.size());
    r.subs[0] = "not paid or archived";
    r.captions[1] = "TOTAL AMOUNT PENDING";
    r.values[1] = W::money(total);
    r.subs[1] = "sum of all incomplete bills";
    r.captions[2] = "ON HOLD";
    r.values[2] = QString::number(hold);
    r.subs[2] = QString("%1 in progress").arg(progress);
    r.footerLabel = "Total of all incomplete orders:";
    r.footerValue = W::money(total);
    r.footerExtra = QString("%1 order%2").arg(list.size()).arg(list.size() == 1 ? "" : "s");
    r.emptyText = "No incomplete orders. Everything is paid or archived.";
    return r;
}

// 2. Orders that have been completed (paid or archived), with the total amount.
ReportsPage::Report ReportsPage::completed() const
{
    Report r;
    r.title = "Completed Orders";
    r.subtitle = "Paid and archived orders";
    double total = 0, discount = 0, gross = 0;
    int n = 0;
    r.headers = {"ORDER ID", "DATE & TIME", "CUSTOMER", "ITEMS", "PAYMENT", "STATUS", "AMOUNT"};
    r.rightCols = {6};
    r.statusCol = 5;
    for (const auto &o : AppState::instance().orders())
    {
        if (!isCompleted(o))
            continue;
        ++n;
        total += o.total;
        discount += o.discount;
        gross += o.subtotal;
        r.rows << QStringList{"#" + o.id, when(o.time), customerOf(o), o.summary(), o.payment, o.status, W::money(o.total)};
    }
    r.captions[0] = "COMPLETED ORDERS";
    r.values[0] = QString::number(n);
    r.subs[0] = "paid or archived";
    r.captions[1] = "TOTAL AMOUNT";
    r.values[1] = W::money(total);
    r.subs[1] = "sum of all completed bills";
    r.captions[2] = "DISCOUNTS GIVEN";
    r.values[2] = W::money(discount);
    r.subs[2] = QString("%1 of gross sales").arg(pct(discount, gross));
    r.footerLabel = "Total of all completed orders:";
    r.footerValue = W::money(total);
    r.footerExtra = QString("%1 order%2").arg(n).arg(n == 1 ? "" : "s");
    r.emptyText = "No completed orders yet.";
    return r;
}

// 3. Cash sales for the chosen period (daily, weekly, monthly, yearly or a specified time period).
ReportsPage::Report ReportsPage::cashSales() const
{
    Report r;
    r.title = "Cash Sales Ledger";
    r.subtitle = m_rangeFrom == m_rangeTo ? m_rangeFrom.toString("dd-MMM-yyyy")
                                          : m_rangeFrom.toString("dd-MMM-yyyy") + "  to  " + m_rangeTo.toString("dd-MMM-yyyy");
    double total = 0, tendered = 0, change = 0, discount = 0, gross = 0;
    int n = 0;
    r.headers = {"DATE & TIME", "ORDER ID", "CUSTOMER", "AMOUNT", "CASH RECEIVED", "BALANCE RETURNED", "DISCOUNT"};
    r.rightCols = {3, 4, 5, 6};
    for (const auto &o : AppState::instance().orders())
    {
        if (!isCash(o) || o.time.date() < m_rangeFrom || o.time.date() > m_rangeTo)
            continue;
        ++n;
        total += o.total;
        tendered += o.tendered;
        change += o.change();
        discount += o.discount;
        gross += o.subtotal;
        r.rows << QStringList{when(o.time), "#" + o.id, customerOf(o), W::money(o.total),
                              o.tendered > 0 ? W::money(o.tendered) : QString("-"),
                              o.tendered > 0 ? W::money(o.change()) : QString("-"), W::money(o.discount)};
    }
    r.captions[0] = "TOTAL CASH SALES";
    r.values[0] = W::money(total);
    r.subs[0] = QString("%1 cash order%2").arg(n).arg(n == 1 ? "" : "s");
    r.captions[1] = "CASH TENDER RECEIVED";
    r.values[1] = W::money(tendered);
    r.subs[1] = "balance returned " + W::money(change);
    r.captions[2] = "DISCOUNTS GRANTED";
    r.values[2] = W::money(discount);
    r.subs[2] = QString("avg %1 per order").arg(pct(discount, gross));
    r.footerLabel = "Cash sales in this period:";
    r.footerValue = W::money(total);
    r.footerExtra = QString("Discounts given %1  •  %2 transaction%3").arg(W::money(discount)).arg(n).arg(n == 1 ? "" : "s");
    r.emptyText = "No cash sales in this period.";
    return r;
}

// 4. All sales with the total amount and the discounts.
ReportsPage::Report ReportsPage::allSales() const
{
    Report r;
    r.title = "All Sales";
    r.subtitle = "Every completed sale with its discount and tax";
    double total = 0, discount = 0, gross = 0, tax = 0;
    int n = 0;
    r.headers = {"ORDER ID", "DATE & TIME", "CUSTOMER", "PAYMENT", "GROSS", "DISCOUNT", "TAX", "TOTAL"};
    r.rightCols = {4, 5, 6, 7};
    for (const auto &o : AppState::instance().orders())
    {
        if (!isCompleted(o))
            continue;
        ++n;
        total += o.total;
        discount += o.discount;
        gross += o.subtotal;
        tax += o.tax;
        r.rows << QStringList{"#" + o.id, when(o.time), customerOf(o), o.payment, W::money(o.subtotal),
                              W::money(o.discount), W::money(o.tax), W::money(o.total)};
    }
    r.captions[0] = "TOTAL SALES";
    r.values[0] = W::money(total);
    r.subs[0] = QString("%1 sale%2").arg(n).arg(n == 1 ? "" : "s");
    r.captions[1] = "TOTAL DISCOUNTS";
    r.values[1] = W::money(discount);
    r.subs[1] = QString("%1 of gross sales").arg(pct(discount, gross));
    r.captions[2] = "TAX COLLECTED";
    r.values[2] = W::money(tax);
    r.subs[2] = "included in the totals";
    r.footerLabel = "Total of all sales:";
    r.footerValue = W::money(total);
    r.footerExtra = QString("Discounts given %1").arg(W::money(discount));
    r.emptyText = "No sales recorded yet.";
    return r;
}

// 5. Ten most sold items in descending order, with their remaining stock.
ReportsPage::Report ReportsPage::topSold() const
{
    Report r;
    r.title = "Top 10 Most Sold Items";
    r.subtitle = "Descending by units sold, with remaining stock";
    QVector<Sold> sold = soldTotals();
    std::sort(sold.begin(), sold.end(), [](const Sold &a, const Sold &b) { return a.qty > b.qty; });
    if (sold.size() > 10)
        sold.resize(10);

    int units = 0, restock = 0;
    const int most = sold.isEmpty() ? 0 : sold.first().qty;
    const int low = AppState::instance().settings().lowStockThreshold;
    r.headers = {"#", "PRODUCT", "ACRONYM", "UNITS SOLD", "REVENUE", "REMAINING STOCK", "SALES VOLUME"};
    r.rightCols = {3, 4, 5};
    r.barCol = 6;
    for (int i = 0; i < sold.size(); ++i)
    {
        const Sold &s = sold[i];
        units += s.qty;
        const ProductInfo *p = productOf(s.productId);
        if (p && p->stock <= low)
            ++restock;
        r.rows << QStringList{QString::number(i + 1), s.name, s.acronym, QString::number(s.qty), W::money(s.revenue),
                              p ? stockText(p->stock) : QString("-"), QString()};
        r.bars << (most > 0 ? double(s.qty) / most : 0.0);
    }
    r.captions[0] = "UNITS SOLD (TOP 10)";
    r.values[0] = QString::number(units);
    r.subs[0] = QString("across %1 product%2").arg(sold.size()).arg(sold.size() == 1 ? "" : "s");
    r.captions[1] = "BEST SELLER";
    r.values[1] = sold.isEmpty() ? QString("-") : sold.first().acronym;
    r.subs[1] = sold.isEmpty() ? QString("no sales yet") : QString("%1 - %2 units").arg(sold.first().name).arg(sold.first().qty);
    r.captions[2] = "NEEDS RESTOCK";
    r.values[2] = QString::number(restock);
    r.subs[2] = "top sellers that are low or depleted";
    r.footerLabel = "Units sold by the top 10:";
    r.footerValue = QString::number(units);
    r.footerExtra = restock > 0 ? QString("%1 need restocking").arg(restock) : QString("stock levels healthy");
    r.emptyText = "No sales yet, so there is no ranking.";
    return r;
}

// 6. Ten items with the most remaining stock, descending, so their purchase is not started.
ReportsPage::Report ReportsPage::topStock() const
{
    Report r;
    r.title = "Top 10 Items by Remaining Stock";
    r.subtitle = "Descending by quantity on hand - do not start purchasing these";
    QVector<ProductInfo> list = AppState::instance().products();
    std::sort(list.begin(), list.end(), [](const ProductInfo &a, const ProductInfo &b) { return a.stock > b.stock; });
    if (list.size() > 10)
        list.resize(10);

    QMap<int, int> soldQty;
    for (const auto &s : soldTotals())
        soldQty[s.productId] = s.qty;

    int units = 0;
    double value = 0;
    const int most = list.isEmpty() ? 0 : list.first().stock;
    r.headers = {"#", "PRODUCT", "ACRONYM", "CATEGORY", "REMAINING STOCK", "UNITS SOLD", "STOCK LEVEL"};
    r.rightCols = {4, 5};
    r.barCol = 6;
    for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); ++i)
    {
        const ProductInfo &p = list[i];
        units += p.stock;
        value += p.stock * p.price;
        const Category *c = UiStore::instance().category(p.ean.left(2));
        r.rows << QStringList{QString::number(i + 1), p.name, p.sku, c ? c->name : p.ean.left(2),
                              QString::number(p.stock), QString::number(soldQty.value(p.id, 0)), QString()};
        r.bars << (most > 0 ? double(p.stock) / most : 0.0);
    }
    r.captions[0] = "UNITS IN TOP 10";
    r.values[0] = QString::number(units);
    r.subs[0] = "quantity on hand";
    r.captions[1] = "HIGHEST STOCK";
    r.values[1] = list.isEmpty() ? QString("-") : list.first().sku;
    r.subs[1] = list.isEmpty() ? QString("no products") : QString("%1 - %2 units").arg(list.first().name).arg(list.first().stock);
    r.captions[2] = "STOCK VALUE";
    r.values[2] = W::money(value);
    r.subs[2] = "at unit price, without tax";
    r.footerLabel = "Units in stock (top 10):";
    r.footerValue = QString::number(units);
    r.footerExtra = "Hold purchase orders for these items";
    r.emptyText = "No products in Product.txt.";
    return r;
}

// ======================================================================= drawing
void ReportsPage::render()
{
    if (!m_table)
        return;
    const Builder builders[6] = {&ReportsPage::incomplete, &ReportsPage::completed, &ReportsPage::cashSales,
                                 &ReportsPage::allSales,   &ReportsPage::topSold,   &ReportsPage::topStock};
    m_current = (this->*builders[qBound(0, m_tab, 5)])();
    const Report &r = m_current;

    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
    {
        m_cap[i]->setText(r.captions[i]);
        m_val[i]->setText(r.values[i]);
        m_sub[i]->setText(r.subs[i]);
    }
    m_tableTitle->setText(r.title);
    m_tableSub->setText(r.subtitle);

    m_table->clear();
    m_table->setColumnCount(r.headers.size());
    m_table->setHorizontalHeaderLabels(r.headers);
    m_table->setRowCount(r.rows.size());
    auto *hh = m_table->horizontalHeader();
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView::Stretch);
    if (r.barCol >= 0)
    {
        hh->setSectionResizeMode(r.barCol, QHeaderView::Fixed);
        m_table->setColumnWidth(r.barCol, 190);
    }
    const bool orderIdFirst = r.headers.value(0).startsWith("ORDER");
    for (int row = 0; row < r.rows.size(); ++row)
    {
        m_table->setRowHeight(row, 48);
        const QStringList &cells = r.rows[row];
        for (int c = 0; c < cells.size(); ++c)
        {
            if (c == r.statusCol)
                m_table->setCellWidget(row, c, wrapW(W::badge(cells[c], W::statusBadgeKind(cells[c]))));
            else if (c == r.barCol)
                m_table->setCellWidget(row, c, barCell(r.bars.value(row)));
            else
            {
                const Qt::Alignment a = (r.rightCols.contains(c) ? Qt::AlignRight : Qt::AlignLeft) | Qt::AlignVCenter;
                const bool firstOrderId = c == 0 && orderIdFirst;
                const bool amount = c == r.headers.size() - 1 && r.barCol < 0;
                m_table->setItem(row, c, item(cells[c], a, firstOrderId ? "#004ac6" : "", firstOrderId || amount));
            }
        }
    }

    const bool empty = r.rows.isEmpty();
    m_table->setVisible(!empty);
    m_empty->setVisible(empty);
    m_empty->setText(r.emptyText);
    m_footLabel->setText(r.footerLabel);
    m_footValue->setText(r.footerValue);
    m_footExtra->setText(r.footerExtra);
    m_footLabel->parentWidget()->setVisible(!empty);
}

// ======================================================================= exports
void ReportsPage::exportCsv()
{
    if (m_current.rows.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast("Nothing to export - this report is empty");
        return;
    }
    QStringList lines;
    QStringList head;
    for (const QString &h : m_current.headers)
        head << csvField(h);
    lines << head.join(',');
    for (const QStringList &row : m_current.rows)
    {
        QStringList cells;
        for (const QString &c : row)
            cells << csvField(c);
        lines << cells.join(',');
    }
    QString slug = m_current.title.toLower();
    slug.replace(' ', '-');
    if (writeCsv(this, "report-" + slug + ".csv", lines))
        emit toast(m_current.title + " exported");
}

void ReportsPage::printPdf()
{
    if (m_current.rows.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast("Nothing to print - this report is empty");
        return;
    }
    QString slug = m_current.title.toLower();
    slug.replace(' ', '-');
    const QString path = QFileDialog::getSaveFileName(this, "Save report as PDF", "report-" + slug + ".pdf", "PDF (*.pdf)");
    if (path.isEmpty())
        return;

    QString html = "<h2 style='color:#0f1b33'>" + htmlEscape(m_current.title) + "</h2><p style='color:#64708a'>" +
                   htmlEscape(m_current.subtitle) + "  -  printed " + QDateTime::currentDateTime().toString("dd-MMM-yyyy HH:mm") +
                   "</p><table width='100%' cellspacing='0' cellpadding='5' style='border-collapse:collapse'><tr>";
    for (int c = 0; c < m_current.headers.size(); ++c)
        html += QString("<th align='%1' style='background:#eaf1ff;border-bottom:1px solid #c3d5ff'>%2</th>")
                    .arg(m_current.rightCols.contains(c) ? "right" : "left", htmlEscape(m_current.headers[c]));
    html += "</tr>";
    for (const QStringList &row : m_current.rows)
    {
        html += "<tr>";
        for (int c = 0; c < row.size(); ++c)
            html += QString("<td align='%1' style='border-bottom:1px solid #e3eaf8'>%2</td>")
                        .arg(m_current.rightCols.contains(c) ? "right" : "left", htmlEscape(row[c]));
        html += "</tr>";
    }
    html += "</table><p><b>" + htmlEscape(m_current.footerLabel) + " " + htmlEscape(m_current.footerValue) + "</b>  " +
            htmlEscape(m_current.footerExtra) + "</p>";

    QPdfWriter pdf(path);
    pdf.setPageSize(QPageSize(QPageSize::A4));
    pdf.setPageMargins(QMarginsF(12, 12, 12, 12), QPageLayout::Millimeter);
    pdf.setTitle(m_current.title);
    pdf.setCreator("Syntro-Check");
    QTextDocument doc;
    doc.setHtml(html);
    doc.print(&pdf);
    emit toast(QFileInfo(path).exists() ? m_current.title + " saved as PDF" : QString("The PDF could not be written"));
}

#include "ScannerPage.h"
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiKit.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QApplication>
#include <QCheckBox>
#include <QDoubleSpinBox>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QFormLayout>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHeaderView>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QListWidget>
#include <QSpinBox>
#include <QTableWidget>
#include <QTextStream>
#include <QTime>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

using namespace Ui; // makeTable, item, wrapW, pageShell, writeCsv (see UiKit.h)


// =================================================================== ScannerPage
ScannerPage::ScannerPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root;
    pageShell(this, root);
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout;
    h->setSpacing(12);

    auto* cam = W::card(this);
    auto* cv = new QVBoxLayout(cam);
    cv->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 14);
    cv->setSpacing(10);
    W::headerRow(cv, "barcode", "Barcode Scanner");
    m_feed = new CameraFeedWidget;
    m_feed->setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Expanding);
    m_feed->setMinimumHeight(300);
    cv->addWidget(m_feed, 1);
    auto* btns = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_toggle = W::button("Start camera", "primary", "play");
    btns->addWidget(m_toggle);
    btns->addStretch();
    cv->addLayout(btns);
    auto* mr = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_manual = new QLineEdit;
    m_manual->setPlaceholderText("Or type an 8-digit EAN and press Enter");
    m_manual->setMaxLength(12);
    auto* go = W::button("Lookup", "soft", "search");
    mr->addWidget(m_manual, 1);
    mr->addWidget(go);
    cv->addLayout(mr);
    h->addWidget(cam, 6);

    auto* side = W::card(this);
    auto* sv = new QVBoxLayout(side);
    sv->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 14);
    sv->setSpacing(10);
    W::headerRow(sv, "clock", "Recent Scans");
    m_last = W::label("No scans yet", "h2");
    sv->addWidget(m_last);
    m_history = new QListWidget;
    m_history->setStyleSheet("QListWidget{border:1px solid #dbe4f7;border-radius:10px;padding:4px;} QListWidget::item{padding:9px;border-bottom:1px solid #e8eefb;}");
    sv->addWidget(m_history, 1);
    m_describe = W::button("Show description", "soft", "search");
    m_describe->setEnabled(false);
    sv->addWidget(m_describe);
    m_send = W::button("Add last scan to order", "primary", "cart");
    m_send->setEnabled(false);
    sv->addWidget(m_send);
    h->addWidget(side, 4);
    root->addLayout(h, 1);

    connect(m_toggle, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        {
            const bool on = !m_feed->running();
            m_feed->setRunning(on);
            const bool running = m_feed->running();
            m_toggle->setText(running ? "Stop camera" : "Start camera");
            m_toggle->setIcon(Theme::icon(running ? "stop" : "play", QColor("white"), 16)); });
    connect(m_feed, &CameraFeedWidget::scanError, this, &ScannerPage::toast);
    connect(m_feed, &CameraFeedWidget::barcodeDetected, this, [this](const QString& code) {
        const auto product = AppState::instance().findByCode(code);
        if (product) { m_feed->showResult(code + "  -  " + product->name, true); show(*product); }
        else { m_feed->showResult(code + "  -  not in Product.txt", false); emit toast("Barcode " + code + " not found in Product.txt"); }
        });
    auto lookup = [this]
        {
            const auto p = AppState::instance().findByCode(m_manual->text());
            if (!p)
            {
                emit toast("No product found for \"" + m_manual->text() + "\"");
                return;
            }
            show(*p);
            m_manual->clear();
        };
    connect(go, &QPushButton::clicked, this, lookup);
    connect(m_manual, &QLineEdit::returnPressed, this, lookup);
    connect(m_send, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        { emit sendToOrder(m_lastEan); });
    connect(m_describe, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        { Dialogs::productDescription(this, m_lastId); });
}

void ScannerPage::scanNext()
{
    if (!m_feed->running()) m_feed->setRunning(true);
    if (!m_feed->running()) emit toast("Unable to start camera scanning");
    else {
        m_toggle->setText("Stop camera");
        m_toggle->setIcon(Theme::icon("stop", QColor("white"), 16));
    }
}

void ScannerPage::show(const ProductInfo& p)
{
    m_feed->setCode(p.ean);
    m_lastEan = p.ean;
    m_lastId = p.id;
    m_last->setText(p.name + " • " + W::money(p.price));
    m_history->insertItem(0, QString("%1   %2   %3").arg(QTime::currentTime().toString("HH:mm:ss"), p.ean, p.name));
    m_send->setEnabled(true);
    m_describe->setEnabled(true);
    // Show description only on request; opening a modal on every camera frame is disruptive.
}
void ScannerPage::setViewOnly(bool on)
{
    m_send->setVisible(!on);
}

#include "SettingsPage.h"
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiKit.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QApplication>
#include <QCheckBox>
#include <QDoubleSpinBox>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QFormLayout>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHeaderView>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QListWidget>
#include <QSpinBox>
#include <QTableWidget>
#include <QTextStream>
#include <QTime>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

using namespace Ui; // makeTable, item, wrapW, pageShell, writeCsv (see UiKit.h)


// =================================================================== SettingsPage
SettingsPage::SettingsPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root;
    pageShell(this, root);
    auto* card = W::card(this);
    card->setMaximumWidth(620);
    auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(card);
    v->setContentsMargins(20, 16, 20, 16);
    v->setSpacing(14);
    W::headerRow(v, "settings", "Terminal Settings");
    auto& s = AppState::instance().settings();

    auto* form = new QFormLayout;
    form->setHorizontalSpacing(20);
    form->setVerticalSpacing(12);
    auto* low = new QSpinBox;
    low->setRange(0, 1000);
    low->setSuffix(" units");
    low->setValue(s.lowStockThreshold);
    auto* autoAdd = new QCheckBox("Add scanned items to the order automatically");
    autoAdd->setChecked(s.autoAddScans);
    auto* beep = new QCheckBox("Play a beep on successful scan");
    beep->setChecked(s.beepOnScan);
    form->addRow("Low-stock limit", low);
    form->addRow("Scanning", autoAdd);
    form->addRow("", beep);
    v->addLayout(form);
    v->addWidget(W::label("Tax and discount come per product from Product.txt. Use the Dark Mode button at the top to switch themes.", "muted"));
    v->addStretch();
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout;
    h->addStretch();
    auto* save = W::button("Apply", "primary", "check");
    h->addWidget(save);
    v->addLayout(h);
    connect(save, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [=, this]
        {
            auto& st = AppState::instance().settings();
            st.lowStockThreshold = low->value();
            AppState::instance().emitChanged();
            st.autoAddScans = autoAdd->isChecked();
            st.beepOnScan = beep->isChecked();
            emit toast("Settings applied"); });
    root->addWidget(card);
    root->addStretch();
}

#include "Theme.h"
#include <QPainter>
#include <cmath>
#include <QRegularExpression>
#include <QSettings>
#include <QPainterPath>
#include <QStyle>
#include <QWidget>

void Theme::repolish(QWidget *w)
{
    w->style()->unpolish(w);
    w->style()->polish(w);
    w->update();
}

QIcon Theme::icon(const QString &name, const QColor &color, int px)
{
    return QIcon(iconPixmap(name, color, px));
}

QPixmap Theme::iconPixmap(const QString &n, const QColor &c, int px)
{
    const qreal dpr = 2.0;
    QPixmap pm(int(px * dpr), int(px * dpr));
    pm.setDevicePixelRatio(dpr);
    pm.fill(Qt::transparent);
    QPainter p(&pm);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    p.scale(px / 24.0, px / 24.0);
    p.setPen(QPen(c, 1.9, Qt::SolidLine, Qt::RoundCap, Qt::RoundJoin));
    p.setBrush(Qt::NoBrush);

    auto line = [&](qreal x1, qreal y1, qreal x2, qreal y2)
    { p.drawLine(QPointF(x1, y1), QPointF(x2, y2)); };
    auto rect = [&](qreal x, qreal y, qreal w, qreal h, qreal r = 2)
    { p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(x, y, w, h), r, r); };
    auto circle = [&](qreal x, qreal y, qreal r)
    { p.drawEllipse(QPointF(x, y), r, r); };
    auto fillShape = [&](auto fn)
    {
        p.save();
        p.setBrush(c);
        fn();
        p.restore();
    };

    if (n == "dashboard")
    {
        rect(3, 3, 7.5, 7.5);
        rect(13.5, 3, 7.5, 7.5);
        rect(3, 13.5, 7.5, 7.5);
        rect(13.5, 13.5, 7.5, 7.5);
    }
    else if (n == "cart")
    {
        QPainterPath path;
        path.moveTo(2, 3.5);
        path.lineTo(5, 3.5);
        path.lineTo(7.2, 15);
        path.lineTo(18.5, 15);
        path.lineTo(20.5, 7);
        path.lineTo(5.6, 7);
        p.drawPath(path);
        circle(9.5, 19.3, 1.5);
        circle(16.5, 19.3, 1.5);
    }
    else if (n == "orders")
    {
        rect(5, 3, 14, 18, 2);
        line(8.5, 8, 15.5, 8);
        line(8.5, 12, 15.5, 12);
        line(8.5, 16, 12.5, 16);
    }
    else if (n == "products")
    {
        rect(3, 4, 18, 5, 1.5);
        rect(4.5, 9, 15, 11, 1.5);
        line(10, 13, 14, 13);
    }
    else if (n == "barcode")
    {
        p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
        p.setBrush(c);
        const qreal xs[] = {3, 5.5, 8, 10, 13, 15, 17.5, 20};
        const qreal ws[] = {1.4, 0.9, 1.8, 0.9, 1.4, 1.8, 0.9, 1.2};
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
            p.drawRect(QRectF(xs[i], 4, ws[i], 16));
    }
    else if (n == "bills")
    {
        QPainterPath path;
        path.moveTo(6, 3);
        path.lineTo(14, 3);
        path.lineTo(19, 8);
        path.lineTo(19, 21);
        path.lineTo(6, 21);
        path.closeSubpath();
        p.drawPath(path);
        line(14, 3, 14, 8);
        line(14, 8, 19, 8);
        line(9, 13, 16, 13);
        line(9, 17, 14, 17);
    }
    else if (n == "settings")
    {
        circle(12, 12, 3);
        circle(12, 12, 7);
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
        {
            p.save();
            p.translate(12, 12);
            p.rotate(i * 45);
            line(0, -7, 0, -10);
            p.restore();
        }
    }
    else if (n == "search")
    {
        circle(10.5, 10.5, 6);
        line(15, 15, 20.5, 20.5);
    }
    else if (n == "trash")
    {
        line(4, 6, 20, 6);
        line(9.5, 3.5, 14.5, 3.5);
        QPainterPath path;
        path.moveTo(6, 6);
        path.lineTo(7, 20);
        path.lineTo(17, 20);
        path.lineTo(18, 6);
        p.drawPath(path);
        line(10, 10, 10, 16);
        line(14, 10, 14, 16);
    }
    else if (n == "plus")
    {
        line(12, 5, 12, 19);
        line(5, 12, 19, 12);
    }
    else if (n == "minus")
    {
        line(5, 12, 19, 12);
    }
    else if (n == "play")
    {
        fillShape([&]
                  { p.drawPolygon(QPolygonF({{7, 4.5}, {19, 12}, {7, 19.5}})); });
    }
    else if (n == "pause")
    {
        line(9, 5, 9, 19);
        line(15, 5, 15, 19);
    }
    else if (n == "stop")
    {
        rect(6, 6, 12, 12, 1.5);
    }
    else if (n == "check")
    {
        circle(12, 12, 9);
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{7.8, 12.3}, {10.7, 15.2}, {16.2, 9.2}}));
    }
    else if (n == "warn")
    {
        p.drawPolygon(QPolygonF({{12, 3.5}, {21.5, 20}, {2.5, 20}}));
        line(12, 9.5, 12, 14);
        line(12, 17, 12, 17.2);
    }
    else if (n == "lock")
    {
        rect(5, 11, 14, 10, 2);
        p.drawArc(QRectF(8, 3, 8, 12), 0, 180 * 16);
    }
    else if (n == "wifi")
    {
        p.drawArc(QRectF(2, 4, 20, 20), 50 * 16, 80 * 16);
        p.drawArc(QRectF(6, 8, 12, 12), 45 * 16, 90 * 16);
        fillShape([&]
                  { circle(12, 18.5, 1.3); });
    }
    else if (n == "clock")
    {
        circle(12, 12, 9);
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{12, 7}, {12, 12}, {15.5, 14}}));
    }
    else if (n == "user")

        circle(12, 8, 4);

        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{4.5, 15}, {4.5, 20}, {19.5, 20}, {19.5, 15}}));
    }
    else if (n == "refresh")

        path.closeSubpath();
        p.drawPath(path);
        line(8, 9.5, 16, 9.5);
        line(8, 12.5, 13, 12.5);
    }
    else if (n == "bolt")
    {
        fillShape([&]
                  { p.drawPolygon(QPolygonF({{13.5, 2.5}, {5.5, 13.5}, {11, 13.5}, {10, 21.5}, {18.5, 10}, {13, 10}})); });
    }
    else if (n == "qr")
    {
        rect(3.5, 3.5, 6, 6, 1);
        rect(14.5, 3.5, 6, 6, 1);
        rect(3.5, 14.5, 6, 6, 1);
        line(14.5, 14.5, 14.5, 14.7);
        line(18, 14.5, 20.5, 14.5);
        line(14.5, 18, 14.5, 20.5);
        line(18, 18, 20.5, 20.5);
    }
    else if (n == "calendar")
    {
        rect(3.5, 5, 17, 15.5, 2.5);
        line(3.5, 10, 20.5, 10);
        line(8, 3, 8, 7);
        line(16, 3, 16, 7);
    }
    else if (n == "wallet")
    {
        rect(3, 6, 18, 13, 2.5);
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{3.5, 9.5}, {17, 9.5}}));
        circle(16.5, 13.5, 1.2);
    }
    else if (n == "idcard")
    {
        rect(3, 5.5, 18, 13, 2);
        circle(8.5, 11.5, 2);
        line(13, 10.5, 18, 10.5);
        line(13, 14, 17, 14);
    }
    else if (n == "undo")
    {
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{8, 6}, {4, 10}, {8, 14}}));
        QPainterPath path;
        path.moveTo(4.5, 10);
        path.lineTo(14, 10);
        path.cubicTo(19, 10, 19.5, 18, 14, 18);
        path.lineTo(9, 18);
        p.drawPath(path);
    }
    else if (n == "star")
    {
        QPolygonF st;
        for (int i = 0; i < 10; ++i)
        {
            const qreal r = i % 2 == 0 ? 9 : 4;
            const qreal a = -M_PI / 2 + i * M_PI / 5;
            st << QPointF(12 + r * std::cos(a), 12.5 + r * std::sin(a));
        }
        p.drawPolygon(st);
    }
    else if (n == "drawer")
    {
        rect(3, 9, 18, 10, 2);
        line(3, 13, 21, 13);
        line(10, 16, 14, 16);
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{6, 9}, {8, 4}, {16, 4}, {18, 9}}));
    }
    else if (n == "building")
    {
        rect(5, 4, 14, 17, 1.5);
        line(9, 8, 9, 8.2);
        line(15, 8, 15, 8.2);
        line(9, 12, 9, 12.2);
        line(15, 12, 15, 12.2);
        line(10, 21, 10, 17);
        line(14, 21, 14, 17);
    }
    else if (n == "sun")
    {
        circle(12, 12, 4);
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
        {
            p.save();
            p.translate(12, 12);
            p.rotate(i * 45);
            line(0, -7, 0, -9.5);
            p.restore();
        }
    }
    else if (n == "scan")
    {
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{4, 9}, {4, 4}, {9, 4}}));
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{15, 4}, {20, 4}, {20, 9}}));
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{20, 15}, {20, 20}, {15, 20}}));
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{9, 20}, {4, 20}, {4, 15}}));
        line(7.5, 12, 16.5, 12);
    }
    return QPixmap(pm);
}

namespace
{
    int g_dark = -1;
}

bool Theme::isDark()
{
    if (g_dark < 0)
        g_dark = QSettings("BarcodeHub", "SmartPOS").value("darkMode", false).toBool() ? 1 : 0;
    return g_dark == 1;
}

void Theme::setDark(bool on)
{
    g_dark = on ? 1 : 0;
    QSettings("BarcodeHub", "SmartPOS").setValue("darkMode", on);
}

QColor Theme::readable(const QColor &c)
{
    // In dark mode a dark colour (e.g. dark blue text) would vanish on the dark cards:
    // keep its hue but raise its lightness.
    if (!isDark() || c.lightnessF() >= 0.6)
        return c;
    return QColor::fromHslF(c.hslHueF() < 0 ? 0 : c.hslHueF(), c.hslSaturationF(), 0.72);
}

QString Theme::styleSheet()
{
    QString s = lightSheet();
    if (!isDark())
        return s;
    // Dark mode: swap the light palette for dark equivalents (order matters: specific first).
    const char *map[][2] = {
        {"#0f1b33", "#e6ecfb"}, {"#1b2a4d", "#dbe4fa"}, {"#f4f7fe", "#141b2e"}, {"#e8eefb", "#0f1524"},
        {"#eaf0fc", "#0f1524"}, {"#f3f6fe", "#18213a"}, {"#eef3ff", "#1d2742"}, {"#f6f9ff", "#1a2340"},
        {"#edf2fd", "#161e36"}, {"#f1f5fd", "#141b2e"}, {"#eef3fe", "#1d2742"}, {"#e4ecfd", "#26324f"},
        {"#e3eafb", "#26324f"}, {"#f3f6fc", "#1a2238"}, {"#eaf1ff", "#22304f"}, {"#d9e6ff", "#1b2845"},
        {"#e6eefe", "#26324f"}, {"#e0eaff", "#22304f"}, {"#e5eeff", "#22304f"}, {"#ffffff", "#1c2540"},
        {"white", "#1c2540"}, {"#dbe4f7", "#2a3556"}, {"#d3ddf3", "#2f3b5e"}, {"#d9e3f8", "#2f3b5e"},
        {"#dfe7f8", "#2a3556"}, {"#cbd8f2", "#26324f"}, {"#bcc9e8", "#232d4a"}, {"#c3d0ee", "#2f3b5e"},
        {"#c9d8fb", "#2f3b5e"}, {"#aebfee", "#232d4a"}, {"#b9c9ee", "#232d4a"}, {"#c6d3ee", "#232d4a"},
        {"#e8eefb", "#243050"}, {"#64708a", "#9aa7c7"}, {"#7a86a0", "#8795b8"}, {"#55617c", "#a9b6d6"},
        {"#1d46b4", "#8fb2ff"}, {"#004ac6", "#8fb2ff"}, {"#2554d8", "#8fb2ff"}, {"#dce9ff", "#2a3556"},
        {"#c3cfea", "#3a4770"}, {"#a3b3d8", "#4a5a8a"}, {"#a3adc5", "#5d6a8f"}, {"#8b96ad", "#6a7799"}};
    s.replace(QRegularExpression("color:\\s*(#ffffff|white)"), "color: @KEEPWHITE@");
    for (const auto &m : map)
        s.replace(QString::fromLatin1(m[0]), QString::fromLatin1(m[1]));
    s.replace("@KEEPWHITE@", "#ffffff");
    return s;
}

#include "UiKit.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QEvent>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHeaderView>
#include <QShowEvent>
#include <QTableWidget>
#include <QTextStream>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace Ui
{
    QTableWidget *makeTable(const QStringList &headers)
    {
        auto *t = new QTableWidget(0, headers.size());
        t->setHorizontalHeaderLabels(headers);
        t->verticalHeader()->hide();
        t->setShowGrid(false);
        t->setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView::NoEditTriggers);
        t->setSelectionBehavior(QAbstractItemView::SelectRows);
        t->setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView::SingleSelection);
        t->setFocusPolicy(Qt::NoFocus);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView::Stretch);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setHighlightSections(false);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setDefaultAlignment(Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter);
        t->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
        return t;
    }

    QTableWidgetItem *item(const QString &s, Qt::Alignment a, const QString &color, bool bold)
    {
        auto *i = new QTableWidgetItem(s);
        i->setTextAlignment(a);
        if (!color.isEmpty())
            i->setForeground(Theme::readable(QColor(color)));
        if (bold)
        {
            QFont f = i->font();
            f.setBold(true);
            i->setFont(f);
        }
        return i;
    }

    QWidget *wrapW(QWidget *w, Qt::Alignment a)
    {
        auto *c = new QWidget;
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(c);
        h->setContentsMargins(6, 0, 6, 0);
        h->setAlignment(a | Qt::AlignVCenter);
        h->addWidget(w);
        return c;
    }

    QString csvField(const QString &s)
    {
        if (!s.contains(',') && !s.contains('"') && !s.contains('\n'))
            return s;
        QString q = s;
        q.replace('"', "\"\"");
        return '"' + q + '"';
    }

    bool writeCsv(QWidget *parent, const QString &suggested, const QStringList &rows)
    {
        const QString path = QFileDialog::getSaveFileName(parent, "Export CSV", suggested, "CSV (*.csv)");
        if (path.isEmpty())
            return false;
        QFile f(path);
        if (!f.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Text))
            return false;
        QTextStream out(&f);
        for (const auto &r : rows)
            out << r << '\n';
        return true;
    }

    QWidget *pageShell(QWidget *page, QVBoxLayout *&body)
    {
        body = W::pageLayout(page);
        return page;
    }

    QWidget *sectionHeader(const QString &eyebrow, const QString &title, const QString &subtitle,
                           const QList<QWidget *> &actions)
    {
        auto *w = new QWidget;
        w->setAttribute(Qt::WA_StyledBackground, false);
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(w);
        h->setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4);
        h->setSpacing(10);
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout;
        v->setSpacing(1);
        if (!eyebrow.isEmpty())
            v->addWidget(W::label(eyebrow, "eyebrow"));
        v->addWidget(W::label(title, "pageH1"));
        if (!subtitle.isEmpty())
        {
            auto *s = W::label(subtitle, "pageDesc");
            s->setWordWrap(true);
            v->addWidget(s);
        }
        h->addLayout(v, 1);
        for (QWidget *a : actions)
            h->addWidget(a, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
        return w;
    }

    QFrame *statTile(const QString &caption, const QString &icon, QLabel *&value, QLabel *&sub, const QString &tint)
    {
        auto *c = W::card(nullptr);
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(c);
        h->setContentsMargins(16, 12, 16, 12);
        h->setSpacing(10);
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout;
        v->setSpacing(2);
        v->addWidget(W::label(caption, "caption"));
        value = W::label("", "bigValue");
        sub = W::label("", "muted");
        v->addWidget(value);
        v->addWidget(sub);
        h->addLayout(v, 1);
        QString top = "#eaf1ff", bottom = "#d9e6ff";
        QColor fg = Theme::primary;
        if (tint == "green") { top = "#e2fbec"; bottom = "#c9f3d9"; fg = Theme::tertiary; }
        else if (tint == "amber") { top = "#fff4d6"; bottom = "#ffe8a8"; fg = QColor("#8a5a00"); }
        else if (tint == "red") { top = "#ffe9e6"; bottom = "#ffd0ca"; fg = Theme::error; }
        h->addWidget(W::iconTile(icon, fg, top, bottom, 38), 0, Qt::AlignTop);
        return c;
    }

    QLabel *kbd(const QString &text)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(text);
        l->setObjectName("kbd");
        l->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        return l;
    }

    QLabel *dotLabel(const QString &text, const QColor &dot)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(QString("<span style='color:%1'>&#9679;</span>&nbsp;%2").arg(dot.name(), text));
        l->setTextFormat(Qt::RichText);
        return l;
    }

    QPushButton *pageButton(const QString &text, bool current)
    {
        auto *b = W::button(text, current ? "primary" : "tab");
        b->setFixedHeight(32);
        return b;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ Modal
    Modal::Modal(QWidget *parent, const QString &icon, const QString &title, const QString &subtitle,
                 const QString &tag, int width)
        : QDialog(parent, Qt::Dialog | Qt::FramelessWindowHint)
    {
        setAttribute(Qt::WA_TranslucentBackground);
        setModal(true);
        auto *outer = new QVBoxLayout(this);
        outer->setContentsMargins(14, 10, 14, 18); // room for the card's shadow
        auto *card = new Card;
        card->setObjectName("modalCard");
        outer->addWidget(card);
        auto *cv = new QVBoxLayout(card);
        cv->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        cv->setSpacing(0);

        auto *head = new QFrame;
        head->setObjectName("modalHead");
        auto *hh = new QHBoxLayout(head);
        hh->setContentsMargins(20, 16, 16, 14);
        hh->setSpacing(12);
        hh->addWidget(W::iconTile(icon, QColor("white"), "#4a86ff", "#2554d8", 44), 0, Qt::AlignTop);
        auto *tv = new QVBoxLayout;
        tv->setSpacing(1);
        auto *tr = new QHBoxLayout;
        tr->setSpacing(8);
        m_title = W::label(title, "modalTitle");
        tr->addWidget(m_title);
        if (!tag.isEmpty())
            tr->addWidget(W::badge(tag, "blue"));
        tr->addStretch();
        tv->addLayout(tr);
        if (!subtitle.isEmpty())
        {
            auto *s = W::label(subtitle, "muted");
            s->setWordWrap(true);
            tv->addWidget(s);
        }
        hh->addLayout(tv, 1);
        auto *x = W::button("", "icon", "close");
        x->setProperty("kind", "flat");
        x->setFixedSize(34, 34);
        x->setToolTip("Close (Esc)");
        connect(x, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &QDialog::reject);
        hh->addWidget(x, 0, Qt::AlignTop);
        cv->addWidget(head);

        auto *bodyW = new QWidget;
        m_body = new QVBoxLayout(bodyW);
        m_body->setContentsMargins(20, 16, 20, 16);
        m_body->setSpacing(12);
        cv->addWidget(bodyW, 1);

        m_footerBar = new QFrame;
        m_footerBar->setObjectName("modalFoot");
        m_footer = new QHBoxLayout(m_footerBar);
        m_footer->setContentsMargins(20, 12, 20, 12);
        m_footer->setSpacing(10);
        cv->addWidget(m_footerBar);

        setFixedWidth(width + 28);
    }

    void Modal::setTitle(const QString &t) { m_title->setText(t); }
    void Modal::hideFooter() { m_footerBar->hide(); }

    void Modal::showEvent(QShowEvent *e)
    {
        QDialog::showEvent(e);
        if (QWidget *host = parentWidget() ? parentWidget()->window() : nullptr)
        {
            if (!m_dim)
            {
                m_dim = new QWidget(host);
                m_dim->setObjectName("dim");
                m_dim->setAttribute(Qt::WA_StyledBackground, true);
                m_dim->setStyleSheet("background: rgba(15,27,51,46%);");
            }
            m_dim->setGeometry(host->rect());
            m_dim->show();
            m_dim->raise();
            raise();
            // centre over the host window
            const QPoint c = host->mapToGlobal(host->rect().center());
            move(c.x() - width() / 2, qMax(host->mapToGlobal(QPoint(0, 0)).y() + 16, c.y() - height() / 2));
        }
    }

    void Modal::hideEvent(QHideEvent *e)
    {
        QDialog::hideEvent(e);
        if (m_dim)
        {
            m_dim->hide();
            m_dim->deleteLater();
            m_dim = nullptr;
        }
    }
}

#include "UiStore.h"
#include <QRegularExpression>
#include <algorithm>

QString Season::statusOn(const QDate &d) const
{
    if (end < d)
        return "expired";
    if (start > d)
        return "scheduled";
    return active ? "active" : "scheduled";
}

int ParkedOrder::itemCount() const
{
    int n = 0;
    for (const auto &l : lines)
        n += l.qty;
    return n;
}

double ParkedOrder::total() const
{
    double t = 0;
    for (const auto &l : lines)
        t += (l.price - l.discount + l.tax) * l.qty;
    return t;
}

UiStore &UiStore::instance()
{
    static UiStore s;
    return s;
}

UiStore::UiStore()
{
    // Categories: the ones from the design plus "01" for the products already in Product.txt (Increment 2 barcodes).
    m_categories = {
        {"01", "General Store", "GEN", "#2554d8"},
        {"10", "Dairy & Milk Products", "DAI", "#2554d8"},
        {"20", "Bakery & Fresh Bread", "BAK", "#535e7a"},
        {"30", "Beverages & Tea", "BEV", "#006e4c"},
        {"40", "Snacks & Confectionery", "SNK", "#2554d8"},
        {"50", "Grocery & Staples", "GRC", "#b6c4ff"},
        {"60", "Personal Care & Hygiene", "PER", "#b6c4ff"},
        {"70", "Household & Cleaning", "HSD", "#b6c4ff"},
    };

    const QDate today = QDate::currentDate();
    auto mk = [](const QString &id, const QString &name, QDate a, QDate b, double pct, bool on) {
        Season s;
        s.id = id; s.name = name; s.start = a; s.end = b; s.percent = pct; s.active = on;
        return s;
    };
    const int y = today.year();
    m_seasons = {
        mk(QString("SEA-%1-01").arg(y), "Winter Warmth Sale", today.addDays(-3), today.addDays(4), 10, true),
        mk(QString("SEA-%1-02").arg(y + 1), "Eid-ul-Fitr Grand Fest", QDate(y + 1, 3, 20), QDate(y + 1, 3, 27), 15, false),
        mk(QString("SEA-%1-03").arg(y + 1), "Pakistan Day Mega Deal", QDate(y + 1, 3, 23), QDate(y + 1, 3, 24), 8, false),
        mk(QString("SEA-%1-08").arg(y), "Summer Clearance", QDate(y, 6, 15), QDate(y, 6, 30), 5, false),
        mk(QString("SEA-%1-12").arg(y - 1), "Winter Promo", QDate(y - 1, 12, 1), QDate(y - 1, 12, 31), 12, false),
    };

    // A couple of parked orders so the "Parked" strip is not empty on first launch.
    auto line = [](const char *name, const char *ac, int q, double price, double tax, double disc, int id) {
        ReceiptLine l;
        l.name = name; l.acronym = ac; l.qty = q; l.price = price; l.tax = tax; l.discount = disc; l.productId = id;
        return l;
    };
    ParkedOrder a;
    a.code = "HLD-108"; a.customer = "Asad Malik"; a.contact = "0301-5551212";
    a.parkedAt = QDateTime::currentDateTime().addSecs(-4 * 60);
    a.lines = {line("Olpers Full Cream Milk 1L", "OLPMILK", 2, 340, 0, 10, 1),
               line("National Whole Wheat Bread", "NBREAD", 1, 260, 18, 0, 2)};
    ParkedOrder b;
    b.code = "HLD-107"; b.customer = "Sana Malik"; b.contact = "0321-9876543";
    b.parkedAt = QDateTime::currentDateTime().addSecs(-17 * 60);
    b.lines = {line("Tapal Danedar Tea 190g", "TAPALTEA", 3, 210, 15, 5, 3)};
    m_parked = {a, b};
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- categories
const Category *UiStore::category(const QString &id) const
{
    for (const auto &c : m_categories)
        if (c.id == id)
            return &c;
    return nullptr;
}

int UiStore::productCount(const QString &id) const
{
    int n = 0;
    for (const auto &p : AppState::instance().products())
        if (p.ean.left(2) == id)
            ++n;
    return n;
}

QString UiStore::rangeText(const QString &id) const
{
    return QString("%100000 - %199999").arg(id);
}

QStringList UiStore::freeIds(int n) const
{
    QStringList out;
    for (int i = 10; i <= 99 && out.size() < n; i += (out.isEmpty() ? 1 : 5))
    {
        const QString id = QString::number(i);
        if (!category(id))
            out << id;
    }
    return out;
}

QString UiStore::validateCategory(const QString &id, const QString &name, const QString &acronym,
                                  const QString &ignoreId) const
{
    static const QRegularExpression twoDigits("^\\d{2}$");
    if (!twoDigits.match(id).hasMatch())
        return "Category ID must be exactly 2 digits.";
    if (const Category *c = category(id); c && id != ignoreId)
        return QString("Category ID \"%1\" is already in use by \"%2\". Please pick an unused 2-digit code (e.g. %3).")
            .arg(id, c->name, freeIds(1).value(0, "80"));
    if (name.trimmed().isEmpty())
        return "Category name is required.";
    if (acronym.trimmed().isEmpty() || acronym.trimmed().size() > 3)
        return "Acronym must be 1 to 3 characters.";
    for (const auto &c : m_categories)
        if (c.id != ignoreId && c.acronym.compare(acronym.trimmed(), Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0)
            return QString("Acronym \"%1\" is already used by \"%2\".").arg(acronym.trimmed().toUpper(), c.name);
    return QString();
}

bool UiStore::addCategory(const Category &c, QString *error)
{
    const QString why = validateCategory(c.id, c.name, c.acronym);
    if (!why.isEmpty())
    {
        if (error) *error = why;
        return false;
    }
    Category n = c;
    n.name = c.name.trimmed();
    n.acronym = c.acronym.trimmed().toUpper();
    m_categories.push_back(n);
    std::sort(m_categories.begin(), m_categories.end(), [](const Category &a, const Category &b) { return a.id < b.id; });
    emit categoriesChanged();
    return true;
}

bool UiStore::updateCategory(const QString &oldId, const Category &c, QString *error)
{
    const QString why = validateCategory(c.id, c.name, c.acronym, oldId);
    if (!why.isEmpty())
    {
        if (error) *error = why;
        return false;
    }
    if (oldId != c.id && productCount(oldId) > 0)
    {
        if (error) *error = "This category already has products; changing its id would break their barcodes.";
        return false;
    }
    for (auto &x : m_categories)
        if (x.id == oldId)
        {
            x = c;
            x.name = c.name.trimmed();
            x.acronym = c.acronym.trimmed().toUpper();
        }
    std::sort(m_categories.begin(), m_categories.end(), [](const Category &a, const Category &b) { return a.id < b.id; });
    emit categoriesChanged();
    return true;
}

bool UiStore::removeCategory(const QString &id, QString *error)
{
    if (productCount(id) > 0)
    {
        if (error) *error = QString("%1 product(s) still use this category.").arg(productCount(id));
        return false;
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < m_categories.size(); ++i)
        if (m_categories[i].id == id)
        {
            m_categories.removeAt(i);
            emit categoriesChanged();
            return true;
        }
    return false;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- seasons
void UiStore::addSeason(const Season &s)
{
    m_seasons.push_back(s);
    emit seasonsChanged();
}

void UiStore::updateSeason(const QString &id, const Season &s)
{
    for (auto &x : m_seasons)
        if (x.id == id)
            x = s;
    emit seasonsChanged();
}

void UiStore::removeSeason(const QString &id)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < m_seasons.size(); ++i)
        if (m_seasons[i].id == id)
        {
            m_seasons.removeAt(i);
            break;
        }
    emit seasonsChanged();
}

void UiStore::setSeasonActive(const QString &id, bool on)
{
    for (auto &x : m_seasons)
        if (x.id == id)
            x.active = on;
    emit seasonsChanged();
}

QString UiStore::nextSeasonId() const
{
    int maxN = 0;
    for (const auto &s : m_seasons)
        maxN = qMax(maxN, s.id.section('-', 2).toInt());
    return QString("SEA-%1-%2").arg(QDate::currentDate().year()).arg(maxN + 1, 2, 10, QLatin1Char('0'));
}

const Season *UiStore::activeSeason(const QDate &d) const
{
    for (const auto &s : m_seasons)
        if (s.active && s.start <= d && d <= s.end)
            return &s;
    return nullptr;
}

QString UiStore::validateSeason(const QString &name, const QDate &start, const QDate &end, double percent) const
{
    if (name.trimmed().isEmpty())
        return "Give the season a name.";
    if (!start.isValid() || !end.isValid())
        return "Enter valid start and end dates.";
    if (end < start)
        return "The end date must not be before the start date.";
    if (percent <= 0 || percent > 100)
        return "Discount must be between 0 and 100 percent.";
    return QString();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- parked orders
QString UiStore::park(const QString &customer, const QString &contact, const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines)
{
    ParkedOrder p;
    p.code = QString("HLD-%1").arg(m_nextHold++);
    p.customer = customer.trimmed().isEmpty() ? "Walk-in Customer" : customer;
    p.contact = contact;
    p.parkedAt = QDateTime::currentDateTime();
    p.lines = lines;
    m_parked.prepend(p);
    emit parkedChanged();
    return p.code;
}

bool UiStore::takeParked(const QString &code, ParkedOrder &out)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < m_parked.size(); ++i)
        if (m_parked[i].code == code)
        {
            out = m_parked[i];
            m_parked.removeAt(i);
            emit parkedChanged();
            return true;
        }
    return false;
}

void UiStore::discardParked(const QString &code)
{
    ParkedOrder ignored;
    takeParked(code, ignored);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- manager sign-off
bool UiStore::verifyManager(const QString &staffId, const QString &pin, QString *managerName) const
{
    const bool ok = staffId.trimmed().compare(demoManagerId(), Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0 && pin == demoManagerPin();
    if (ok && managerName)
        *managerName = demoManagerName();
    return ok;
}

// Short "scanner" beep after a successful scan (if enabled in Settings).
// Windows Beep() blocks while it plays, so it runs on its own short-lived thread.
static void scanBeep()
{
    if (!AppState::instance().settings().beepOnScan)
        return;
#ifdef Q_OS_WIN
    std::thread([] { Beep(1800, 120); }).detach(); // 1800 Hz for 120 ms
#else
    QApplication::beep();
#endif
}

namespace W
{
    QFrame *card(QWidget *parent, const char *objectName)
    {
        const QString n = objectName;
        if (n == "card" || n == "strip" || n == "totals")
            return new Card(parent, false);
        if (n == "cardAlert")
            return new Card(parent, true);
        auto *f = new QFrame(parent);
        f->setObjectName(objectName);
        return f;
    }

    QVBoxLayout *pageLayout(QWidget *page)
    {
        page->setObjectName("page");
        auto *l = new QVBoxLayout(page);
        l->setContentsMargins(14, 10, 14, 6);
        l->setSpacing(0);
        return l;
    }

    QLabel *iconTile(const QString &icon, const QColor &fg, const QString &top, const QString &bottom, int size)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel;
        l->setFixedSize(size, size);
        l->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        l->setStyleSheet(QString("background: qlineargradient(x1:0,y1:0,x2:0,y2:1, stop:0 %1, stop:1 %2);"
                                 "border-radius:%3px; border-bottom:3px solid rgba(0,0,0,18%);")
                             .arg(top, bottom)
                             .arg(size / 3));
        l->setPixmap(Theme::iconPixmap(icon, fg, size / 2));
        return l;
    }

    QLabel *label(const QString &text, const char *objectName, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(text, parent);
        if (objectName)
            l->setObjectName(objectName);
        return l;
    }

    void setBadge(QLabel *l, const QString &text, const QString &kind)
    {
        l->setText(text);
        l->setProperty("badge", kind);
        Theme::repolish(l);
    }

    QLabel *badge(const QString &text, const QString &kind, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(parent);
        l->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        setBadge(l, text, kind);
        return l;
    }

    QPushButton *button(const QString &text, const QString &kind, const QString &icon, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *b = new QPushButton(text, parent);
        if (!kind.isEmpty())
            b->setProperty("kind", kind);
        if (!icon.isEmpty())
        {
            const bool light = kind == "primary";
            const bool danger = kind == "danger";
            const QColor c = light ? QColor("white") : danger ? Theme::error
                                                              : Theme::primary;
            b->setIcon(Theme::icon(icon, c, 16));
            b->setIconSize({16, 16});
        }
        b->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
        return b;
    }

    QLabel *iconLabel(const QString &icon, const QColor &color, int px, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(parent);
        l->setPixmap(Theme::iconPixmap(icon, color, px));
        l->setFixedSize(px, px);
        l->setAttribute(Qt::WA_TranslucentBackground);
        return l;
    }

    }

        f->setFixedHeight(1);

        return f;
    }

    QString money(double v, bool sign)
    {
        const QString s = QLocale(QLocale::English).toString(qAbs(v), 'f', 2);
        if (v < 0)
            return "-PKR " + s;
        return (sign ? "+PKR " : "PKR ") + s;
    }

    QString statusBadgeKind(const QString &status)
    {
        if (status == "Paid")
            return "green";
        if (status == "In Progress")
            return "blue";
        if (status == "On Hold")
            return "amber";
        return "grey";
    }

    QHBoxLayout *headerRow(QVBoxLayout *into, const QString &icon, const QString &title, QWidget *right)
    {
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout;
        h->setSpacing(8);
        if (!icon.isEmpty())
            h->addWidget(iconLabel(icon, Theme::primary, 18));
        h->addWidget(label(title, "h1"));
        h->addStretch();
        if (right)
            h->addWidget(right, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
        into->addLayout(h);
        return h;
    }
